  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# Verification script for Goose hook/lifecycle research.\n#\n# Wires an Entire capture plugin into a throwaway probe workspace using\n# Goose's PROJECT-scope plugin discovery (<workspace>/.agents/plugins/), so\n# no global Goose config (~/.config/goose, ~/.agents) is ever modified.\n#\n# Usage:\n#   verify-goose.sh                  # automated: goose run -t <prompt> in probe workspace\n#   verify-goose.sh --run-cmd '<cmd>'  # automated with a custom command (runs in workspace)\n#   verify-goose.sh --manual-live    # you drive goose manually in the workspace\n#   verify-goose.sh --keep           # keep the probe dir after the run\n\nAGENT_NAME=\"Goose\"\nAGENT_SLUG=\"goose\"\nAGENT_BIN=\"goose\"\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\nPROJECT_DIR=$(dirname \"$SCRIPT_DIR\")\nPROBE_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/.probe-${AGENT_SLUG}-$(date +%s)\"\nWORKSPACE=\"$PROBE_DIR/workspace\"\nCAPTURE_DIR=\"$PROBE_DIR/captures\"\nPLUGIN_DIR=\"$WORKSPACE/.agents/plugins/entire-verify\"\n\nMODE=\"auto\"\nRUN_CMD=\"\"\nKEEP=\"false\"\nwhile [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do\n  case \"$1\" in\n    --run-cmd) RUN_CMD=\"$2\"; shift 2 ;;\n    --manual-live) MODE=\"manual\"; shift ;;\n    --keep) KEEP=\"true\"; shift ;;\n    *) echo \"unknown arg: $1\" >&2; exit 2 ;;\n  esac\ndone\n\npass=0; warn=0; fail=0\ncheck() { # check <PASS|WARN|FAIL> <label> [detail]\n  local status=\"$1\" label=\"$2\" detail=\"${3:-}\"\n  case \"$status\" in\n    PASS) pass=$((pass+1)) ;;\n    WARN) warn=$((warn+1)) ;;\n    FAIL) fail=$((fail+1)) ;;\n  esac\n  printf '%-4s | %-28s | %s\\n' \"$status\" \"$label\" \"$detail\"\n}\n\necho \"== 1. Static checks ==\"\nif command -v \"$AGENT_BIN\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  check PASS \"binary present\" \"$(command -v \"$AGENT_BIN\")\"\nelse\n  check FAIL \"binary present\" \"goose not on PATH\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nVERSION=$(\"$AGENT_BIN\" --version 2>&1 | tr -d '[:space:]' || true)\n[[ -n \"$VERSION\" ]] && check PASS \"version\" \"$VERSION\" || check WARN \"version\" \"no output\"\nHELP=$(\"$AGENT_BIN\" --help 2>&1 || true)\ngrep -qi \"session\" <<<\"$HELP\" && check PASS \"session keywords in help\" \"session\" || check WARN \"session keywords\" \"none\"\ngrep -qi \"run\" <<<\"$HELP\" && check PASS \"headless run command\" \"goose run\" || check WARN \"headless run\" \"not found\"\n\nDATA_DIR=\"${XDG_DATA_HOME:-$HOME/.local/share}/goose\"\nSESSIONS_DB=\"$DATA_DIR/sessions/sessions.db\"\n[[ -f \"$SESSIONS_DB\" ]] && check PASS \"sessions.db present\" \"$SESSIONS_DB\" || check WARN \"sessions.db\" \"not found at $SESSIONS_DB (created on first session)\"\n\necho\necho \"== 2. Hook wiring (project-scope plugin, non-destructive) ==\"\nmkdir -p \"$PLUGIN_DIR/hooks\" \"$CAPTURE_DIR\" \"$WORKSPACE\"\n\ncat > \"$PLUGIN_DIR/capture.sh\" <<'EOF'\n#!/bin/sh\n# Dump hook stdin JSON to the capture dir, named by event. Always exit 0 so\n# blocking hooks (Stop) allow the agent to proceed.\npayload=$(cat)\nevent=$(printf '%s' \"$payload\" | sed -n 's/.*\"event\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"\\([^\"]*\\)\".*/\\1/p')\ndir=$(dirname \"$0\")/../../../../../captures\nprintf '%s' \"$payload\" > \"$dir/${event:-unknown}-$(date +%s%N).json\"\nexit 0\nEOF\nchmod +x \"$PLUGIN_DIR/capture.sh\"\n\ncat > \"$PLUGIN_DIR/hooks/hooks.json\" <<'EOF'\n{\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"SessionStart\":   [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"SessionEnd\":     [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"UserPromptSubmit\": [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"Stop\":           [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"PreToolUse\":     [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"PostToolUse\":    [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"PostToolUseFailure\": [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"SubagentStart\":  [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }],\n    \"SubagentStop\":   [{ \"hooks\": [{ \"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"${PLUGIN_ROOT}/capture.sh\" }] }]\n  }\n}\nEOF\ncheck PASS \"capture plugin written\" \"$PLUGIN_DIR\"\n\n(cd \"$WORKSPACE\" && git init -q .) || true\necho \"# probe workspace\" > \"$WORKSPACE/README.md\"\n\necho\necho \"== 3. Run ==\"\nDEFAULT_PROMPT='Create a file named verify.txt containing exactly ENTIRE_VERIFY_FILE, then reply with exactly: ENTIRE_VERIFY_OK'\nif [[ \"$MODE\" == \"manual\" ]]; then\n  echo \"Run goose manually in: $WORKSPACE\"\n  echo \"Suggested: (cd $WORKSPACE && goose run -t '$DEFAULT_PROMPT')\"\n  read -r -p \"Press Enter when done...\"\nelse\n  if [[ -z \"$RUN_CMD\" ]]; then\n    RUN_CMD=\"goose run -t \\\"$DEFAULT_PROMPT\\\"\"\n  fi\n  echo \"+ (cd $WORKSPACE && $RUN_CMD)\"\n  (cd \"$WORKSPACE\" && eval \"$RUN_CMD\") || check WARN \"agent run\" \"non-zero exit\"\nfi\n\necho\necho \"== 4. Captures ==\"\nshopt -s nullglob\ncaptures=(\"$CAPTURE_DIR\"/*.json)\nif [[ ${#captures[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then\n  check FAIL \"hook captures\" \"no payloads captured\"\nelse\n  check PASS \"hook captures\" \"${#captures[@]} payload(s)\"\n  for f in \"${captures[@]}\"; do\n    echo \"--- $f\"\n    python3 -m json.tool \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || cat \"$f\"\n  done\nfi\n\necho\necho \"== 5. Verdict ==\"\nfor event in SessionStart UserPromptSubmit PreToolUse PostToolUse Stop SessionEnd; do\n  if ls \"$CAPTURE_DIR/$event\"-*.json >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n    check PASS \"event: $event\" \"captured\"\n  else\n    check WARN \"event: $event\" \"not captured\"\n  fi\ndone\necho\necho \"PASS=$pass WARN=$warn FAIL=$fail\"\necho \"Probe dir: $PROBE_DIR\"\nif [[ \"$KEEP\" != \"true\" ]]; then\n  echo \"(probe dir kept for analysis; delete manually or rerun with cleanup)\"\nfi\n[[ $fail -eq 0 ]]\n"
}

  remove       Remove sessions. Runs interactively if no ID, name, or regex is provided.
  export       Export a session
  import       Import a session from JSON or an encrypted Nostr share link
  diagnostics  
  help         Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
  -n, --name <NAME>
          Specify a name for your chat session. When used with --resume, will resume this specific session if it exists.

      --session-id <SESSION_ID>
          Specify a session ID directly. When used with --resume, will resume this specific session if it exists.

      --path <PATH>
          Legacy parameter for backward compatibility. Extracts session ID from the file path (e.g., '/path/to/20250325_200615.
          jsonl' -> '20250325_200615').

  -r, --resume
          Continue from a previous session. If --name or --session-id is provided, resumes that specific session. Otherwise, resumes the most recently used session.

      --fork
          Create a new session by copying all messages from a previous session. Must be used with --resume. If --name or --session-id is provided, forks that specific session. Otherwise, forks the most recently used session.

      --history
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/AGENT.md",
  "content": "# Goose — External Agent Research\n\n## Verdict: COMPATIBLE\n\nGoose (Block's open-source AI coding agent, Rust) has a native lifecycle hooks\nsystem, durable session storage with full message content and token totals, and\na headless run mode. All findings below were verified against goose 1.37.0 with\nreal captured payloads unless marked `(unverified)`.\n\n## Static Checks\n| Check | Result | Notes |\n|-------|--------|-------|\n| Binary present | PASS | `~/.local/bin/goose` |\n| Help available | PASS | `goose --help` |\n| Version info | PASS | `1.37.0` |\n| Hook keywords | PASS | hooks system in source (`crates/goose/src/hooks/mod.rs`); not surfaced in `--help` |\n| Session keywords | PASS | `session`, `--resume`, `export` |\n| Config directory | PASS | `~/.config/goose/config.yaml` (YAML) |\n| Documentation | PASS | Open Plugins hooks spec: https://open-plugins.com/agent-builders/components/hooks |\n\n## Binary\n- Name: `goose`\n- Version: 1.37.0\n- Install: `curl -fsSL https://github.com/block/goose/releases/download/stable/download_cli.sh | bash` (or Homebrew `brew install block-goose-cli`)\n\n## Hook Mechanism\n- Config file: `<project-root>/.agents/plugins/<plugin-name>/hooks/hooks.json` (project scope) or `~/.agents/plugins/<plugin-name>/hooks/hooks.json` (user scope) **(verified, project scope)**\n- Config format: JSON (Open Plugins hooks spec)\n- Hook registration: drop a plugin directory containing `hooks/hooks.json`; plugins are **enabled by default** when present (no settings change needed). `enabledPlugins`/`disabledPlugins` in `~/.config/goose/settings.json` or `<project>/.config/goose/settings.json` / `settings.local.json` can override. Project root for discovery = goose's working directory.\n- Hook actions: `{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"...\", \"timeout\": <secs>}`; command runs via `sh -c` with `PLUGIN_ROOT` env var and `${PLUGIN_ROOT}` placeholder substitution; default timeout 30s; exit 0 = success. `Stop` and `PreToolUse` are blocking: exit code 2 or `{\"decision\":\"block\",\"reason\":\"...\"}` on stdout denies.\n- Hook names and protocol mapping **(all verified via captures)**:\n  | Native Hook Name | When It Fires | Protocol Event Type |\n  |-----------------|---------------|---------------------|\n  | `SessionStart` | session begins (interactive + headless `goose run`) | 1=SessionStart |\n  | `UserPromptSubmit` | user prompt accepted, before agent works | 2=TurnStart |\n  | `Stop` | agent finished responding (blocking hook — always exit 0) | 3=TurnEnd |\n  | `SessionEnd` | session terminates | 5=SessionEnd |\n  | `SubagentStart` / `SubagentStop` | subagent spawned/completed | 6/7 (not declared in v1) |\n  | `PreToolUse` / `PostToolUse` / `PostToolUseFailure` | around each tool call | not needed (modified files come from transcript) |\n  | `BeforeReadFile` / `AfterFileEdit` / `BeforeShellExecution` / `AfterShellExecution` | file/shell granularity | not needed |\n  - There is **no Compaction hook** (event type 4). Goose compacts context internally without emitting a hook.\n- Hook input format: JSON on stdin (`HookContext`, snake_case). Captured payloads:\n  ```json\n  {\"event\":\"SessionStart\",\"session_id\":\"20260611_1\",\"matcher_context\":null}\n  {\"event\":\"UserPromptSubmit\",\"session_id\":\"20260611_1\",\"matcher_context\":\"<prompt>\",\"message\":\"<prompt>\"}\n  {\"event\":\"Stop\",\"session_id\":\"20260611_1\",\"matcher_context\":null}\n  {\"event\":\"SessionEnd\",\"session_id\":\"20260611_1\",\"matcher_context\":null}\n  ```\n  Tool events additionally carry `tool_name`, `tool_input`, and `working_dir`.\n  **Session-level events do NOT include `working_dir`, `session_ref`, or a timestamp** — the binary derives the transcript reference from `session_id` and stamps events at receipt time.\n\n## Session Management\n- Session directory: `${XDG_DATA_HOME:-~/.local/share}/goose/sessions/` **(verified on macOS)**. `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` env var overrides all goose paths (`$GOOSE_PATH_ROOT/data/sessions/`) — useful for hermetic e2e tests.\n- Session ID source: `session_id` field of every hook payload. Format `YYYYMMDD_N` (e.g. `20260611_1`), incrementing per day.\n- Session file format: **SQLite database** `sessions.db` (tables: `sessions`, `messages`, `threads`). There is no per-session file; stray `*.jsonl` files in the sessions dir are empty legacy artifacts.\n- `sessions` table carries `working_dir`, `created_at`/`updated_at` (UTC `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`), `provider_name`, `model_config_json`, and token totals. Sessions for a repo are findable via `WHERE working_dir = ?`.\n\n## Transcript\n- Location: no native file. Recommended approach **(verified)**: materialize via\n  `goose session export --session-id <id> --format json`, written to\n  `<session-dir>/<session-id>.export.json` by the `prepare-transcript` subcommand\n  (`transcript_preparer` capability). `session_ref` = that exported path.\n- Format: single JSON object with session metadata + `conversation` array of messages.\n- Export top-level fields **(verified)**: `id`, `working_dir`, `name`, `session_type`, `created_at`/`updated_at` (RFC 3339), `total_tokens`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `accumulated_total_tokens`, `accumulated_input_tokens`, `accumulated_output_tokens`, `accumulated_cost`, `conversation`, `message_count`, `provider_name`, `model_config` (has `model_name`).\n- Message schema (camelCase content, unlike snake_case hook payloads) **(verified)**:\n  ```json\n  {\"id\":\"msg_...\",\"role\":\"user\",\"created\":1781175769,\n   \"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"...\"}],\n   \"metadata\":{\"userVisible\":true,\"agentVisible\":true}}\n  ```\n- User prompt field: `conversation[].content[].text` where `role==\"user\"` and `content[].type==\"text\"` (skip `toolResponse` user messages).\n- Modified files field: `conversation[].content[]` where `type==\"toolRequest\"`, `toolCall.value.name` in `write`/`text_editor`/`edit`-style tools; file path at `toolCall.value.arguments.path`. Shell commands at `arguments.command` for heuristics.\n- Token usage field: session-level `accumulated_input_tokens`/`accumulated_output_tokens` (export and `sessions` table). Per-message `tokens` column exists but was NULL in verification. No cache token split available.\n- Example tool entry **(verified)**:\n  ```json\n  {\"type\":\"toolRequest\",\"id\":\"toolu_...\",\"toolCall\":{\"status\":\"success\",\n   \"value\":{\"name\":\"write\",\"arguments\":{\"path\":\"/abs/path/verify.txt\",\"content\":\"...\"}}}}\n  ```\n\n## Data Storage Verification\n- Session files contain actual assistant content: **YES** — real response text (`ENTIRE_VERIFY_OK`), full tool calls with arguments, and tool results; no placeholders.\n- Secondary storage location: none needed — `sessions.db` / `goose session export` is complete.\n- Cross-reference key: `session_id` (hook payload) → `sessions.id` / `messages.session_id` / export `--session-id`.\n- Hook data flow verified: **YES** — stdin JSON received for all 6 session/turn events plus tool events; `PLUGIN_ROOT` env set; no stdin redirect issues.\n- Verification method: capture plugin in probe workspace + `goose run -t` with distinctive strings, then grepped `sessions.db` and export output.\n\n## Protocol Mapping\n| Subcommand | Native Concept | Implementation Notes | Feasibility |\n|-----------|---------------|---------------------|-------------|\n| `info` | — | static metadata; `protected_dirs: [\".agents\"]` | Required |\n| `detect` | `goose` binary | `exec.LookPath(\"goose\")` | Required |\n| `get-session-id` | hook `session_id` | from HookInput | Required |\n| `get-session-dir` | sessions dir | `$GOOSE_PATH_ROOT/data/sessions` else `${XDG_DATA_HOME:-~/.local/share}/goose/sessions` | Required |\n| `resolve-session-file` | export path | `<session-dir>/<session-id>.export.json` | Required |\n| `read-session` | export JSON | session metadata from export/DB; `start_time` from `created_at` | Required |\n| `write-session` | — | persist AgentSession native_data alongside export (no goose import needed for checkpoint flow) | Required |\n| `read-transcript` | export file | raw bytes of `session_ref` | Required |\n| `chunk-transcript` / `reassemble-transcript` | — | generic base64 chunking | Required |\n| `format-resume-command` | resume flag | `goose session --resume --session-id <id>` | Required |\n| `parse-hook` | HookContext JSON | map per table above; TurnStart prompt from `message` | hooks |\n| `install-hooks` | project plugin | write `<repo>/.agents/plugins/entire/hooks/hooks.json` calling the entire hook handler | hooks |\n| `uninstall-hooks` | — | remove that plugin dir | hooks |\n| `are-hooks-installed` | — | check plugin dir + hooks.json content | hooks |\n| `get-transcript-position` | export size | file size (re-export shifts bytes; position resets via prepare) | transcript_analyzer |\n| `extract-modified-files` | `toolRequest` entries | parse `conversation` for write/edit tool calls | transcript_analyzer |\n| `extract-prompts` | user text messages | parse `conversation` | transcript_analyzer |\n| `extract-summary` | session `name` | auto-generated session name (`\"File verification request\"`) as summary | transcript_analyzer |\n| `prepare-transcript` | `goose session export` | shell out: `goose session export --session-id <id> --format json -o <path>` | transcript_preparer |\n| `calculate-tokens` | export token fields | `accumulated_input/output_tokens`; `api_call_count` = assistant message count | token_calculator |\n\n## Selected Capabilities\n| Capability | Declared | Justification |\n|-----------|----------|---------------|\n| hooks | true | native hooks.json plugin system, verified end-to-end |\n| transcript_analyzer | true | export JSON has prompts, tool calls with file paths |\n| transcript_preparer | true | transcript must be materialized from sessions.db via `goose session export` |\n| token_calculator | true | accumulated input/output tokens in export |\n| text_generator | false | possible later via headless `goose run -t --no-session`, but output includes UI text; defer |\n| hook_response_writer | false | no clean native channel for display-only messages (Stop deny blocks the turn) |\n| subagent_aware_extractor | false | subagent sessions are separate `sessions.db` rows (`session_type='subagent'`), not per-dir transcripts; defer |\n\n## Gaps & Limitations\n- Hook payloads have **no timestamp and no session file reference** — events are stamped at receipt; transcript path is derived from `session_id`.\n- Session-level hook events lack `working_dir` (tool events have it). Entire sets cwd to repo root, so this doesn't block.\n- **No Compaction hook** — goose's internal context compaction can't trigger event type 4; token deltas may jump after compaction.\n- Per-message token counts were NULL in verification; only session-level accumulated totals are reliable.\n- `prepare-transcript` shells out to `goose` (must be on PATH — already guaranteed by `detect`). Direct SQLite reads are a fallback but are exposed to schema migrations (schema v10 at time of research).\n- Hook payload contains full `tool_input` (file contents) — payloads can be large.\n\n## Captured Payloads\n- Verification script: `agents/entire-agent-goose/scripts/verify-goose.sh`\n- Capture directory: `agents/entire-agent-goose/.probe-goose-*/captures/`\n- Verification status: **VERIFIED** — script ran headless `goose run -t`; all 6 session/turn/tool event payloads captured; stored data completeness confirmed against sessions.db and `goose session export`.\n- Notable differences from docs: none material; source matched behavior. Hook JSON is snake_case while exported message content is camelCase — easy to conflate.\n\n## E2E Test Prerequisites\n- Entire CLI binary: `entire` from PATH or `E2E_ENTIRE_BIN`\n- Agent CLI binary: `goose` (1.37.0 verified); install via download_cli.sh or Homebrew\n- Non-interactive prompt command: `goose run -t \"<prompt>\"` (runs SessionStart → UserPromptSubmit → Stop → SessionEnd)\n- Interactive mode: supported — `goose session`; resume via `goose session --resume --session-id <id>`\n- Expected prompt pattern: `goose is ready` banner on session start (unverified for interactive input prompt regex)\n- Timeout multiplier: 2.0 (provider on this machine: openrouter / anthropic claude — network-bound)\n- Bootstrap steps: provider must be configured (`~/.config/goose/config.yaml` + keyring/secret); `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT=<tmp>` gives hermetic config/data dirs but then provider config must be seeded under `$GOOSE_PATH_ROOT/config/`\n- Transient error patterns: `rate limit`, `overloaded`, `503`, `529`, `tunnel`\n"
}

1	---
2	name: write-tests
3	description: >
4	  Phase 2: Scaffold the external agent binary and add the correct testing hooks:
5	  protocol compliance through external-agents-tests and lifecycle integration
6	  through this repo's e2e harness.
7	---
8	
9	# Write-Tests Procedure
10	
11	Scaffold the external agent binary and wire it into the current testing split.
12	
13	Do not add new generic protocol tests under this repo's `e2e/` directory.
14	
15	## Prerequisites
16	
17	Ensure these are available:
18	
19	- `AGENT_NAME`
20	- `AGENT_SLUG`
21	- `LANGUAGE`
22	- `PROJECT_DIR`
23	- `<PROJECT_DIR>/AGENT.md`
24	
25	## Step 1: Scaffold the Binary
26	
27	Create a compilable binary that already exposes the protocol subcommands with valid JSON shapes.
28	
29	Read at runtime:
30	
31	1. `https://github.com/entireio/cli/blob/main/docs/architecture/external-agent-protocol.md`
32	2. `https://github.com/entireio/cli/blob/main/cmd/entire/cli/agent/external/types.go`
33	3. `https://github.com/entireio/cli/blob/main/cmd/entire/cli/agent/external/external.go`
34	4. `<PROJECT_DIR>/AGENT.md`
35	
36	For Go agents, prefer this shape:
37	
38	```text
39	<PROJECT_DIR>/
40	  go.mod
41	  mise.toml
42	  README.md
43	  AGENT.md
44	  cmd/
45	    entire-agent-<slug>/
46	      main.go
47	  internal/
48	    protocol/
49	    <agent>/
50	```
51	
52	The scaffold must:
53	
54	- build successfully
55	- return valid `info` JSON
56	- exit non-zero for an unknown subcommand
57	
58	## Step 2: Prepare Protocol Compliance
59	
60	The generic protocol suite lives in `external-agents-tests`.
61	
62	Your job in this repo is to make the agent easy to validate there:
63	
64	1. Keep the binary layout compatible with a simple build command:
65	   `go build -o entire-agent-<slug> ./cmd/entire-agent-<slug>`
66	2. If stronger black-box assertions are useful, add an optional fixture file under the agent module, for example:
67	   `<PROJECT_DIR>/testdata/compliance.json`
68	3. Document any required fixture paths in `<PROJECT_DIR>/README.md` and `<PROJECT_DIR>/AGENT.md`
69	
70	Validate the scaffold locally with the `external-agents-tests` CLI (installed via `mise install`):
71	
72	```bash
73	cd <PROJECT_DIR>
74	mise run build
75	external-agents-tests verify ./entire-agent-<slug>
76	```
77	
78	At this stage the tests are expected to fail. The goal is just to confirm the harness reaches the binary.
79	
80	## Step 3: Wire the Agent into Lifecycle Tests
81	
82	Lifecycle integration remains in this repo.
83	
84	Read these files before editing:
85	
86	1. `e2e/setup_test.go`
87	2. `e2e/build.go`
88	3. `e2e/lifecycle_test.go`
89	4. `e2e/agents/agent.go`
90	5. `e2e/agents/kiro.go`
91	6. `e2e/testutil/repo.go`
92	7. `e2e/entire/entire.go`
93	
94	Then:
95	
96	1. Add `e2e/agents/<slug>.go` implementing the `Agent` interface.
97	2. Register the agent in `init()` and set a concurrency gate.
98	3. Implement `RunPrompt`, `StartSession`, `PromptPattern`, timeout multiplier, and any external-agent marker behavior needed by `SetupRepo`.
99	4. Reuse the shared lifecycle scenarios in `e2e/lifecycle_test.go`. Add new lifecycle tests only if the new agent needs behavior that is not already covered.
100	
101	## Step 4: Verify the Scaffolding
102	
103	Run these checks:
104	
105	```bash
106	cd <PROJECT_DIR>
107	mise run build
108	./entire-agent-<slug> info
109	go test ./...
110	
111	cd /path/to/repo/e2e
112	go test -c -tags=e2e
113	```
114	
115	Also run one failing compliance pass against the built binary:
116	
117	```bash
118	external-agents-tests verify ./entire-agent-<slug>
119	```
120	
121	## Step 5: Commit Gate
122	
123	Create a commit once:
124	
125	- the binary compiles
126	- `info` returns valid JSON
127	- lifecycle harness compiles
128	- `external-agents-tests verify` can invoke the binary, even if assertions still fail
129	
130	## Output Checklist
131	
132	Summarize:
133	
134	- files created under `<PROJECT_DIR>`
135	- lifecycle adapter files added or updated under `e2e/`
136	- optional compliance fixture paths
137	- commands run and their status
138	
```

1	// Package external provides an adapter that bridges external agent binaries
2	// (discovered via PATH as entire-agent-<name>) to the agent.Agent interface.
3	// Communication uses a subcommand-based protocol with JSON over stdin/stdout.
4	package external
5	
6	import (
7		"encoding/base64"
8		"encoding/json"
9		"errors"
10		"fmt"
11		"time"
12	
13		"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
14	)
15	
16	// ProtocolVersion is the current protocol version expected by the CLI.
17	const ProtocolVersion = 1
18	
19	// InfoResponse is the JSON returned by the "info" subcommand.
20	type InfoResponse struct {
21		ProtocolVersion int                `json:"protocol_version"`
22		Name            string             `json:"name"`
23		Type            string             `json:"type"`
24		Description     string             `json:"description"`
25		IsPreview       bool               `json:"is_preview"`
26		ProtectedDirs   []string           `json:"protected_dirs"`
27		ProtectedFiles  []string           `json:"protected_files"`
28		HookNames       []string           `json:"hook_names"`
29		Capabilities    agent.DeclaredCaps `json:"capabilities"`
30	}
31	
32	// DetectResponse is the JSON returned by the "detect" subcommand.
33	type DetectResponse struct {
34		Present bool `json:"present"`
35	}
36	
37	// SessionIDResponse is the JSON returned by the "get-session-id" subcommand.
38	type SessionIDResponse struct {
39		SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
40	}
41	
42	// SessionDirResponse is the JSON returned by the "get-session-dir" subcommand.
43	type SessionDirResponse struct {
44		SessionDir string `json:"session_dir"`
45	}
46	
47	// SessionFileResponse is the JSON returned by the "resolve-session-file" subcommand.
48	type SessionFileResponse struct {
49		SessionFile string `json:"session_file"`
50	}
51	
52	// ChunkResponse is the JSON returned by the "chunk-transcript" subcommand.
53	type ChunkResponse struct {
54		Chunks [][]byte `json:"chunks"`
55	}
56	
57	// ResumeCommandResponse is the JSON returned by the "format-resume-command" subcommand.
58	type ResumeCommandResponse struct {
59		Command string `json:"command"`
60	}
61	
62	// HooksInstalledCountResponse is the JSON returned by the "install-hooks" subcommand.
63	type HooksInstalledCountResponse struct {
64		HooksInstalled int `json:"hooks_installed"`
65	}
66	
67	// AreHooksInstalledResponse is the JSON returned by the "are-hooks-installed" subcommand.
68	type AreHooksInstalledResponse struct {
69		Installed bool `json:"installed"`
70	}
71	
72	// TranscriptPositionResponse is the JSON returned by the "get-transcript-position" subcommand.
73	type TranscriptPositionResponse struct {
74		Position int `json:"position"`
75	}
76	
77	// ExtractFilesResponse is the JSON returned by file-extraction subcommands.
78	type ExtractFilesResponse struct {
79		Files           []string `json:"files"`
80		CurrentPosition int      `json:"current_position"`
81	}
82	
83	// ExtractPromptsResponse is the JSON returned by the "extract-prompts" subcommand.
84	type ExtractPromptsResponse struct {
85		Prompts []string `json:"prompts"`
86	}
87	
88	// ExtractSummaryResponse is the JSON returned by the "extract-summary" subcommand.
89	type ExtractSummaryResponse struct {
90		Summary    string `json:"summary"`
91		HasSummary bool   `json:"has_summary"`
92	}
93	
94	// TokenUsageResponse is the JSON returned by token calculation subcommands.
95	type TokenUsageResponse struct {
96		InputTokens         int                 `json:"input_tokens"`
97		CacheCreationTokens int                 `json:"cache_creation_tokens"`
98		CacheReadTokens     int                 `json:"cache_read_tokens"`
99		OutputTokens        int                 `json:"output_tokens"`
100		APICallCount        int                 `json:"api_call_count"`
101		SubagentTokens      *TokenUsageResponse `json:"subagent_tokens,omitempty"`
102	}
103	
104	// GenerateTextResponse is the JSON returned by the "generate-text" subcommand.
105	type GenerateTextResponse struct {
106		Text string `json:"text"`
107	}
108	
109	// CompactTranscriptResponse is the JSON returned by the "compact-transcript" subcommand.
110	type CompactTranscriptResponse struct {
111		Transcript string                           `json:"transcript"`
112		Assets     []CompactTranscriptAssetResponse `json:"assets,omitempty"`
113	}
114	
115	// CompactTranscriptAssetResponse describes one extracted binary asset.
116	type CompactTranscriptAssetResponse struct {
117		Name      string `json:"name"`
118		MediaType string `json:"media_type"`
119		Data      string `json:"data"`
120	}
121	
122	func (r *CompactTranscriptResponse) toCompactedTranscript() (*agent.CompactedTranscript, error) {
123		if r.Transcript == "" {
124			return nil, errors.New("compact-transcript: missing transcript")
125		}
126	
127		transcript, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(r.Transcript)
128		if err != nil {
129			return nil, fmt.Errorf("compact-transcript: decode transcript base64: %w", err)
130		}
131	
132		result := &agent.CompactedTranscript{
133			Transcript: transcript,
134		}
135		if len(r.Assets) == 0 {
136			return result, nil
137		}
138	
139		result.Assets = make([]agent.CompactedTranscriptAsset, 0, len(r.Assets))
140		for i, asset := range r.Assets {
141			if asset.Name == "" {
142				return nil, fmt.Errorf("compact-transcript: asset[%d] missing name", i)
143			}
144			if asset.MediaType == "" {
145				return nil, fmt.Errorf("compact-transcript: asset %q missing media_type", asset.Name)
146			}
147			data, decodeErr := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(asset.Data)
148			if decodeErr != nil {
149				return nil, fmt.Errorf("compact-transcript: decode asset %q base64: %w", asset.Name, decodeErr)
150			}
151			result.Assets = append(result.Assets, agent.CompactedTranscriptAsset{
152				Name:      asset.Name,
153				MediaType: asset.MediaType,
154				Data:      data,
155			})
156		}
157	
158		return result, nil
159	}
160	
161	// AgentSessionJSON is the JSON representation of agent.AgentSession for stdin/stdout transfer.
162	type AgentSessionJSON struct {
163		SessionID     string   `json:"session_id"`
164		AgentName     string   `json:"agent_name"`
165		RepoPath      string   `json:"repo_path"`
166		SessionRef    string   `json:"session_ref"`
167		StartTime     string   `json:"start_time"`
168		NativeData    []byte   `json:"native_data"`
169		ModifiedFiles []string `json:"modified_files"`
170		NewFiles      []string `json:"new_files"`
171		DeletedFiles  []string `json:"deleted_files"`
172	}
173	
174	// eventJSON is the JSON-tagged representation of agent.Event for protocol transfer.
175	type eventJSON struct {
176		Type              int               `json:"type"`
177		SessionID         string            `json:"session_id"`
178		PreviousSessionID string            `json:"previous_session_id,omitempty"`
179		SessionRef        string            `json:"session_ref,omitempty"`
180		Prompt            string            `json:"prompt,omitempty"`
181		Model             string            `json:"model,omitempty"`
182		Timestamp         string            `json:"timestamp,omitempty"`
183		ToolUseID         string            `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
184		SubagentID        string            `json:"subagent_id,omitempty"`
185		ToolInput         json.RawMessage   `json:"tool_input,omitempty"`
186		SubagentType      string            `json:"subagent_type,omitempty"`
187		TaskDescription   string            `json:"task_description,omitempty"`
188		ResponseMessage   string            `json:"response_message,omitempty"`
189		Metadata          map[string]string `json:"metadata,omitempty"`
190	}
191	
192	func (ej *eventJSON) toEvent() (*agent.Event, error) {
193		ev := &agent.Event{
194			Type:              agent.EventType(ej.Type),
195			SessionID:         ej.SessionID,
196			PreviousSessionID: ej.PreviousSessionID,
197			SessionRef:        ej.SessionRef,
198			Prompt:            ej.Prompt,
199			Model:             ej.Model,
200			ToolUseID:         ej.ToolUseID,
201			SubagentID:        ej.SubagentID,
202			ToolInput:         ej.ToolInput,
203			SubagentType:      ej.SubagentType,
204			TaskDescription:   ej.TaskDescription,
205			ResponseMessage:   ej.ResponseMessage,
206			Metadata:          ej.Metadata,
207		}
208		if ej.Timestamp != "" {
209			t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, ej.Timestamp)
210			if err != nil {
211				return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid timestamp: %w", err)
212			}
213			ev.Timestamp = t
214		}
215		return ev, nil
216	}
217	
218	// HookInputJSON is the JSON representation of agent.HookInput for stdin/stdout transfer.
219	type HookInputJSON struct {
220		HookType   string                 `json:"hook_type"`
221		SessionID  string                 `json:"session_id"`
222		SessionRef string                 `json:"session_ref"`
223		Timestamp  string                 `json:"timestamp"`
224		UserPrompt string                 `json:"user_prompt,omitempty"`
225		ToolName   string                 `json:"tool_name,omitempty"`
226		ToolUseID  string                 `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
227		ToolInput  json.RawMessage        `json:"tool_input,omitempty"`
228		RawData    map[string]interface{} `json:"raw_data,omitempty"`
229	}
230

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"os"
6	
7		"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/kiro"
8		"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-kiro/internal/protocol"
9	)
10	
11	func main() {
12		agent := kiro.New()
13	
14		if len(os.Args) < 2 {
15			fatalf("usage: entire-agent-kiro <subcommand> [args]")
16		}
17	
18		var err error
19	
20		switch os.Args[1] {
21		case "info":
22			err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, agent.Info())
23		case "detect":
24			err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, agent.Detect())
25		case "get-session-id":
26			err = protocol.HandleGetSessionID(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
27		case "get-session-dir":
28			err = protocol.HandleGetSessionDir(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
29		case "resolve-session-file":
30			err = protocol.HandleResolveSessionFile(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
31		case "read-session":
32			err = protocol.HandleReadSession(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
33		case "write-session":
34			err = protocol.HandleWriteSession(os.Stdin, agent)
35		case "read-transcript":
36			err = protocol.HandleReadTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
37		case "chunk-transcript":
38			err = protocol.HandleChunkTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
39		case "reassemble-transcript":
40			err = protocol.HandleReassembleTranscript(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
41		case "compact-transcript":
42			err = protocol.HandleCompactTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
43		case "format-resume-command":
44			err = protocol.HandleFormatResumeCommand(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
45		case "parse-hook":
46			err = protocol.HandleParseHook(os.Args[2:], os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
47		case "install-hooks":
48			err = protocol.HandleInstallHooks(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
49		case "uninstall-hooks":
50			err = agent.UninstallHooks()
51		case "are-hooks-installed":
52			err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, protocol.AreHooksInstalledResponse{
53				Installed: agent.AreHooksInstalled(),
54			})
55		case "get-transcript-position":
56			err = protocol.HandleGetTranscriptPosition(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
57		case "extract-modified-files":
58			err = protocol.HandleExtractModifiedFiles(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
59		case "extract-prompts":
60			err = protocol.HandleExtractPrompts(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
61		case "extract-summary":
62			err = protocol.HandleExtractSummary(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
63		default:
64			fatalf("unknown subcommand: %s", os.Args[1])
65		}
66	
67		if err != nil {
68			fatalf("%v", err)
69		}
70	}
71	
72	func fatalf(format string, args ...any) {
73		_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, format+"\n", args...)
74		os.Exit(1)
75	}
76

1	package protocol
2	
3	import (
4		"encoding/json"
5		"flag"
6		"io"
7		"os"
8		"path/filepath"
9		"time"
10	)
11	
12	type sessionDirResolver interface {
13		GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
14	}
15	
16	type sessionFileResolver interface {
17		ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID string) string
18	}
19	
20	type sessionIDProvider interface {
21		GetSessionID(*HookInputJSON) string
22	}
23	
24	type sessionReader interface {
25		ReadSession(*HookInputJSON) (AgentSessionJSON, error)
26	}
27	
28	type sessionWriter interface {
29		WriteSession(AgentSessionJSON) error
30	}
31	
32	type transcriptReader interface {
33		ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
34	}
35	
36	type transcriptChunker interface {
37		ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
38		ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
39	}
40	
41	type transcriptCompactor interface {
42		CompactTranscript(sessionRef string) (CompactTranscriptResponse, error)
43	}
44	
45	type resumeFormatter interface {
46		FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
47	}
48	
49	type hookParser interface {
50		ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*EventJSON, error)
51		InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
52		UninstallHooks() error
53		AreHooksInstalled() bool
54	}
55	
56	type transcriptAnalyzer interface {
57		GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
58		ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error)
59		ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, offset int) ([]string, error)
60		ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, bool, error)
61	}
62	
63	func WriteJSON(w io.Writer, v any) error {
64		enc := json.NewEncoder(w)
65		enc.SetEscapeHTML(false)
66		return enc.Encode(v)
67	}
68	
69	func ReadJSON[T any](r io.Reader) (*T, error) {
70		var value T
71		if err := json.NewDecoder(r).Decode(&value); err != nil {
72			return nil, err
73		}
74		return &value, nil
75	}
76	
77	func RepoRoot() string {
78		if root := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_REPO_ROOT"); root != "" {
79			return root
80		}
81		root, _ := os.Getwd()
82		return root
83	}
84	
85	func HandleGetSessionID(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, provider sessionIDProvider) error {
86		input, err := ReadJSON[HookInputJSON](stdin)
87		if err != nil {
88			return err
89		}
90		return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionIDResponse{SessionID: provider.GetSessionID(input)})
91	}
92	
93	func HandleGetSessionDir(args []string, stdout io.Writer, resolver sessionDirResolver) error {
94		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("get-session-dir", flag.ContinueOnError)
95		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
96		repoPath := fs.String("repo-path", "", "repo path")
97		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
98			return err
99		}
100		sessionDir, err := resolver.GetSessionDir(*repoPath)
101		if err != nil {
102			return err
103		}
104		return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionDirResponse{SessionDir: sessionDir})
105	}
106	
107	func HandleResolveSessionFile(args []string, stdout io.Writer, resolver sessionFileResolver) error {
108		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("resolve-session-file", flag.ContinueOnError)
109		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
110		sessionDir := fs.String("session-dir", "", "session dir")
111		sessionID := fs.String("session-id", "", "session id")
112		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
113			return err
114		}
115		return WriteJSON(stdout, SessionFileResponse{SessionFile: resolver.ResolveSessionFile(*sessionDir, *sessionID)})
116	}
117	
118	func HandleReadSession(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, reader sessionReader) error {
119		input, err := ReadJSON[HookInputJSON](stdin)
120		if err != nil {
121			return err
122		}
123		session, err := reader.ReadSession(input)
124		if err != nil {
125			return err
126		}
127		return WriteJSON(stdout, session)
128	}
129	
130	func HandleWriteSession(stdin io.Reader, writer sessionWriter) error {
131		session, err := ReadJSON[AgentSessionJSON](stdin)
132		if err != nil {
133			return err
134		}
135		return writer.WriteSession(*session)
136	}
137	
138	func HandleReadTranscript(args []string, stdout io.Writer, reader transcriptReader) error {
139		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("read-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
140		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
141		sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
142		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
143			return err
144		}
145		data, err := reader.ReadTranscript(*sessionRef)
146		if err != nil {
147			return err
148		}
149		_, err = stdout.Write(data)
150		return err
151	}
152	
153	func HandleChunkTranscript(args []string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, chunker transcriptChunker) error {
154		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("chunk-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
155		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
156		maxSize := fs.Int("max-size", 0, "max size")
157		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
158			return err
159		}
160		content, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
161		if err != nil {
162			return err
163		}
164		chunks, err := chunker.ChunkTranscript(content, *maxSize)
165		if err != nil {
166			return err
167		}
168		return WriteJSON(stdout, ChunkResponse{Chunks: chunks})
169	}
170	
171	func HandleReassembleTranscript(stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, chunker transcriptChunker) error {
172		input, err := ReadJSON[ChunkResponse](stdin)
173		if err != nil {
174			return err
175		}
176		data, err := chunker.ReassembleTranscript(input.Chunks)
177		if err != nil {
178			return err
179		}
180		_, err = stdout.Write(data)
181		return err
182	}
183	
184	func HandleCompactTranscript(args []string, stdout io.Writer, compactor transcriptCompactor) error {
185		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("compact-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
186		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
187		sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
188		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
189			return err
190		}
191		compacted, err := compactor.CompactTranscript(*sessionRef)
192		if err != nil {
193			return err
194		}
195		return WriteJSON(stdout, compacted)
196	}

198	func HandleFormatResumeCommand(args []string, stdout io.Writer, formatter resumeFormatter) error {
199		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("format-resume-command", flag.ContinueOnError)
200		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
201		sessionID := fs.String("session-id", "", "session id")
202		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
203			return err
204		}
205		return WriteJSON(stdout, ResumeCommandResponse{Command: formatter.FormatResumeCommand(*sessionID)})
206	}
207	
208	// readStdinWithTimeout reads from r but returns empty data if nothing arrives
209	// within the timeout. This prevents blocking when an IDE keeps stdin open
210	// without sending data (e.g., for hooks that have no payload).
211	func readStdinWithTimeout(r io.Reader, timeout time.Duration) ([]byte, error) {
212		type result struct {
213			data []byte
214			err  error
215		}
216		ch := make(chan result, 1)
217		go func() {
218			data, err := io.ReadAll(r)
219			ch <- result{data, err}
220		}()
221		select {
222		case res := <-ch:
223			return res.data, res.err
224		case <-time.After(timeout):
225			return nil, nil
226		}
227	}
228	
229	func HandleParseHook(args []string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, parser hookParser) error {
230		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("parse-hook", flag.ContinueOnError)
231		fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
232		hook := fs.String("hook", "", "hook name")
233		if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
234			return err
235		}
236		input, err := readStdinWithTimeout(stdin, 100*time.Millisecond)
237		if err != nil {
238			return err
239		}
240		event, err := parser.ParseHook(*hook, input)
241		if err != nil {
242			return err
243		}
244		if event == nil {
245			_, err = io.WriteString(stdout, "null\n")
246			return err
247		}
248		return WriteJSON(stdout, event)
249	}
250

257			return err
258		}

260		if err != nil {
261			return err
262		}
263		return WriteJSON(stdout, HooksInstalledCountResponse{HooksInstalled: count})
264	}

31	type ideHookDef struct {
32		Filename    string
33		TriggerType string
34		CLIVerb     string
35	}
36	
37	var ideHookDefs = []ideHookDef{
38		{Filename: "entire-prompt-submit", TriggerType: "promptSubmit", CLIVerb: HookNameUserPromptSubmit},
39		{Filename: "entire-stop", TriggerType: "agentStop", CLIVerb: HookNameStop},
40		{Filename: "entire-pre-tool-use", TriggerType: "preToolUse", CLIVerb: HookNamePreToolUse},
41		{Filename: "entire-post-tool-use", TriggerType: "postToolUse", CLIVerb: HookNamePostToolUse},

43	
44	func (a *Agent) ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*protocol.EventJSON, error) {
45		var raw hookInputRaw
46		if len(input) > 0 {
47			if err := json.Unmarshal(input, &raw); err != nil {
48				return nil, err
49			}
50		}
51	
52		switch hookName {
53		case HookNameAgentSpawn:
54			sessionID := a.generateAndCacheSessionID()
55			return &protocol.EventJSON{
56				Type:      1,
57				SessionID: sessionID,
58				Timestamp: time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
59			}, nil
60		case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
61			cwd := raw.CWD
62			if cwd == "" {
63				cwd = protocol.RepoRoot()
64			}
65			identity := a.resolveHookSessionIdentity(cwd, raw)
66			if identity.entireSessionID == "" {
67				identity.entireSessionID = a.generateAndCacheSessionID()
68			} else {
69				a.commitSessionIdentity(identity)
70			}
71			// Bind this turn to the resolved IDE chat so post-tool-use and
72			// stop hooks read/write the same chat even if the user switches
73			// tabs mid-turn (re-resolving by mtime alone could rebind).
74			// Only write when this is actually an IDE prompt — a CLI
75			// prompt with empty ideSessionID would otherwise DELETE the
76			// cache and strand any in-flight IDE turn's binding.
77			if identity.ideSessionID != "" {
78				a.writeActiveTurnIDESessionID(identity.ideSessionID)
79			}
80			prompt := raw.Prompt
81			if prompt == "" {
82				prompt = os.Getenv("USER_PROMPT")
83			}
84			return &protocol.EventJSON{
85				Type:      2,
86				SessionID: identity.entireSessionID,
87				Prompt:    prompt,
88				Timestamp: time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
89			}, nil
90		case HookNamePreToolUse:
91			return nil, nil
92		case HookNamePostToolUse:
93			if raw.ToolName != "" {
94				cwd := raw.CWD
95				if cwd == "" {
96					cwd = protocol.RepoRoot()
97				}
98				a.appendToolCall(raw.ToolName, raw.ToolInput, a.resolvePostToolUseChatKey(cwd, raw))
99			}
100			return nil, nil
101		case HookNameStop:
102			cwd := raw.CWD
103			if cwd == "" {
104				cwd = protocol.RepoRoot()
105			}
106			identity := a.resolveStopIdentity(cwd, raw)
107			if identity.entireSessionID == "" {
108				identity.entireSessionID = fallbackStopSessionID()
109			}
110			a.commitSessionIdentity(identity)
111			sessionRef := a.captureTranscriptForStop(cwd, identity)
112			a.clearToolCalls(identity.ideSessionID)
113			// Only clear the IDE turn binding when this stop owns it. CLI
114			// stops (no ideSessionID) must leave kiro-active-turn alone so
115			// a concurrent in-flight IDE turn isn't stranded; and an IDE
116			// stop must only clear the cache when it still names THIS
117			// stop's chat — otherwise overlapping IDE turns
118			// (A prompt -> B prompt -> A stop) would let A's stop delete
119			// B's binding.
120			if identity.ideSessionID != "" {
121				if cached := a.readActiveTurnIDESessionID(); cached == identity.ideSessionID {
122					a.clearActiveTurnIDESessionID()
123				}
124			}
125			return &protocol.EventJSON{
126				Type:       3,
127				SessionID:  identity.entireSessionID,
128				SessionRef: sessionRef,
129				Timestamp:  time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
130			}, nil
131		default:
132			return nil, nil
133		}
134	}
135	
136	func (a *Agent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
137		repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
138		if !force && allHooksInstalled(repoRoot, localDev) && trustedCommandsPresent(repoRoot, localDev) {
139			return 0, nil
140		}
141	
142		if err := writeCLIHooks(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
143			return 0, err
144		}
145		if err := writeIDEHooks(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
146			return 0, err
147		}
148		if err := installTrustedCommands(repoRoot, localDev); err != nil {
149			return 0, err
150		}
151	
152		return len(defaultHookNames()) + len(ideHookDefs), nil
153	}
154	
155	func (a *Agent) UninstallHooks() error {
156		repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
157		cliPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
158		if err := os.Remove(cliPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
159			return err
160		}
161		for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
162			path := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
163			if err := os.Remove(path); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
164				return err
165			}
166		}
167		if err := uninstallTrustedCommands(repoRoot); err != nil {
168			return err
169		}
170		return nil
171	}
172	
173	func (a *Agent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
174		repoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()
175		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", "agents", "entire.json")); err == nil {
176			return true
177		}
178		if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", "hooks", "entire-stop.kiro.hook")); err == nil {
179			return true
180		}
181		return false
182	}

186			HookNameAgentSpawn,
187			HookNameUserPromptSubmit,

189			HookNamePostToolUse,

194	func writeCLIHooks(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
195		hooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
196		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o700); err != nil {
197			return err
198		}
199		commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
200	
201		file := kiroAgentFile{
202			Name: "entire",
203			Tools: []string{
204				"read", "write", "shell", "grep", "glob",
205				"aws", "report", "introspect", "knowledge",
206				"thinking", "todo", "delegate",
207			},
208			Hooks: kiroHooks{
209				AgentSpawn:       []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameAgentSpawn}},
210				UserPromptSubmit: []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameUserPromptSubmit}},
211				PreToolUse:       []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNamePreToolUse}},
212				PostToolUse:      []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNamePostToolUse}},
213				Stop:             []kiroHookEntry{{Command: commandBase + HookNameStop}},
214			},
215		}
216	
217		data, err := marshalJSON(file)
218		if err != nil {
219			return err
220		}
221		return os.WriteFile(hooksPath, data, 0o600)
222	}
223	
224	func writeIDEHooks(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
225		dir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir)
226		if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o700); err != nil {
227			return err
228		}
229		commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
230	
231		for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
232			hook := kiroIDEHookFile{
233				Enabled:     true,
234				Name:        def.Filename,
235				Description: "Entire CLI " + def.TriggerType + " hook",
236				Version:     ideHookVersion,
237				When: kiroIDEHookWhen{
238					Type: def.TriggerType,
239				},
240				Then: kiroIDEHookThen{
241					Type:    "runCommand",
242					Command: shellWrappedCommand(commandBase + def.CLIVerb),
243				},
244			}
245			data, err := marshalJSON(hook)
246			if err != nil {
247				return err
248			}
249			path := filepath.Join(dir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
250			if err := os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600); err != nil {
251				return err
252			}
253		}
254	
255		return nil
256	}

258	func allHooksInstalled(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
259		cliPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", hooksDir, hooksFileName)
260		commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
261		if data, err := os.ReadFile(cliPath); err == nil {
262			var file kiroAgentFile
263			if json.Unmarshal(data, &file) == nil &&
264				hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.AgentSpawn, commandBase+HookNameAgentSpawn) &&
265				hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, commandBase+HookNameUserPromptSubmit) &&
266				hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.PreToolUse, commandBase+HookNamePreToolUse) &&
267				hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.PostToolUse, commandBase+HookNamePostToolUse) &&
268				hookCommandExists(file.Hooks.Stop, commandBase+HookNameStop) &&
269				allIDEHooksPresent(repoRoot, localDev) {
270				return true
271			}
272		}
273		return false
274	}
275	
276	func hookCommandExists(entries []kiroHookEntry, command string) bool {
277		for _, entry := range entries {
278			if entry.Command == command {
279				return true
280			}
281		}
282		return false
283	}
284	
285	func allIDEHooksPresent(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
286		commandBase := hookCommandBase(localDev)
287		for _, def := range ideHookDefs {
288			path := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".kiro", ideHooksDir, def.Filename+ideHookFileSuffix)
289			data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
290			if err != nil {
291				return false
292			}
293			var hook kiroIDEHookFile
294			if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hook); err != nil {
295				return false
296			}
297			if hook.Then.Command != shellWrappedCommand(commandBase+def.CLIVerb) {
298				return false
299			}
300		}
301		return true
302	}

304	func trustedCommandsPresent(repoRoot string, localDev bool) bool {
305		settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
306		data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
307		if err != nil {
308			return false
309		}
310		var settings map[string]json.RawMessage
311		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
312			return false
313		}
314		raw, ok := settings[trustedCommandsKey]
315		if !ok {
316			return false
317		}
318		var commands []string
319		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &commands); err != nil {
320			return false
321		}
322		want := trustedCommand(localDev)
323		for _, command := range commands {
324			if command == want {
325				return true
326			}
327		}
328		return false
329	}
330	
331	func installTrustedCommands(repoRoot string, localDev bool) error {
332		settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
333	
334		settings, err := readSettings(settingsPath)
335		if err != nil {
336			return err
337		}
338		commands, err := readTrustedCommands(settings)
339		if err != nil {
340			return err
341		}
342		want := trustedCommand(localDev)
343		commands = removeKnownTrustedCommands(commands)
344		commands = append(commands, want)
345		raw, err := json.Marshal(commands)
346		if err != nil {
347			return err
348		}
349		settings[trustedCommandsKey] = raw
350		return writeSettings(settingsPath, settings)
351	}
352	
353	func uninstallTrustedCommands(repoRoot string) error {
354		settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, vscodeSettingsDir, vscodeSettingsFile)
355		if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err != nil {
356			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
357				return nil
358			}
359			return err
360		}
361		settings, err := readSettings(settingsPath)
362		if err != nil {
363			return err
364		}
365		commands, err := readTrustedCommands(settings)
366		if err != nil {
367			return err
368		}
369		filtered := commands[:0]
370		for _, command := range commands {
371			if !isKnownTrustedCommand(command) {
372				filtered = append(filtered, command)
373			}
374		}
375		if len(filtered) == 0 {
376			delete(settings, trustedCommandsKey)
377		} else {
378			raw, err := json.Marshal(filtered)
379			if err != nil {
380				return err
381			}
382			settings[trustedCommandsKey] = raw
383		}
384		return writeSettings(settingsPath, settings)
385	}
386	
387	func hookCommandBase(localDev bool) string {
388		if localDev {
389			if runtimeGOOS == osWindows {
390				return "go run %KIRO_PROJECT_DIR%/cmd/entire/main.go hooks kiro "
391			}
392			return "go run ${KIRO_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks kiro "
393		}
394		return "entire hooks kiro "
395	}
396	
397	// shellWrappedCommand wraps a hook command so the IDE-spawned process always
398	// has a closed stdin. IDEs run hook commands directly without a shell, so a
399	// bare redirect token is passed as a literal arg instead of being interpreted.
400	// On POSIX we wrap in `sh -c '<cmd> </dev/null'`; on Windows we wrap in
401	// `cmd /c "<cmd> <NUL"` so the parent CLI process does not block reading from
402	// an inherited Kiro IDE stdin pipe that never gets closed.
403	// The command content is built from compile-time constants (not user input).
404	func shellWrappedCommand(cmd string) string {
405		if runtimeGOOS == osWindows {
406			return `cmd /c "` + cmd + ` <NUL"`
407		}
408		return "sh -c '" + cmd + " </dev/null'"
409	}
410	
411	func trustedCommand(localDev bool) string {
412		if localDev {
413			if runtimeGOOS == osWindows {
414				return `cmd /c "go run %KIRO_PROJECT_DIR%/cmd/entire/main.go hooks *`
415			}
416			return "sh -c 'go run ${KIRO_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks *"
417		}
418		if runtimeGOOS == osWindows {
419			return `cmd /c "entire hooks *`
420		}
421		return "sh -c 'entire hooks *"
422	}
423	
424	func knownTrustedCommands() []string {
425		return []string{
426			"sh -c 'entire hooks *",
427			"sh -c 'go run ${KIRO_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks *",
428			`cmd /c "entire hooks *`,
429			`cmd /c "go run %KIRO_PROJECT_DIR%/cmd/entire/main.go hooks *`,
430			"entire hooks *",
431			"go run %KIRO_PROJECT_DIR%/cmd/entire/main.go hooks *",
432		}
433	}
434	
435	func isKnownTrustedCommand(command string) bool {
436		for _, known := range knownTrustedCommands() {
437			if command == known {
438				return true
439			}
440		}
441		return false
442	}
443	
444	func removeKnownTrustedCommands(commands []string) []string {
445		filtered := commands[:0]
446		for _, command := range commands {
447			if !isKnownTrustedCommand(command) {
448				filtered = append(filtered, command)
449			}
450		}
451		return filtered
452	}

454	func readSettings(path string) (map[string]json.RawMessage, error) {
455		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
456		if err != nil {
457			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
458				return map[string]json.RawMessage{}, nil
459			}
460			return nil, err
461		}
462		var settings map[string]json.RawMessage
463		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
464			return nil, err
465		}
466		if settings == nil {
467			settings = map[string]json.RawMessage{}
468		}
469		return settings, nil
470	}
471	
472	func readTrustedCommands(settings map[string]json.RawMessage) ([]string, error) {
473		raw, ok := settings[trustedCommandsKey]
474		if !ok {
475			return []string{}, nil
476		}
477		var commands []string
478		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &commands); err != nil {
479			return nil, err
480		}
481		return commands, nil
482	}
483	
484	func writeSettings(path string, settings map[string]json.RawMessage) error {
485		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {
486			return err
487		}
488		data, err := marshalJSON(settings)
489		if err != nil {
490			return err
491		}
492		return os.WriteFile(path, data, 0o600)
493	}
494	
495	func marshalJSON(v any) ([]byte, error) {
496		var buf bytes.Buffer
497		enc := json.NewEncoder(&buf)
498		enc.SetEscapeHTML(false)
499		enc.SetIndent("", "  ")
500		if err := enc.Encode(v); err != nil {
501			return nil, err
502		}
503		return buf.Bytes(), nil
504	}
505	
506	func (a *Agent) generateAndCacheSessionID() string {
507		sessionID := generateSessionID()
508		cachePath := a.sessionIDCachePath()
509		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(cachePath), 0o700); err == nil {
510			_ = os.WriteFile(cachePath, []byte(sessionID), 0o600)
511		}
512		return sessionID
513	}
514	
515	func (a *Agent) readCachedSessionID() string {
516		data, err := os.ReadFile(a.sessionIDCachePath())
517		if err != nil {
518			return ""
519		}
520		return strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
521	}
522	
523	func (a *Agent) cacheSessionID(sessionID string) {
524		if sessionID == "" {
525			return
526		}
527		cachePath := a.sessionIDCachePath()
528		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(cachePath), 0o700); err == nil {
529			_ = os.WriteFile(cachePath, []byte(sessionID), 0o600)
530		}
531	}
532	
533	// clearToolCalls removes the per-chat tool-calls jsonl file (if any).
534	// Called defensively at end-of-turn in case enrichment skipped the
535	// readAndClearToolCalls path. chatKey="" targets the legacy global file
536	// used by Kiro CLI sessions that have no IDE chat ID.
537	func (a *Agent) clearToolCalls(chatKey string) {
538		_ = os.Remove(a.toolCallsPath(chatKey))
539	}
540	
541	// appendToolCall records one post-tool-use entry to the jsonl file for the
542	// given chat. Per-chat keying prevents one Kiro chat's tool calls from
543	// being captured by another chat's stop hook in multi-chat workspaces.
544	func (a *Agent) appendToolCall(name string, input json.RawMessage, chatKey string) {
545		call := kiroToolCall{Name: name, Args: input}
546		line, err := json.Marshal(call)
547		if err != nil {
548			return
549		}
550		path := a.toolCallsPath(chatKey)
551		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err != nil {
552			return
553		}
554		f, err := os.OpenFile(path, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY, 0o600)
555		if err != nil {
556			return
557		}
558		defer func() { _ = f.Close() }()
559		_, _ = f.Write(append(line, '\n'))
560	}
561	
562	// readAndClearToolCalls drains the per-chat tool-calls jsonl into memory
563	// and removes the file. chatKey="" reads the legacy global file (CLI flow).
564	func (a *Agent) readAndClearToolCalls(chatKey string) []kiroToolCall {
565		path := a.toolCallsPath(chatKey)
566		data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
567		if err != nil {
568			return nil
569		}
570		_ = os.Remove(path)
571	
572		var calls []kiroToolCall
573		for _, line := range strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(data)), "\n") {
574			if line == "" {
575				continue
576			}
577			var call kiroToolCall
578			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &call); err == nil {
579				calls = append(calls, call)
580			}
581		}
582		return calls
583	}
584	
585	// toolCallsPath returns the per-chat tool-calls file. chatKey="" returns
586	// the legacy global file shared by all Kiro CLI sessions on this repo
587	// (CLI is single-conversation per-cwd, so global is fine there).
588	func (a *Agent) toolCallsPath(chatKey string) string {
589		dir := filepath.Join(protocol.RepoRoot(), ".entire", "tmp")
590		if chatKey == "" {
591			return filepath.Join(dir, toolCallsFile)
592		}
593		return filepath.Join(dir, "kiro-tool-calls-"+sanitizeForFilename(chatKey)+".jsonl")
594	}
595	
596	func (a *Agent) sessionIDCachePath() string {
597		return filepath.Join(protocol.RepoRoot(), ".entire", "tmp", sessionIDFile)
598	}
599	
600	// activeTurnIDECachePath holds the IDE session ID for the in-flight turn
601	// (set at user-prompt-submit, cleared at stop). It binds the whole turn —
602	// post-tool-use writes and stop reads — to the chat that fired prompt-submit
603	// so a tab switch mid-turn cannot rebind to a different chat.
604	func (a *Agent) activeTurnIDECachePath() string {
605		return filepath.Join(protocol.RepoRoot(), ".entire", "tmp", activeTurnFile)
606	}
607	
608	func (a *Agent) readActiveTurnIDESessionID() string {
609		data, err := os.ReadFile(a.activeTurnIDECachePath())
610		if err != nil {
611			return ""
612		}
613		return strings.TrimSpace(string(data))
614	}
615	
616	func (a *Agent) writeActiveTurnIDESessionID(ideSessionID string) {
617		path := a.activeTurnIDECachePath()
618		if ideSessionID == "" {
619			_ = os.Remove(path)
620			return
621		}
622		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o700); err == nil {
623			_ = os.WriteFile(path, []byte(ideSessionID), 0o600)
624		}
625	}
626	
627	func (a *Agent) clearActiveTurnIDESessionID() {
628		_ = os.Remove(a.activeTurnIDECachePath())
629	}
630	
631	// resolvePostToolUseChatKey decides which tool-calls jsonl file a
632	// post-tool-use hook should append to. The key MUST match what the
633	// corresponding stop hook will read with, or the tool calls get
634	// stranded in a file the stop never consumes.
635	//
636	// Precedence:
637	//  1. Explicit IDE session ID in payload — write to that IDE chat's file
638	//     (matches resolveStopIdentity / resolveHookSessionIdentity).
639	//  2. Explicit CLI conversation_id in payload — write to the global file
640	//     (key=""), matching CLI stop's `clearToolCalls("")`. We do NOT fall
641	//     back to the latest IDE chat here; that would silently route CLI
642	//     tool calls into an IDE-scoped file that CLI stop never clears.
643	//  3. Active-turn IDE cache — write to that IDE chat's file.
644	//  4. Most-recently-active IDE chat by mtime — best-effort guess for a
645	//     stray post-tool-use without prior prompt-submit.
646	//  5. "" (global file) when no IDE chat exists at all (pure CLI flow).
647	func (a *Agent) resolvePostToolUseChatKey(cwd string, raw hookInputRaw) string {
648		if ideID := rawIDESessionID(raw); ideID != "" {
649			return ideID
650		}
651		if convID := rawConversationID(raw); convID != "" {
652			// CLI flow — global tool-calls file (CLI is single-conversation
653			// per cwd, and stop will clear with key="").
654			return ""
655		}
656		if id := a.readActiveTurnIDESessionID(); id != "" {
657			return id
658		}
659		if cwd == "" {
660			return ""
661		}
662		id, err := mostRecentlyActiveIDESessionID(cwd)
663		if err != nil {
664			return ""
665		}
666		return id
667	}
668	
669	// resolveStopIdentity is the stop-hook variant of resolveHookSessionIdentity
670	// that prefers the IDE session ID bound to the current turn (set at
671	// user-prompt-submit) over fresh mtime resolution. Without this, a tab
672	// switch between user-prompt-submit and stop would let the turn finalize
673	// against a different chat than the one that fired the prompt.
674	func (a *Agent) resolveStopIdentity(cwd string, raw hookInputRaw) sessionIdentity {
675		if ideID := rawIDESessionID(raw); ideID != "" {
676			return sessionIdentity{
677				entireSessionID: ideID,
678				ideSessionID:    ideID,
679				conversationID:  rawConversationID(raw),
680			}
681		}
682		// Explicit CLI conversation_id in the payload must beat the IDE
683		// active-turn cache. The cache is repo-global, so a concurrent IDE
684		// turn could otherwise hijack a CLI stop and route it through the
685		// IDE transcript path.
686		if convID := rawConversationID(raw); convID != "" {
687			return sessionIdentity{
688				entireSessionID: convID,
689				conversationID:  convID,
690			}
691		}
692		if turnIDE := a.readActiveTurnIDESessionID(); turnIDE != "" {
693			// Intentionally leave conversationID empty. We have no proof
694			// that any CLI conversation in this repo belongs to this IDE
695			// turn, and if ensureIDETranscript later degrades the CLI
696			// fallback would otherwise capture an unrelated CLI transcript
697			// under the IDE session's file.
698			return sessionIdentity{
699				entireSessionID: turnIDE,
700				ideSessionID:    turnIDE,
701			}
702		}
703		return a.resolveHookSessionIdentity(cwd, raw)
704	}
705	
706	// sessionIdentity is the resolved session state for a single hook invocation.
707	//   - entireSessionID is the stable Entire-side ID emitted on EventJSON. For
708	//     IDE-backed chats it equals ideSessionID, which keeps each Kiro chat
709	//     mapped to a deterministic, lifetime-stable Entire session without
710	//     needing per-chat state files.
711	//   - ideSessionID is the Kiro IDE session UUID for the current chat, used
712	//     for transcript file lookup and per-chat trim-offset bucketing. Empty
713	//     when no IDE chat is in play (CLI-only sessions).
714	//   - conversationID is the Kiro CLI conversation_id, used for SQLite
715	//     transcript queries. Empty when no CLI session is in play.
716	type sessionIdentity struct {
717		entireSessionID string
718		ideSessionID    string
719		conversationID  string
720	}
721	
722	// resolveHookSessionIdentity decides the (entireSessionID, ideSessionID,
723	// conversationID) tuple for the current hook event.
724	//
725	// Precedence:
726	//  1. Explicit `session_id` / `sessionId` / `chatSessionId` / `conversation_id`
727	//     in the raw hook payload — Kiro is telling us directly.
728	//  2. Most-recently-active IDE chat detected via mtime of `<id>.json` files
729	//     in the workspace-sessions directory. The IDE writes the user prompt /
730	//     assistant response to the active chat's transcript file before firing
731	//     the hook, so the freshest mtime points at the chat that fired. We use
732	//     that IDE session UUID as both the Kiro and Entire session identifiers
733	//     so each chat is keyed deterministically — no global cache that could
734	//     overwrite another chat's state, and no rekey needed.
735	//  3. Repo-global `kiro-active-session` cache (CLI flow), then CLI
736	//     conversation_id, then empty (caller mints a UUID).
737	func (a *Agent) resolveHookSessionIdentity(cwd string, raw hookInputRaw) sessionIdentity {
738		identity := sessionIdentity{}
739	
740		// Explicit IDE session ID in the payload wins for IDE flows.
741		if ideID := rawIDESessionID(raw); ideID != "" {
742			identity.entireSessionID = ideID
743			identity.ideSessionID = ideID
744			identity.conversationID = rawConversationID(raw)
745			return identity
746		}
747	
748		// Explicit CLI conversation_id in the payload tells us this is a CLI
749		// turn — short-circuit before IDE mtime discovery so a repo that
750		// happens to have IDE workspace data alongside CLI usage doesn't
751		// accidentally checkpoint an unrelated IDE chat. ideSessionID stays
752		// empty so captureTranscriptForStop skips IDE transcript capture.
753		if convID := rawConversationID(raw); convID != "" {
754			identity.entireSessionID = convID
755			identity.conversationID = convID
756			return identity
757		}
758	
759		// No payload signals — fall back to disk discovery. CLI conversation
760		// queried from SQLite goes only into conversationID; mtime-detected
761		// IDE chat sets both entireSessionID and ideSessionID.
762		if cwd != "" {
763			if cid, err := a.querySessionID(cwd); err == nil {
764				identity.conversationID = cid
765			}
766		}
767	
768		if cwd != "" {
769			if activeIDE, err := mostRecentlyActiveIDESessionID(cwd); err == nil && activeIDE != "" {
770				identity.entireSessionID = activeIDE
771				identity.ideSessionID = activeIDE
772				return identity
773			}
774		}
775	
776		if cached := a.readCachedSessionID(); cached != "" {
777			identity.entireSessionID = cached
778			return identity
779		}
780	
781		if identity.conversationID != "" {
782			identity.entireSessionID = identity.conversationID
783		}
784		return identity
785	}
786	
787	// commitSessionIdentity persists the resolved identity to the legacy
788	// repo-global cache so subsequent hooks in the CLI (non-IDE) flow can reuse
789	// it. IDE-backed identities don't need persistence — they're re-derived
790	// from the IDE workspace-sessions directory each time, which is the only
791	// way to correctly handle multi-chat workspaces without per-tab signals.
792	func (a *Agent) commitSessionIdentity(identity sessionIdentity) {
793		if identity.ideSessionID != "" {
794			// Per-chat IDE state is derived from disk; no cache write needed.
795			// Doing nothing here also avoids overwriting the CLI cache from an
796			// IDE turn (which would corrupt a concurrent Kiro CLI session).
797			return
798		}
799		a.cacheSessionID(identity.entireSessionID)
800	}
801	
802	// rawIDESessionID returns the IDE session UUID supplied in the hook
803	// payload, if any. CLI's `conversation_id` is intentionally NOT
804	// considered here: mistaking it for an IDE session ID would cause the
805	// stop hook to attempt IDE transcript capture against a non-existent
806	// `<conversation_id>.json` and then silently fall back to the latest IDE
807	// session, checkpointing an unrelated chat.
808	func rawIDESessionID(raw hookInputRaw) string {
809		for _, value := range []string{
810			raw.SessionID,
811			raw.SessionIDAlt,
812			raw.ChatSessionID,
813		} {
814			if v := strings.TrimSpace(value); v != "" {
815				return v
816			}
817		}
818		return ""
819	}
820	
821	// rawConversationID returns only the CLI `conversation_id` field. It does
822	// NOT fall back to `chatSessionId`, which references a Kiro IDE
823	// execution log and would point ensureCachedTranscript at a wrong
824	// SQLite row.
825	func rawConversationID(raw hookInputRaw) string {
826		return strings.TrimSpace(raw.ConversationID)
827	}
828	
829	func fallbackStopSessionID() string {
830		return generateSessionID()
831	}
832	
833	func generateSessionID() string {
834		buf := make([]byte, 16)
835		if _, err := rand.Read(buf); err != nil {
836			return fmt.Sprintf("kiro-%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
837		}
838		return hex.EncodeToString(buf)
839	}
840

1	package agents
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"os"
6		"strconv"
7		"strings"
8		"time"
9	)
10	
11	// Output holds the result of running an agent prompt.
12	type Output struct {
13		Command  string
14		Stdout   string
15		Stderr   string
16		ExitCode int
17	}
18	
19	// Option configures how an agent prompt is executed.
20	type Option func(*runConfig)
21	type runConfig struct {
22		Model          string
23		PermissionMode string
24		PromptTimeout  time.Duration
25	}
26	
27	// WithModel sets the model to use for the prompt.
28	func WithModel(model string) Option {
29		return func(c *runConfig) { c.Model = model }
30	}
31	
32	// WithPermissionMode sets the permission mode for the prompt.
33	func WithPermissionMode(mode string) Option {
34		return func(c *runConfig) { c.PermissionMode = mode }
35	}
36	
37	// WithPromptTimeout sets a custom timeout for the prompt execution.
38	func WithPromptTimeout(d time.Duration) Option {
39		return func(c *runConfig) { c.PromptTimeout = d }
40	}
41	
42	// Agent defines the interface for an AI coding agent that can be tested.
43	type Agent interface {
44		Name() string
45		// Binary returns the CLI binary name (e.g. "kiro-cli-chat").
46		Binary() string
47		EntireAgent() string
48		PromptPattern() string
49		// TimeoutMultiplier returns a factor applied to per-test timeouts.
50		// Slower agents return values > 1.
51		TimeoutMultiplier() float64
52		RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error)
53		StartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error)
54		// Bootstrap performs one-time CI setup (auth config, warmup, etc.).
55		// Called before any tests run. Implementations should be idempotent.
56		Bootstrap() error
57		// IsTransientError returns true if the error from RunPrompt looks like
58		// a transient API failure (e.g. 500, rate limit, network error) that
59		// is worth retrying.
60		IsTransientError(out Output, err error) bool
61	}
62	
63	// Session represents an interactive terminal session with an agent.
64	type Session interface {
65		Send(input string) error
66		WaitFor(pattern string, timeout time.Duration) (string, error)
67		Capture() string
68		Close() error
69	}
70	
71	// ExternalAgent is an optional interface for agents discovered via the
72	// external agent protocol (entire-agent-* binaries). SetupRepo uses this
73	// to pre-configure external_agents in settings before running `entire enable`.
74	type ExternalAgent interface {
75		IsExternalAgent() bool
76	}
77	
78	// registry and gates are populated by init() functions in agent implementation
79	// files (e.g. kiro.go). Go guarantees init() functions run sequentially, so
80	// no synchronization is needed. Do not call Register/RegisterGate from tests.
81	var registry []Agent
82	var gates = map[string]chan struct{}{}
83	
84	// Register adds an agent to the global registry.
85	func Register(a Agent) {
86		registry = append(registry, a)
87	}
88	
89	// RegisterGate sets a concurrency limit for an agent's tests.
90	// Tests call AcquireSlot/ReleaseSlot to respect this limit.
91	// The limit can be overridden via E2E_CONCURRENT_TEST_LIMIT.
92	func RegisterGate(name string, defaultMax int) {
93		max := defaultMax
94		if v, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv("E2E_CONCURRENT_TEST_LIMIT")); err == nil && v > 0 {
95			max = v
96		}
97		gates[name] = make(chan struct{}, max)
98	}
99	
100	// AcquireSlot blocks until a test slot is available for the agent or the
101	// context is cancelled. Returns a non-nil error if the context expires
102	// before a slot opens.
103	func AcquireSlot(ctx context.Context, a Agent) error {
104		g, ok := gates[a.Name()]
105		if !ok {
106			return nil
107		}
108		select {
109		case g <- struct{}{}:
110			return nil
111		case <-ctx.Done():
112			return ctx.Err()
113		}
114	}
115	
116	// ReleaseSlot frees a test slot for the agent.
117	func ReleaseSlot(a Agent) {
118		if g, ok := gates[a.Name()]; ok {
119			<-g
120		}
121	}
122	
123	// All returns all registered agents.
124	func All() []Agent {
125		return registry
126	}
127	
128	// filterEnv returns env with entries matching any of the given variable names
129	// removed. Used to strip test-only overrides from agent processes.
130	func filterEnv(env []string, names ...string) []string {
131		out := make([]string, 0, len(env))
132		for _, e := range env {
133			skip := false
134			for _, name := range names {
135				if strings.HasPrefix(e, name+"=") {
136					skip = true
137					break
138				}
139			}
140			if !skip {
141				out = append(out, e)
142			}
143		}
144		return out
145	}
146

1	package agents

3	import (
4		"context"
