14	func init() {
15		if env := os.Getenv("E2E_AGENT"); env != "" && env != "amp" {
16			return
17		}
18		Register(&Amp{})
19		RegisterGate("amp", 2)
20	}
21	
22	type Amp struct{}
23	
24	func (a *Amp) Name() string               { return "amp" }
25	func (a *Amp) Binary() string             { return "amp" }
26	func (a *Amp) EntireAgent() string        { return "amp" }
27	func (a *Amp) PromptPattern() string      { return `>` }
28	func (a *Amp) TimeoutMultiplier() float64 { return 1.5 }
29	func (a *Amp) IsExternalAgent() bool      { return true }
30	
31	func (a *Amp) IsTransientError(out Output, _ error) bool {
32		combined := strings.ToLower(out.Stdout + out.Stderr)
33		patterns := []string{
34			"overloaded",
35			"rate limit",
36			"429",
37			"500",
38			"503",
39			"econnreset",
40			"etimedout",
41			"timeout",
42		}
43		for _, pattern := range patterns {
44			if strings.Contains(combined, pattern) {
45				return true
46			}
47		}
48		return false
49	}
50	
51	func (a *Amp) Bootstrap() error {
52		return nil
53	}
54	
55	func (a *Amp) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error) {
56		cfg := &runConfig{}
57		for _, o := range opts {
58			o(cfg)
59		}
60	
61		bin, err := exec.LookPath(a.Binary())
62		if err != nil {
63			return Output{}, fmt.Errorf("%s not in PATH: %w", a.Binary(), err)
64		}
65	
66		args := []string{"--dangerously-allow-all", "--no-notifications", "--no-ide", "-x", prompt}
67		displayArgs := []string{"--dangerously-allow-all", "--no-notifications", "--no-ide", "-x", fmt.Sprintf("%q", prompt)}
68	
69		env := append(filterEnv(os.Environ(), "ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"), "PLUGINS=all")
70	
71		cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, bin, args...)
72		cmd.Dir = dir
73		cmd.Env = env
74		cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}
75		cmd.Cancel = func() error {
76			return syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)
77		}
78		cmd.WaitDelay = 5 * time.Second
79	
80		var stdout, stderr strings.Builder
81		cmd.Stdout = &stdout
82		cmd.Stderr = &stderr
83	
84		err = cmd.Run()
85		exitCode := 0
86		if err != nil {
87			exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
88			if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
89				exitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
90			} else {
91				exitCode = -1
92			}
93		}
94	
95		return Output{
96			Command:  "PLUGINS=all " + a.Binary() + " " + strings.Join(displayArgs, " "),
97			Stdout:   stdout.String(),
98			Stderr:   stderr.String(),
99			ExitCode: exitCode,
100		}, err
101	}
102	
103	func (a *Amp) StartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error) {
104		name := fmt.Sprintf("amp-test-%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
105		s, err := NewTmuxSession(name, dir, []string{"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY"}, "env", "PLUGINS=all", a.Binary())
106		if err != nil {
107			return nil, err
108		}
109		if _, err := s.WaitFor(a.PromptPattern(), 30*time.Second); err != nil {
110			_ = s.Close()
111			return nil, fmt.Errorf("waiting for initial prompt: %w", err)
112		}
113		s.stableAtSend = ""
114		return s, nil
115	}
116

19	func TestMain(m *testing.M) {
20		tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "e2e-agents-*")
21		if err != nil {
22			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to create temp dir: %v\n", err)
23			os.Exit(1)
24		}
25		defer os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
26	
27		discoveredAgents, err := DiscoverAgents()
28		if err != nil {
29			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to discover agents: %v\n", err)
30			os.Exit(1)
31		}
32	
33		if len(discoveredAgents) == 0 {
34			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "no agents found in agents/ directory")
35			os.Exit(1)
36		}
37	
38		for _, agentDir := range discoveredAgents {
39			agentName := filepath.Base(agentDir)
40			fmt.Printf("Building %s...\n", agentName)
41			binPath, err := BuildAgent(agentName, tmpDir)
42			if err != nil {
43				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "failed to build %s: %v\n", agentName, err)
44				os.Exit(1)
45			}
46			AgentBinaries[agentName] = binPath
47			fmt.Printf("Built %s -> %s\n", agentName, binPath)
48		}
49	
50		// Add the temp bin directory to PATH so that `entire enable` can discover
51		// the agent binaries (e.g. entire-agent-kiro) during lifecycle tests.
52		os.Setenv("PATH", tmpDir+string(os.PathListSeparator)+os.Getenv("PATH"))
53	
54		// --- Artifact directory setup ---
55		runDir := os.Getenv("E2E_ARTIFACT_DIR")
56		if runDir == "" {
57			_, file, _, _ := runtime.Caller(0)
58			testutil.ArtifactRoot = filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(file), "artifacts")
59			runDir = testutil.ArtifactRunDir()
60		}
61		_ = os.MkdirAll(runDir, 0o755)
62		testutil.SetRunDir(runDir)
63	
64		// Prepend the entire binary's directory to PATH so git hooks resolve
65		// to the same binary the test harness uses.
66		entireBin := entire.BinPath()
67		if dir := filepath.Dir(entireBin); dir != "." {
68			os.Setenv("PATH", dir+string(os.PathListSeparator)+os.Getenv("PATH"))
69		}
70	
71		// --- Preflight checks ---
72		if _, err := exec.LookPath("tmux"); err != nil {
73			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "warning: tmux not found — interactive session tests will fail")
74		}
75	
76		// Write entire version info to artifact dir.
77		version := "unknown"
78		if out, err := exec.Command(entireBin, "version").Output(); err == nil {
79			version = string(out)
80		}
81		preflight := fmt.Sprintf("entire binary:  %s\nentire version: %s\n", entireBin, version)
82		_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(runDir, "entire-version.txt"), []byte(preflight), 0o644)
83	
84		// Isolate git config to prevent user's ~/.gitconfig from interfering.
85		os.Setenv("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
86	
87		os.Exit(m.Run())
88	}

	input, err := ReadJSON[ChunkResponse](stdin)

	}

func HandleCompactTranscript(args []string, stdout io.Writer, compactor transcriptCompactor) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("compact-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
	sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	compacted, err := compactor.CompactTranscript(*sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return WriteJSON(stdout, compacted)
}

func HandlePrepareTranscript(args []string, preparer transcriptPreparer) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("prepare-transcript", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
	sessionRef := fs.String("session-ref", "", "session ref")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return preparer.PrepareTranscript(*sessionRef)
}

func HandleFormatResumeCommand(args []string, stdout io.Writer, formatter resumeFormatter) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("format-resume-command", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
	sessionID := fs.String("session-id", "", "session id")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return WriteJSON(stdout, ResumeCommandResponse{Command: formatter.FormatResumeCommand(*sessionID)})
}

func readStdinWithTimeout(r io.Reader, timeout time.Duration) ([]byte, error) {
	type result struct {
		data []byte
		err  error
	}
	ch := make(chan result, 1)
	go func() {
		data, err := io.ReadAll(r)
		ch <- result{data, err}
	}()
	select {
	case res := <-ch:
		return res.data, res.err
	case <-time.After(timeout):
		return nil, nil
		return err
	}
	summary, hasSummary, err := analyzer.ExtractSummary(*sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return WriteJSON(stdout, ExtractSummaryResponse{Summary: summary, HasSummary: hasSummary})
}

func HandleCalculateTokens(args []string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, calculator tokenCalculator) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("calculate-tokens", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(io.Discard)
	offset := fs.Int("offset", 0, "offset")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	usage, err := calculator.CalculateTokens(data, *offset)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return WriteJSON(stdout, usage)
}

func DefaultSessionDir(repoPath string) string {
	return filepath.Join(repoPath, ".entire", "tmp")
}

func ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID string) string {
	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, sessionID+".json")
}

package main

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"

	"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/amp"
	"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/protocol"
)

func main() {
	agent := amp.New()

	if len(os.Args) < 2 {
		fatalf("usage: entire-agent-amp <subcommand> [args]")
	}

	var err error

	switch os.Args[1] {
	case "info":
		err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, agent.Info())
	case "detect":
		err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, agent.Detect())
	case "get-session-id":
		err = protocol.HandleGetSessionID(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
	case "get-session-dir":
		err = protocol.HandleGetSessionDir(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "resolve-session-file":
		err = protocol.HandleResolveSessionFile(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "read-session":
		err = protocol.HandleReadSession(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
	case "write-session":
		err = protocol.HandleWriteSession(os.Stdin, agent)
	case "read-transcript":
		err = protocol.HandleReadTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "chunk-transcript":
		err = protocol.HandleChunkTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
	case "reassemble-transcript":
		err = protocol.HandleReassembleTranscript(os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
	case "compact-transcript":
		err = protocol.HandleCompactTranscript(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "prepare-transcript":
		err = protocol.HandlePrepareTranscript(os.Args[2:], agent)
	case "format-resume-command":
		err = protocol.HandleFormatResumeCommand(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "parse-hook":
		err = protocol.HandleParseHook(os.Args[2:], os.Stdin, os.Stdout, agent)
	case "install-hooks":
		err = protocol.HandleInstallHooks(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "uninstall-hooks":
		err = agent.UninstallHooks()
	case "are-hooks-installed":
		err = protocol.WriteJSON(os.Stdout, protocol.AreHooksInstalledResponse{
			Installed: agent.AreHooksInstalled(),
		})
	case "get-transcript-position":
		err = protocol.HandleGetTranscriptPosition(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
	case "extract-modified-files":
		err = protocol.HandleExtractModifiedFiles(os.Args[2:], os.Stdout, agent)
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/goose/agent.go",
  "content": "// Package goose implements the Entire external agent protocol for Goose,\n// Block's open-source AI coding agent. See AGENT.md for the protocol mapping\n// and verified payload formats.\npackage goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/protocol\"\n)\n\n// Hook names registered with the Entire CLI. These are the verbs Entire uses\n// when routing hook payloads back to parse-hook, and the verbs install-hooks\n// wires into the goose plugin's hooks.json commands.\nconst (\n\tHookNameSessionStart     = \"session-start\"\n\tHookNameUserPromptSubmit = \"user-prompt-submit\"\n\tHookNameStop             = \"stop\"\n\tHookNameSessionEnd       = \"session-end\"\n)\n\ntype Agent struct{}\n\nfunc New() *Agent {\n\treturn &Agent{}\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) Info() protocol.InfoResponse {\n\treturn protocol.InfoResponse{\n\t\tProtocolVersion: protocol.ProtocolVersion,\n\t\tName:            \"goose\",\n\t\tType:            \"Goose\",\n\t\tDescription:     \"Goose - Block's open-source AI coding agent\",\n\t\tIsPreview:       true,\n\t\tProtectedDirs:   []string{\".agents\"},\n\t\tHookNames: []string{\n\t\t\tHookNameSessionStart,\n\t\t\tHookNameUserPromptSubmit,\n\t\t\tHookNameStop,\n\t\t\tHookNameSessionEnd,\n\t\t},\n\t\tCapabilities: protocol.DeclaredCapabilities{\n\t\t\tHooks:              true,\n\t\t\tTranscriptAnalyzer: true,\n\t\t\tTranscriptPreparer: true,\n\t\t\tTokenCalculator:    true,\n\t\t},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) Detect() protocol.DetectResponse {\n\t_, err := exec.LookPath(\"goose\")\n\treturn protocol.DetectResponse{Present: err == nil}\n}\n\n// GetSessionID extracts the goose session ID from a hook input. Every goose\n// hook payload carries session_id (format YYYYMMDD_N), which Entire passes\n// through on the HookInput.\nfunc (a *Agent) GetSessionID(input *protocol.HookInputJSON) string {\n\treturn input.SessionID\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {\n\treturn \"goose session --resume --session-id \" + sessionID\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/goose/paths.go",
  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n)\n\n// sessionsDir returns goose's session storage directory. Goose stores all\n// sessions in a SQLite database (sessions.db) inside this directory.\n// GOOSE_PATH_ROOT overrides all goose paths (used by goose for hermetic\n// testing); otherwise the data dir follows XDG conventions.\nfunc sessionsDir() string {\n\tif root := os.Getenv(\"GOOSE_PATH_ROOT\"); root != \"\" {\n\t\treturn filepath.Join(root, \"data\", \"sessions\")\n\t}\n\tdataHome := os.Getenv(\"XDG_DATA_HOME\")\n\tif dataHome == \"\" {\n\t\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\"\n\t\t}\n\t\tdataHome = filepath.Join(home, \".local\", \"share\")\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(dataHome, \"goose\", \"sessions\")\n}\n\n// GetSessionDir returns the session directory. Goose keys sessions by\n// session ID globally (not per-repo), so the repo path does not change the\n// location.\nfunc (a *Agent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {\n\tdir := sessionsDir()\n\tif dir == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", os.ErrNotExist\n\t}\n\treturn dir, nil\n}\n\n// ResolveSessionFile returns the transcript file for a session. Goose has no\n// native per-session file; prepare-transcript materializes one at this path\n// via `goose session export`.\nfunc (a *Agent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, sessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir, sessionID+\".export.json\")\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"os\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/protocol\"\n)\n\n// ReadSession builds an AgentSession from a hook input. Implemented in\n// Phase 3 (reads session metadata from the goose export).\nfunc (a *Agent) ReadSession(input *protocol.HookInputJSON) (protocol.AgentSessionJSON, error) {\n\treturn protocol.AgentSessionJSON{\n\t\tSessionID:  input.SessionID,\n\t\tAgentName:  \"goose\",\n\t\tRepoPath:   protocol.RepoRoot(),\n\t\tSessionRef: input.SessionRef,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// WriteSession persists session data. Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) WriteSession(session protocol.AgentSessionJSON) error {\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {\n\tif maxSize <= 0 || len(content) == 0 {\n\t\treturn [][]byte{content}, nil\n\t}\n\tvar chunks [][]byte\n\tfor len(content) > maxSize {\n\t\tchunks = append(chunks, content[:maxSize])\n\t\tcontent = content[maxSize:]\n\t}\n\treturn append(chunks, content), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {\n\tvar out []byte\n\tfor _, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\tout = append(out, chunk...)\n\t}\n\treturn out, nil\n}\n\n// PrepareTranscript materializes the transcript by exporting the goose\n// session (`goose session export --format json`). Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\tinfo, err := os.Stat(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn int(info.Size()), nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFiles parses write/edit tool calls out of the exported\n// transcript. Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error) {\n\tposition, err := a.GetTranscriptPosition(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn []string{}, position, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractPrompts parses user prompts out of the exported transcript.\n// Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, offset int) ([]string, error) {\n\treturn []string{}, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary returns the goose session name as the summary.\n// Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, bool, error) {\n\treturn \"\", false, nil\n}\n\n// CalculateTokens reads accumulated token totals from the exported\n// transcript. Implemented in Phase 3.\nfunc (a *Agent) CalculateTokens(data []byte, offset int) (protocol.TokenUsageResponse, error) {\n\treturn protocol.TokenUsageResponse{}, nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/README.md",
  "content": "# entire-agent-goose\n\nExternal agent binary that teaches [Entire CLI](https://github.com/entireio/cli)\nhow to work with [Goose](https://github.com/block/goose), Block's open-source\nAI coding agent.\n\nSee [AGENT.md](AGENT.md) for the full research one-pager: hook mechanism,\nsession storage, transcript format, and protocol mapping (all verified against\ngoose 1.37.0).\n\n## How it works\n\n- **Hooks** — `install-hooks` writes a project-scope goose plugin at\n  `<repo>/.agents/plugins/entire/` whose `hooks/hooks.json` forwards goose's\n  native lifecycle events (`SessionStart`, `UserPromptSubmit`, `Stop`,\n  `SessionEnd`) to `entire hooks goose <name>`. Goose discovers project\n  plugins from its working directory automatically; no global config is\n  touched.\n- **Sessions** — goose stores sessions in a SQLite database\n  (`~/.local/share/goose/sessions/sessions.db`). There is no per-session\n  file, so `prepare-transcript` materializes one via\n  `goose session export --session-id <id> --format json`.\n- **Tokens** — accumulated input/output token totals come from the exported\n  session JSON.\n\n## Build\n\n```bash\nmise run build       # go build -o entire-agent-goose ./cmd/entire-agent-goose\nmise run test        # go test ./...\n```\n\n## Install\n\nPut `entire-agent-goose` on your `PATH`, then opt in to external agents in\nthe repo's `.entire/settings.json`:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"external_agents\": true\n}\n```\n\n## Requirements\n\n- `goose` CLI on `PATH` (verified against 1.37.0) with a configured provider\n- Entire CLI\n\n## Research verification\n\n`scripts/verify-goose.sh` wires a capture plugin into a throwaway probe\nworkspace and runs a headless `goose run -t` to capture real hook payloads.\nIt never modifies global goose config.\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/e2e/agents/goose.go",
  "content": "package agents\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\nfunc init() {\n\tif env := os.Getenv(\"E2E_AGENT\"); env != \"\" && env != \"goose\" {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tRegister(&Goose{})\n\tRegisterGate(\"goose\", 2)\n}\n\ntype Goose struct{}\n\nfunc (g *Goose) Name() string          { return \"goose\" }\nfunc (g *Goose) Binary() string        { return \"goose\" }\nfunc (g *Goose) EntireAgent() string   { return \"goose\" }\nfunc (g *Goose) PromptPattern() string { return `\\( O\\)>` }\n\n// TimeoutMultiplier is generous: goose runs against remote providers and\n// streams full tool loops.\nfunc (g *Goose) TimeoutMultiplier() float64 { return 2.0 }\nfunc (g *Goose) IsExternalAgent() bool      { return true }\n\nfunc (g *Goose) IsTransientError(out Output, _ error) bool {\n\tcombined := strings.ToLower(out.Stdout + out.Stderr)\n\tpatterns := []string{\n\t\t\"overloaded\",\n\t\t\"rate limit\",\n\t\t\"429\",\n\t\t\"500\",\n\t\t\"503\",\n\t\t\"529\",\n\t\t\"econnreset\",\n\t\t\"etimedout\",\n\t\t\"timeout\",\n\t}\n\tfor _, pattern := range patterns {\n\t\tif strings.Contains(combined, pattern) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc (g *Goose) Bootstrap() error {\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (g *Goose) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, dir string, prompt string, opts ...Option) (Output, error) {\n\tcfg := &runConfig{}\n\tfor _, o := range opts {\n\t\to(cfg)\n\t}\n\n\tbin, err := exec.LookPath(g.Binary())\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn Output{}, fmt.Errorf(\"%s not in PATH: %w\", g.Binary(), err)\n\t}\n\n\targs := []string{\"run\", \"-t\", prompt}\n\tdisplayArgs := []string{\"run\", \"-t\", fmt.Sprintf(\"%q\", prompt)}\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, bin, args...)\n\tcmd.Dir = dir\n\tcmd.Env = filterEnv(os.Environ(), \"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY\")\n\tcmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setpgid: true}\n\tcmd.Cancel = func() error {\n\t\treturn syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)\n\t}\n\tcmd.WaitDelay = 5 * time.Second\n\n\tvar stdout, stderr strings.Builder\n\tcmd.Stdout = &stdout\n\tcmd.Stderr = &stderr\n\n\terr = cmd.Run()\n\texitCode := 0\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\texitErr := &exec.ExitError{}\n\t\tif errors.As(err, &exitErr) {\n\t\t\texitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\texitCode = -1\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn Output{\n\t\tCommand:  g.Binary() + \" \" + strings.Join(displayArgs, \" \"),\n\t\tStdout:   stdout.String(),\n\t\tStderr:   stderr.String(),\n\t\tExitCode: exitCode,\n\t}, err\n}\n\nfunc (g *Goose) StartSession(ctx context.Context, dir string) (Session, error) {\n\tname := fmt.Sprintf(\"goose-test-%d\", time.Now().UnixNano())\n\ts, err := NewTmuxSession(name, dir, []string{\"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY\"}, g.Binary(), \"session\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif _, err := s.WaitFor(g.PromptPattern(), 30*time.Second); err != nil {\n\t\t_ = s.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"waiting for initial prompt: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn s, nil\n}\n"
}
```

4	  Phase 3: Implement the external agent binary using protocol compliance first,
5	  lifecycle integration second, and unit tests last.
6	---
7	
8	# Implement Procedure
9	
10	Implement the agent with black-box-first TDD.
11	
12	The order is:
13	
14	1. protocol compliance against `external-agents-tests`
15	2. lifecycle integration in this repo's `e2e/` harness
16	3. unit tests in the agent module
17	
18	## Prerequisites
19	
20	Ensure the following are available:
21	
22	- `AGENT_NAME`
23	- `AGENT_SLUG`
24	- `PROJECT_DIR`
25	- `<PROJECT_DIR>/AGENT.md`
26	- compiling scaffold from the write-tests phase
27	
28	## Step 1: Read Before Coding
29	
30	Read:
31	
32	1. the protocol spec
33	2. the current agent code
34	3. `<PROJECT_DIR>/AGENT.md`
35	4. the lifecycle adapter in `e2e/agents/<slug>.go` if it already exists
36	
37	## Step 2: Establish the First Failing Compliance Run
38	
39	Build the binary and run the shared compliance suite first using the `external-agents-tests` CLI (installed via `mise install`):
40	
41	```bash
42	cd <PROJECT_DIR>
43	mise run build
44	external-agents-tests verify ./entire-agent-<slug>
45	```
46	
47	Do not start by adding new protocol tests to this repo.
48	
49	## Step 3: Fix Compliance Failures Incrementally
50	
51	For each failing compliance assertion:
52	
53	1. rerun `external-agents-tests verify ./entire-agent-<slug>`
54	2. inspect the exact subcommand behavior
55	3. implement the minimum fix
56	4. rerun until it passes
57	
58	Areas the compliance suite typically drives:
59	
60	- `info` and capability declarations
61	- `detect`
62	- session helpers
63	- transcript chunking and reassembly
64	- session read/write behavior
65	- hooks capability
66	- transcript analysis capability
67	
68	## Step 4: Run Lifecycle Tests
69	
70	Once protocol compliance is in good shape, validate the real integration path:
71	
72	```bash
73	cd /path/to/repo
74	E2E_AGENT=<slug> mise run test-e2e
75	```
76	
77	These tests require:
78	
79	- the Entire CLI
80	- the real agent CLI on `PATH`
81	- `tmux` for interactive scenarios
82	
83	If those dependencies are not available, note the gap explicitly and continue with the protocol and unit-test work.
84	
85	## Step 5: Fix Lifecycle Failures Incrementally
86	
87	Use lifecycle failures to refine:
88	
89	- the agent CLI adapter in `e2e/agents/<slug>.go`
90	- prompt execution details
91	- hook installation behavior after `entire enable`
92	- rewind and checkpoint interactions
93	- interactive session handling
94	
95	Keep protocol fixes in the agent binary itself. Keep real-CLI orchestration fixes in the lifecycle adapter.
96	
97	## Step 6: Add Unit Tests Last
98	
99	After the behavior is working end to end, add unit tests in the agent module for:
100	
101	- hook parsing
102	- transcript parsing
103	- config file read-modify-write behavior
104	- session file handling
105	- protocol handlers
106	
107	Prefer using real payloads or fixtures captured during compliance and lifecycle runs.
108	
109	## Step 7: Final Validation
110	
111	Run:
112	
113	```bash
114	cd <PROJECT_DIR>
115	mise run build
116	mise run test
117	external-agents-tests verify ./entire-agent-<slug>
118	
119	cd /path/to/repo
120	E2E_AGENT=<slug> mise run test-e2e
121	```
122	
123	## Output Checklist
124	
125	Summarize:
126	
127	- compliance status
128	- lifecycle status
129	- unit-test status
130	- any dependencies you could not satisfy locally
131	- remaining gaps or TODOs
132

func (a *Agent) ReadSession(input *protocol.HookInputJSON) (protocol.AgentSessionJSON, error) {
	var sessionID string
	var sessionRef string
	if input != nil {
		sessionID = input.SessionID
		sessionRef = input.SessionRef
		if sessionRef == "" && sessionID != "" {
			sessionRef = transcriptPath(sessionID)
		}
	}
	if sessionRef == "" {
		return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{}, errors.New("session_ref or session_id is required")
	}

	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{}, err
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{}, err
	}
	if thread.ID == "" {
		if sessionID == "" {
			sessionID = strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(sessionRef), filepath.Ext(sessionRef))
		}
		return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{
			SessionID:     sessionID,
			AgentName:     "amp",
			RepoPath:      protocol.RepoRoot(),
			SessionRef:    sessionRef,
			StartTime:     time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),
			NativeData:    data,
			ModifiedFiles: []string{},
			NewFiles:      []string{},
			DeletedFiles:  []string{},
		}, nil
	}

	startTime := thread.Created.Time()
	if startTime.IsZero() && !thread.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {
		startTime = thread.UpdatedAt.UTC()
	}
	if startTime.IsZero() {
		startTime = time.Now().UTC()
	}

	return protocol.AgentSessionJSON{
		SessionID:     thread.ID,
		AgentName:     "amp",
		RepoPath:      protocol.RepoRoot(),
		SessionRef:    sessionRef,
		StartTime:     startTime.Format(time.RFC3339),
		NativeData:    data,
		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFilesFromThread(thread),
		NewFiles:      []string{},
		DeletedFiles:  []string{},
	}, nil
}

func (a *Agent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
	if strings.TrimSpace(sessionRef) == "" {
		return errors.New("session_ref is required")
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("read transcript for prepare: %w", err)
	}
	threadID, err := threadIDFromTranscriptData(data)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if threadID == "" {
		return nil
	}
	runner := a.CommandRunner
	if runner == nil {
		runner = &DefaultCommandRunner{}
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), prepareTranscriptTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	_, err = runner.ExportThread(ctx, threadID, sessionRef)
	return err
}

func (a *Agent) WriteSession(session protocol.AgentSessionJSON) error {
	if session.SessionRef == "" {
		return errors.New("session_ref is required")
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(session.SessionRef), 0o700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600)
}

func (a *Agent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if _, err := parseAmpThread(data); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return data, nil
}

```
package amp

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/protocol"
)

const (
	pluginDir        = ".amp/plugins"
	pluginFile       = ".amp/plugins/entire.ts"
	transcriptSubdir = "amp"
)

type ampHookPayload struct {
	Type          string            `json:"type"`
	Cwd           string            `json:"cwd,omitempty"`
	ThreadID      string            `json:"thread_id,omitempty"`
	MessageID     string            `json:"message_id,omitempty"`
	Message       string            `json:"message,omitempty"`
	Status        string            `json:"status,omitempty"`
	Messages      []json.RawMessage `json:"messages,omitempty"`
	ModifiedFiles []string          `json:"modified_files,omitempty"`
}

func (a *Agent) ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*protocol.EventJSON, error) {
	if len(input) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}

	var payload ampHookPayload
	if err := json.Unmarshal(input, &payload); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse hook payload: %w", err)
	}

	sessionID := payload.ThreadID
	if sessionID == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}

	now := time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)
	sessionRef := transcriptPath(sessionID)

	switch hookName {
	case "session.start":
		_ = a.exportThread(sessionID, sessionRef)
		return &protocol.EventJSON{
			Type:       1,
			SessionID:  sessionID,
			SessionRef: sessionRef,
			Model:      latestModelFromSessionRef(sessionRef),
			Timestamp:  now,
		}, nil

	case "agent.start":
		model := latestModelFromSessionRef(sessionRef)
		if model == "" {
			// Fallback: ensure we have a prepared transcript so we can populate the model.
			if exportErr := a.exportThread(sessionID, sessionRef); exportErr == nil {
				model = latestModelFromSessionRef(sessionRef)
			}
		}
		return &protocol.EventJSON{
			Type:       2,
			SessionID:  sessionID,
			SessionRef: sessionRef,
			Prompt:     payload.Message,
			Model:      model,
			Timestamp:  now,
		}, nil

	case "agent.end":
		if err := a.exportThread(sessionID, sessionRef); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("export thread on agent.end: %w", err)
		}
		return &protocol.EventJSON{
			Type:       3,
			SessionID:  sessionID,
			SessionRef: sessionRef,
			Model:      latestModelFromSessionRef(sessionRef),
			Timestamp:  now,
		}, nil

	default:
		return nil, nil
	}
}

// exportThread runs `amp threads export` for the given thread and writes the
// result to sessionRef. The parent directory is created if needed. Callers
// generally ignore the returned error and fall back to reporting an empty
// model so events are still emitted.
func (a *Agent) exportThread(threadID, sessionRef string) error {
	if strings.TrimSpace(threadID) == "" || strings.TrimSpace(sessionRef) == "" {
		return errors.New("thread id and session ref are required")
	}
	runner := a.CommandRunner
	if runner == nil {
		runner = &DefaultCommandRunner{}
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionRef), 0o750); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), prepareTranscriptTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	_, err := runner.ExportThread(ctx, threadID, sessionRef)
	return err
}

// latestModelFromSessionRef returns the most recent model string recorded in
// the prepared transcript at sessionRef. It returns an empty string if the
// file is missing or not yet a prepared Thread JSON.
func latestModelFromSessionRef(sessionRef string) string {
	if strings.TrimSpace(sessionRef) == "" {
		return ""
	}
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil || len(bytes.TrimSpace(data)) == 0 {
		return ""
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return ""
	}
	return latestThreadModel(thread)
}

func (a *Agent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
	if maxSize <= 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("max-size must be positive, got %d", maxSize)
	}
	var chunks [][]byte
	for len(content) > 0 {
		end := min(maxSize, len(content))
		chunks = append(chunks, content[:end])
		content = content[end:]
	}
	return chunks, nil
}

func (a *Agent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
	var data []byte
	for _, chunk := range chunks {
		data = append(data, chunk...)
	}
	return data, nil
}

func (a *Agent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return 0, nil
		}
		return 0, err
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	return len(thread.Messages), nil
}

func (a *Agent) ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, 0, err
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, 0, err
	}
	return modifiedFilesFromThreadFromOffset(thread, offset), len(thread.Messages), nil
}

func (a *Agent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, offset int) ([]string, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var prompts []string
	for _, msg := range threadMessagesFromOffset(thread, offset) {
		if msg.Role != ThreadMessageRoleUser {
			continue
		}
		if text := threadMessageText(msg); text != "" {
			prompts = append(prompts, text)
		}
	}
	return prompts, nil
}

func (a *Agent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, bool, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	for i := len(thread.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		msg := thread.Messages[i]
		if msg.Role != ThreadMessageRoleAssistant {
			continue
		}
		if text := threadMessageText(msg); text != "" {
			return text, true, nil
		}
	}
	return "", false, nil
}

func (a *Agent) CalculateTokens(data []byte, offset int) (protocol.TokenUsageResponse, error) {
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return protocol.TokenUsageResponse{}, err
	}
	var usage protocol.TokenUsageResponse
	for _, msg := range threadMessagesFromOffset(thread, offset) {
		if msg.Usage == nil {
			continue
		}
		usage.InputTokens += msg.Usage.InputTokens
		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Usage.OutputTokens
		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Usage.CacheReadInputTokens
		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Usage.CacheCreationInputTokens
		usage.APICallCount++
	}
	return usage, nil
}

func threadMessagesFromOffset(thread *Thread, offset int) []ThreadMessage {
	if thread == nil || len(thread.Messages) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	if offset < 0 {
		offset = 0
	}
	if offset >= len(thread.Messages) {
		return nil
	}
	return thread.Messages[offset:]
}

func latestThreadModel(thread *Thread) string {
	if thread == nil {
		return ""

package amp

import "github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/protocol"

func (a *Agent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {

package amp

import (
	"os/exec"

	"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/protocol"
)

type Agent struct {
	CommandRunner CommandRunner
}

func New() *Agent {
	return &Agent{CommandRunner: &DefaultCommandRunner{}}
}

func (a *Agent) Info() protocol.InfoResponse {
	return protocol.InfoResponse{
		ProtocolVersion: protocol.ProtocolVersion,
		Name:            "amp",
		Type:            "Amp",
		Description:     "Amp coding agent integration for Entire",
		IsPreview:       true,
		ProtectedDirs:   []string{".amp"},
		ProtectedFiles:  []string{".amp/plugins/entire.ts"},
		HookNames:       []string{"session.start", "agent.start", "agent.end"},
		Capabilities: protocol.DeclaredCapabilities{
			Hooks:              true,
			TranscriptAnalyzer: true,
			TranscriptPreparer: true,
			TokenCalculator:    true,
			CompactTranscript:  true,
			UsesTerminal:       true,
		},
	}
}

func (a *Agent) Detect() protocol.DetectResponse {
	_, err := exec.LookPath("amp")
	return protocol.DetectResponse{Present: err == nil}
}

func (a *Agent) GetSessionID(input *protocol.HookInputJSON) string {
	if input != nil && input.SessionID != "" {
		return input.SessionID
	}
	return ""
}

func (a *Agent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
	if sessionID == "" {
		return "PLUGINS=all amp threads continue --last"
	}
	return "PLUGINS=all amp threads continue " + sessionID
}

  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\nconst exportTimeout = 30 * time.Second\n\n// CommandRunner abstracts the goose CLI invocations so tests can stub them.\ntype CommandRunner interface {\n\t// ExportSession runs `goose session export --session-id <id> --format json`\n\t// and writes the result to outPath.\n\tExportSession(ctx context.Context, sessionID, outPath string) error\n}\n\ntype DefaultCommandRunner struct{}\n\nfunc (r *DefaultCommandRunner) ExportSession(ctx context.Context, sessionID, outPath string) error {\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"goose\", \"session\", \"export\",\n\t\t\"--session-id\", sessionID, \"--format\", \"json\", \"-o\", outPath)\n\t// Goose resolves its data dir from the environment; pass it through so\n\t// GOOSE_PATH_ROOT-based test sandboxes work.\n\tcmd.Env = os.Environ()\n\tout, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"goose session export %s: %w: %s\", sessionID, err, string(out))\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/protocol\"\n)\n\n// Goose stores sessions in a SQLite database, not per-session files, so the\n// session dir holds transcripts materialized by prepare-transcript /\n// parse-hook via `goose session export`. They live under the repo's\n// .entire/tmp scratch space like other external agents' prepared transcripts.\n\nfunc sessionDir(repoPath string) string {\n\tif repoPath == \"\" {\n\t\trepoPath = protocol.RepoRoot()\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(repoPath, \".entire\", \"tmp\", \"goose\")\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {\n\treturn sessionDir(repoPath), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDirPath, sessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDirPath, sessionID+\".json\")\n}\n\n// transcriptPath is the materialized export location for a session.\nfunc transcriptPath(sessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir(\"\"), sessionID+\".json\")\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/goose/hooks.go",
  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/protocol\"\n)\n\n// Plugin layout: goose discovers project-scope plugins from\n// <project-root>/.agents/plugins/ (enabled by default once present) and runs\n// hooks declared in <plugin>/hooks/hooks.json. See AGENT.md.\nconst (\n\tpluginRelDir   = \".agents/plugins/entire\"\n\thooksRelFile   = \".agents/plugins/entire/hooks/hooks.json\"\n\thookCommand    = \"entire hooks goose \"\n\thookTimeoutSec = 60\n)\n\n// gooseHookPayload is the HookContext JSON goose writes to hook stdin\n// (snake_case, verified against goose 1.37.0 captures).\ntype gooseHookPayload struct {\n\tEvent          string          `json:\"event\"`\n\tSessionID      string          `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tMatcherContext string          `json:\"matcher_context\"`\n\tToolName       string          `json:\"tool_name,omitempty\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input,omitempty\"`\n\tMessage        string          `json:\"message,omitempty\"`\n\tWorkingDir     string          `json:\"working_dir,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// nativeHookEvents maps Entire hook verbs to goose hooks.json event names.\n// Order is stable for deterministic hooks.json output.\nvar nativeHookEvents = []struct {\n\tVerb  string\n\tEvent string\n}{\n\t{HookNameSessionStart, \"SessionStart\"},\n\t{HookNameUserPromptSubmit, \"UserPromptSubmit\"},\n\t{HookNameStop, \"Stop\"},\n\t{HookNameSessionEnd, \"SessionEnd\"},\n}\n\n// ParseHook converts a raw goose hook payload into a normalized Entire event.\n// Goose payloads carry no timestamp or transcript reference, so events are\n// stamped at receipt and the transcript path is derived from the session ID.\nfunc (a *Agent) ParseHook(hookName string, input []byte) (*protocol.EventJSON, error) {\n\tif len(bytes.TrimSpace(input)) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar payload gooseHookPayload\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(input, &payload); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse hook payload: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif payload.SessionID == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tsessionID := payload.SessionID\n\tsessionRef := transcriptPath(sessionID)\n\tnow := time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)\n\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase HookNameSessionStart:\n\t\t// Best effort: the session row exists by SessionStart, but a failed\n\t\t// export must not block the session from starting.\n\t\t_ = a.exportSession(sessionID, sessionRef)\n\t\treturn &protocol.EventJSON{\n\t\t\tType:       1,\n\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n\t\t\tSessionRef: sessionRef,\n\t\t\tModel:      modelFromSessionRef(sessionRef),\n\t\t\tTimestamp:  now,\n\t\t}, nil\n\n\tcase HookNameUserPromptSubmit:\n\t\treturn &protocol.EventJSON{\n\t\t\tType:       2,\n\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n\t\t\tSessionRef: sessionRef,\n\t\t\tPrompt:     payload.Message,\n\t\t\tModel:      modelFromSessionRef(sessionRef),\n\t\t\tTimestamp:  now,\n\t\t}, nil\n\n\tcase HookNameStop:\n\t\t// The turn is complete; the transcript must be fresh for checkpointing.\n\t\tif err := a.exportSession(sessionID, sessionRef); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"export session on stop: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn &protocol.EventJSON{\n\t\t\tType:       3,\n\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n\t\t\tSessionRef: sessionRef,\n\t\t\tModel:      modelFromSessionRef(sessionRef),\n\t\t\tTimestamp:  now,\n\t\t}, nil\n\n\tcase HookNameSessionEnd:\n\t\t_ = a.exportSession(sessionID, sessionRef)\n\t\treturn &protocol.EventJSON{\n\t\t\tType:       5,\n\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n\t\t\tSessionRef: sessionRef,\n\t\t\tTimestamp:  now,\n\t\t}, nil\n\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) exportSession(sessionID, sessionRef string) error {\n\tif sessionID == \"\" || sessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session id and session ref are required\")\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionRef), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\trunner := a.CommandRunner\n\tif runner == nil {\n\t\trunner = &DefaultCommandRunner{}\n\t}\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), exportTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\treturn runner.ExportSession(ctx, sessionID, sessionRef)\n}\n\n// hooksFileContent renders the goose plugin hooks.json forwarding native\n// lifecycle events to the Entire CLI. Hook commands read the HookContext\n// JSON from stdin and must exit 0; goose treats Stop as a blocking hook.\nfunc hooksFileContent() ([]byte, error) {\n\ttype hookAction struct {\n\t\tType    string `json:\"type\"`\n\t\tCommand string `json:\"command\"`\n\t\tTimeout int    `json:\"timeout\"`\n\t}\n\ttype hookRule struct {\n\t\tHooks []hookAction `json:\"hooks\"`\n\t}\n\trules := map[string][]hookRule{}\n\tfor _, h := range nativeHookEvents {\n\t\trules[h.Event] = []hookRule{{\n\t\t\tHooks: []hookAction{{\n\t\t\t\tType:    \"command\",\n\t\t\t\tCommand: hookCommand + h.Verb,\n\t\t\t\tTimeout: hookTimeoutSec,\n\t\t\t}},\n\t\t}}\n\t}\n\tvar buf bytes.Buffer\n\tenc := json.NewEncoder(&buf)\n\tenc.SetEscapeHTML(false)\n\tenc.SetIndent(\"\", \"  \")\n\tif err := enc.Encode(map[string]any{\"hooks\": rules}); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn buf.Bytes(), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) InstallHooks(_ bool, force bool) (int, error) {\n\tif !force && a.AreHooksInstalled() {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\trepoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, hooksRelFile)\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tcontent, err := hooksFileContent()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, content, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\treturn len(nativeHookEvents), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) UninstallHooks() error {\n\trepoRoot := protocol.RepoRoot()\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, hooksRelFile)\n\tif err := os.Remove(hooksPath); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\t// Remove the now-empty plugin directories; leave them if the user added\n\t// other files to the plugin.\n\t_ = os.Remove(filepath.Dir(hooksPath))\n\t_ = os.Remove(filepath.Join(repoRoot, pluginRelDir))\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(protocol.RepoRoot(), hooksRelFile))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tvar file struct {\n\t\tHooks map[string][]struct {\n\t\t\tHooks []struct {\n\t\t\t\tCommand string `json:\"command\"`\n\t\t\t} `json:\"hooks\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"hooks\"`\n\t}\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &file); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tfor _, h := range nativeHookEvents {\n\t\tfound := false\n\t\tfor _, rule := range file.Hooks[h.Event] {\n\t\t\tfor _, action := range rule.Hooks {\n\t\t\t\tif action.Command == hookCommand+h.Verb {\n\t\t\t\t\tfound = true\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !found {\n\t\t\treturn false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn true\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/internal/protocol\"\n)\n\n// gooseExport models the output of `goose session export --format json`\n// (verified against goose 1.37.0). Message content is camelCase, unlike the\n// snake_case hook payloads.\ntype gooseExport struct {\n\tID                     string         `json:\"id\"`\n\tWorkingDir             string         `json:\"working_dir\"`\n\tName                   string         `json:\"name\"`\n\tCreatedAt              string         `json:\"created_at\"`\n\tAccumulatedInputTokens int            `json:\"accumulated_input_tokens\"`\n\tAccumulatedOutput      int            `json:\"accumulated_output_tokens\"`\n\tConversation           []gooseMessage `json:\"conversation\"`\n\tProviderName           string         `json:\"provider_name\"`\n\tModelConfig            struct {\n\t\tModelName string `json:\"model_name\"`\n\t} `json:\"model_config\"`\n}\n\ntype gooseMessage struct {\n\tID      string         `json:\"id\"`\n\tRole    string         `json:\"role\"`\n\tCreated int64          `json:\"created\"`\n\tContent []gooseContent `json:\"content\"`\n}\n\ntype gooseContent struct {\n\tType     string `json:\"type\"`\n\tText     string `json:\"text,omitempty\"`\n\tToolCall *struct {\n\t\tStatus string `json:\"status\"`\n\t\tValue  struct {\n\t\t\tName      string          `json:\"name\"`\n\t\t\tArguments json.RawMessage `json:\"arguments\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"value\"`\n\t} `json:\"toolCall,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// parseGooseExport is tolerant: any valid JSON object parses, and callers\n// check ID to decide whether it is a real goose export.\nfunc parseGooseExport(data []byte) (*gooseExport, error) {\n\tvar export gooseExport\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &export); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse goose export: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &export, nil\n}\n\n// gooseSessionIDPattern matches goose's YYYYMMDD_N session IDs.\nvar gooseSessionIDPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^\\d{8}_\\d+$`)\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ReadSession(input *protocol.HookInputJSON) (protocol.AgentSessionJSON, error) {\n\tvar sessionID, sessionRef string\n\tif input != nil {\n\t\tsessionID = input.SessionID\n\t\tsessionRef = input.SessionRef\n\t\tif sessionRef == \"\" && sessionID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tsessionRef = transcriptPath(sessionID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif sessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn protocol.AgentSessionJSON{}, errors.New(\"session_ref or session_id is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn protocol.AgentSessionJSON{}, err\n\t}\n\n\texport, parseErr := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif parseErr != nil || export.ID == \"\" {\n\t\t// Not a goose export (e.g. round-tripped opaque native data); return\n\t\t// the session as stored without goose-specific enrichment.\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tsessionID = strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(sessionRef), filepath.Ext(sessionRef))\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn protocol.AgentSessionJSON{\n\t\t\tSessionID:     sessionID,\n\t\t\tAgentName:     \"goose\",\n\t\t\tRepoPath:      protocol.RepoRoot(),\n\t\t\tSessionRef:    sessionRef,\n\t\t\tStartTime:     time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339),\n\t\t\tNativeData:    data,\n\t\t\tModifiedFiles: []string{},\n\t\t\tNewFiles:      []string{},\n\t\t\tDeletedFiles:  []string{},\n\t\t}, nil\n\t}\n\n\tstartTime := export.CreatedAt\n\tif _, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, startTime); err != nil {\n\t\tstartTime = time.Now().UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)\n\t}\n\n\treturn protocol.AgentSessionJSON{\n\t\tSessionID:     export.ID,\n\t\tAgentName:     \"goose\",\n\t\tRepoPath:      protocol.RepoRoot(),\n\t\tSessionRef:    sessionRef,\n\t\tStartTime:     startTime,\n\t\tNativeData:    data,\n\t\tModifiedFiles: modifiedFilesFromExport(export, 0),\n\t\tNewFiles:      []string{},\n\t\tDeletedFiles:  []string{},\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) WriteSession(session protocol.AgentSessionJSON) error {\n\tif session.SessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session_ref is required\")\n\t}\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(session.SessionRef), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600)\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {\n\tif maxSize <= 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"max-size must be positive, got %d\", maxSize)\n\t}\n\tvar chunks [][]byte\n\tfor len(content) > 0 {\n\t\tend := min(maxSize, len(content))\n\t\tchunks = append(chunks, content[:end])\n\t\tcontent = content[end:]\n\t}\n\treturn chunks, nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {\n\tvar out []byte\n\tfor _, chunk := range chunks {\n\t\tout = append(out, chunk...)\n\t}\n\treturn out, nil\n}\n\n// PrepareTranscript materializes the transcript by exporting the goose\n// session named by the file (<session-id>.json). Files that don't follow\n// goose's session ID naming are left untouched so opaque round-tripped data\n// survives.\nfunc (a *Agent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {\n\tif strings.TrimSpace(sessionRef) == \"\" {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session_ref is required\")\n\t}\n\tsessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(sessionRef), filepath.Ext(sessionRef))\n\tif !gooseSessionIDPattern.MatchString(sessionID) {\n\t\tif _, err := os.Stat(sessionRef); err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"cannot prepare transcript: %q is not a goose session id\", sessionID)\n\t}\n\treturn a.exportSession(sessionID, sessionRef)\n}\n\n// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of conversation messages in the\n// exported transcript. Positions are message indexes, not bytes: every\n// export rewrites the whole JSON document, so byte offsets would not be\n// stable across turns.\nfunc (a *Agent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\treturn len(export.Conversation), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractModifiedFiles(path string, offset int) ([]string, int, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, err\n\t}\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn []string{}, 0, nil\n\t}\n\treturn modifiedFilesFromExport(export, offset), len(export.Conversation), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, offset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn []string{}, nil\n\t}\n\tprompts := []string{}\n\tfor _, msg := range messagesFromOffset(export, offset) {\n\t\tif msg.Role != \"user\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, content := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif content.Type == \"text\" && content.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, content.Text)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary returns goose's session name, which goose auto-generates\n// from the conversation.\nfunc (a *Agent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, bool, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", false, err\n\t}\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil || export.ID == \"\" || export.Name == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", false, nil\n\t}\n\treturn export.Name, true, nil\n}\n\n// CalculateTokens reports goose's session-level accumulated token totals.\n// Goose does not persist reliable per-message token counts, so the offset\n// cannot slice usage by turn; totals always cover the whole session.\nfunc (a *Agent) CalculateTokens(data []byte, _ int) (protocol.TokenUsageResponse, error) {\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn protocol.TokenUsageResponse{}, err\n\t}\n\tusage := protocol.TokenUsageResponse{\n\t\tInputTokens:  export.AccumulatedInputTokens,\n\t\tOutputTokens: export.AccumulatedOutput,\n\t}\n\tfor _, msg := range export.Conversation {\n\t\tif msg.Role == \"assistant\" {\n\t\t\tusage.APICallCount++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn usage, nil\n}\n\nfunc messagesFromOffset(export *gooseExport, offset int) []gooseMessage {\n\tif export == nil || offset >= len(export.Conversation) {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\treturn export.Conversation[offset:]\n}\n\n// fileWritingTools maps goose developer-extension tool names to the argument\n// key holding the file path.\nvar fileWritingTools = map[string]string{\n\t\"write\":       \"path\",\n\t\"text_editor\": \"path\",\n\t\"edit\":        \"path\",\n}\n\nfunc modifiedFilesFromExport(export *gooseExport, offset int) []string {\n\tfiles := []string{}\n\tseen := map[string]bool{}\n\tfor _, msg := range messagesFromOffset(export, offset) {\n\t\tfor _, content := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif content.Type != \"toolRequest\" || content.ToolCall == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tname := content.ToolCall.Value.Name\n\t\t\t// Extension-qualified names look like developer__text_editor.\n\t\t\tif idx := strings.LastIndex(name, \"__\"); idx >= 0 {\n\t\t\t\tname = name[idx+2:]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tpathKey, ok := fileWritingTools[name]\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar args map[string]json.RawMessage\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(content.ToolCall.Value.Arguments, &args); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar path string\n\t\t\tif raw, ok := args[pathKey]; ok {\n\t\t\t\t_ = json.Unmarshal(raw, &path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif path != \"\" && !seen[path] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[path] = true\n\t\t\t\tfiles = append(files, path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn files\n}\n\n// modelFromSessionRef returns the model recorded in the materialized export,\n// or \"\" when the export is missing or not yet written.\nfunc modelFromSessionRef(sessionRef string) string {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\texport, err := parseGooseExport(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn export.ModelConfig.ModelName\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "// RewindList runs `entire rewind --list` and parses the JSON output.\nfunc RewindList(t *testing.T, dir string) []RewindPoint {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tout := run(t, dir, \"rewind\", \"--list\")\n\n\t// Newer entire versions print a deprecation notice for `rewind` ahead of\n\t// the JSON; parse from the start of the array.\n\tjsonOut := out\n\tif idx := strings.Index(out, \"[\"); idx > 0 {\n\t\tjsonOut = out[idx:]\n\t}\n\n\tvar points []RewindPoint\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(jsonOut), &points); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"parse rewind list: %v\\nraw output: %s\", err, out)\n\t}\n\treturn points\n}"
}

20	// TestLifecycle_SinglePromptManualCommit verifies the basic flow:
21	// agent creates a file → git add + commit → checkpoint exists with trailer.
22	func TestLifecycle_SinglePromptManualCommit(t *testing.T) {
23		testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 2*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
24			_, err := s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called hello.txt with the content 'hello world'. Do not ask for confirmation.")
25			require.NoError(t, err, "prompt failed")
26	
27			testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "hello.txt")
28	
29			s.Git(t, "add", ".")
30			s.Git(t, "commit", "-m", "add hello.txt")
31	
32			testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 30*time.Second)
33	
34			cpID := testutil.AssertHasCheckpointTrailer(t, s.Dir, "HEAD")
35			testutil.AssertCheckpointExists(t, s.Dir, cpID)
36			testutil.ValidateCheckpointDeep(t, s.Dir, testutil.DeepCheckpointValidation{
37				CheckpointID:              cpID,
38				ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{"hello"},
39			})
40		})
41	}
42	
43	// TestLifecycle_MultiplePromptsManualCommit sends two prompts, commits once,
44	// and verifies the checkpoint covers both files.
45	func TestLifecycle_MultiplePromptsManualCommit(t *testing.T) {
46		testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 3*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
47			_, err := s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called foo.txt containing 'foo'. Do not ask for confirmation.")
48			require.NoError(t, err, "first prompt failed")
49	
50			_, err = s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called bar.txt containing 'bar'. Do not ask for confirmation.")
51			require.NoError(t, err, "second prompt failed")
52	
53			testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "foo.txt")
54			testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "bar.txt")
55	
56			s.Git(t, "add", ".")
57			s.Git(t, "commit", "-m", "add foo and bar")

59			testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 30*time.Second)

89	// TestLifecycle_RewindPreCommit verifies pre-commit shadow branch rewind:
90	// agent creates file A → snapshot rewind points → agent creates file B → rewind → file A exists, file B gone.
91	// No commits happen — this tests pure shadow branch rewind points.
92	func TestLifecycle_RewindPreCommit(t *testing.T) {
93		testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 3*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
94			// Agent creates file A (no commit).
95			_, err := s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called alpha.txt with content 'alpha'. Do not commit the file. Do not ask for confirmation.")
96			require.NoError(t, err, "first prompt failed")
97			testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "alpha.txt")
98	
99			// Snapshot rewind points (shadow branch, is_logs_only=false).
100			points := entire.RewindList(t, s.Dir)
101			require.NotEmpty(t, points, "expected at least one rewind point after first prompt")
102			rewindTarget := points[0].ID
103	
104			// Agent creates file B (no commit).
105			_, err = s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called beta.txt with content 'beta'. Do not commit the file. Do not ask for confirmation.")
106			require.NoError(t, err, "second prompt failed")
107			testutil.AssertFileExists(t, s.Dir, "beta.txt")
108	
109			// Rewind to point after file A was created.
110			err = entire.Rewind(t, s.Dir, rewindTarget)

112	
113			// File A should still exist, file B should be gone.
114			_, err = os.Stat(filepath.Join(s.Dir, "alpha.txt"))
115			assert.NoError(t, err, "alpha.txt should exist after rewind")
116			_, err = os.Stat(filepath.Join(s.Dir, "beta.txt"))
117			assert.True(t, os.IsNotExist(err), "beta.txt should not exist after rewind")
118		})
119	}

//     resolution can repair a wrongly-set AgentType. Skipping here would
//     lock in a bad state.
func eventBypassesAgentOwnershipCheck(t agent.EventType) bool {
	return t == agent.SessionStart || t == agent.TurnStart
}

// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ctx context.Context, ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
	if ag == nil {
		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
	}
	if event == nil {
		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
	}

	// Reject path-unsafe identifiers once, here, before any handler uses them to
	// build filesystem paths. Handlers historically validated individually,
	// which is fragile — handleLifecycleTurnEnd builds .entire/metadata/<id>/
	// via os.MkdirAll + os.WriteFile, and handleLifecycleSubagentEnd builds a
	// subagent transcript path from SubagentID and reads it, without their own
	// checks. Centralizing the guard covers every handler (and any future one)
	// uniformly. Empty IDs pass through: handlers apply their own empty-handling
	// (e.g. TurnEnd falls back to a safe constant; SubagentEnd skips the path).
	if event.SessionID != "" {
		if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID in %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
		}
	}
	if event.ToolUseID != "" {
		if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid tool use ID in %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
		}
	}
	if event.SubagentID != "" {
		if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(event.SubagentID); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("invalid subagent ID in %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
		}
	}

	// Filter forwarded hooks: when Cursor IDE forwards events to both
	// .cursor/hooks.json and .claude/settings.json, only the agent that owns
	// the session should process them — otherwise checkpoints, metadata
	// writes, and step counts double.
	if event.SessionID != "" && !eventBypassesAgentOwnershipCheck(event.Type) {
		if state, _ := strategy.LoadSessionState(ctx, event.SessionID); state != nil && state.AgentType != "" && state.AgentType != ag.Type() { //nolint:errcheck // a load failure means we can't filter; let the event reach its handler, which surfaces its own load error
			logging.Info(logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(ctx, "lifecycle"), ag.Name()),
				"skipping forwarded hook for non-owning agent",
				slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
				slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
				slog.String("owning_agent", string(state.AgentType)),
				slog.String("firing_agent", string(ag.Type())),
			)
			return nil
		}
	}

	switch event.Type {
	case agent.SessionStart:
		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.TurnStart:
		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.TurnEnd:
		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.Compaction:
		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.SessionEnd:
		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.SubagentStart:
		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ctx, ag, event)
	case agent.SubagentEnd:
```

	// Fall back to detecting agent from config markers (shadow branches don't have metadata.json).
	// Multiple agent config markers may coexist when users configure multiple agents via
	// `entire configure`. Only return a specific agent type when exactly one agent config
	// marker (directory or file) is present; otherwise return Unknown since we can't
	// determine which agent created the checkpoint.
	var detected types.AgentType
	detectedCount := 0

	if _, err := tree.File(".gemini/settings.json"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeGemini
		detectedCount++
	}
	if _, err := tree.Tree(".claude"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
		detectedCount++
	}
	if _, err := tree.Tree(".opencode"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeOpenCode
		detectedCount++
	} else if _, err := tree.File("opencode.json"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeOpenCode
		detectedCount++
	}
	if _, err := tree.Tree(".codex"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeCodex
		detectedCount++
	}
	if _, err := tree.Tree(".cursor"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeCursor
		detectedCount++
	}
	if _, err := tree.Tree(".factory"); err == nil {
		detected = agent.AgentTypeFactoryAIDroid
		detectedCount++
	}

	if detectedCount == 1 {
		return detected
	}
	return agent.AgentTypeUnknown
}

		// BaseCommit as the base tree (preserving correct agent-line counts
		// when HEAD moved between turns via pull/rebase). Migrate runs BEFORE
		// the LastCheckpointID clear so the reconcile guard can read it.
		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(ctx, repo, state)

	if turnStartErr == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if !errors.Is(turnStartErr, ErrStateNotFound) && !errors.Is(turnStartErr, errPartialState) {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", turnStartErr)
	}

	// Initialize new session (or repair a partial state). The MutateSessionState
	// call below reloads the freshly-persisted state under lock and runs the
	// turn-start mutations on top of it.
	if err := s.initializeSession(ctx, repo, sessionID, resolvedAgentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt, model); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
	}

	mutErr := MutateSessionState(ctx, sessionID, func(state *SessionState) error {
		if transErr := TransitionAndLog(ctx, state, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
			logging.Warn(logging.WithComponent(ctx, "hooks"), "turn start transition failed",
				slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
				slog.String("error", transErr.Error()))
		}
		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(ctx, repo, state)
		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
		return nil
	})
	if mutErr != nil && !errors.Is(mutErr, ErrStateNotFound) {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", mutErr)
	}

	logging.Info(logging.WithComponent(ctx, "hooks"), "initialized shadow session",
		slog.String("session_id", sessionID))
	return nil
}

// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
//

// resolveAgentType picks the best agent type from the context and existing state.
// Priority: existing state > context value.
func resolveAgentType(ctxAgentType types.AgentType, state *SessionState) types.AgentType {
	if state != nil && state.AgentType != "" {
		return state.AgentType
	}
	return ctxAgentType
}

// EnsurePrimaryRef creates or updates the local primary metadata ref. If
// Primary is in Push (i.e. origin tracks it) and the local ref is missing or
// empty, creates/updates the local ref from origin's remote-tracking ref.
// Otherwise creates an empty orphan.
func EnsurePrimaryRef(ctx context.Context, repo *git.Repository) error {
	refs := checkpoint.ResolveCommittedRefs(ctx)
	primaryName := refs.Primary.Short()

	// Origin only tracks Primary when Primary is in Push.
	var remoteRef *plumbing.Reference
	if refs.PrimaryFetchableFromOrigin() {
		var remoteErr error
		remoteRef, remoteErr = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", primaryName), true)
		if remoteErr != nil && !errors.Is(remoteErr, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound) {

	// Split into user content and git comments
	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")

	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
	if userContent == "" {
		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
	}
	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
}

// resolveSessionAgentType picks the most reliable identifier for which agent
// owns a session, given the agent whose hook is firing right now (callerAgentType),
// an optional transcript path, and the SessionStart hint stored under the
// session ID.
//
// Priority (strongest signal wins):
//  1. Transcript path — when transcriptPath is inside a registered agent's
//     GetSessionDir(repoRoot), the file's location is direct evidence.
//  2. SessionStart hint — first agent to fire SessionStart for this session.
//     Useful when transcriptPath is empty (e.g., Claude Code's first TurnStart
//     in a forwarded-hook scenario).
//  3. callerAgentType — the agent whose hook called us. Used when no stronger
//     signal exists.
func resolveSessionAgentType(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, callerAgentType types.AgentType, transcriptPath string) types.AgentType {
	if repoRoot, err := paths.WorktreeRoot(ctx); err == nil && transcriptPath != "" {
		if owner, ok := agent.AgentForTranscriptPath(transcriptPath, repoRoot); ok {
			return owner.Type()
		}
	}
	if hint := LoadAgentTypeHint(ctx, sessionID); hint != "" && hint != agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
		return hint
	}
	return callerAgentType
}

// correctSessionAgentType returns the agent type that owns transcriptPath when
// it disagrees with currentType. Returns (currentType, false) if there's no
// disagreement or no transcript signal — callers should treat that as a no-op.
//
// Used to repair sessions whose AgentType was set incorrectly on an earlier
// turn (e.g., a wrapper agent's hook fired first before the real agent's
// transcript path was available). Only the transcript path is considered;
// the SessionStart hint is intentionally ignored here because it can also be
// the wrong-first-writer that we're trying to correct.
func correctSessionAgentType(ctx context.Context, currentType types.AgentType, transcriptPath string) (types.AgentType, bool) {
	if transcriptPath == "" {
		return currentType, false
	}
	repoRoot, err := paths.WorktreeRoot(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return currentType, false
	}
	owner, ok := agent.AgentForTranscriptPath(transcriptPath, repoRoot)
	if !ok {
		return currentType, false
	}
	if owner.Type() == currentType {
		return currentType, false
	}
	return owner.Type(), true
}

// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
//
// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
//
// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
//
// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as LastPrompt for display).
// model is the LLM model identifier (e.g., "claude-sonnet-4-20250514"); empty if unknown.
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(ctx context.Context, sessionID string, agentType types.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string, model string) error {
	// Concurrent calls for the same session_id are accepted; convergence on
	// AgentType is via correctSessionAgentType on the next turn.
	repo, err := OpenRepository(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
	}
	defer repo.Close()

	// Resolve which agent actually owns this session. The hook firing isn't
	// authoritative when multiple agents' hooks fire for the same session ID
	// (e.g., Cursor IDE forwarding to both .cursor/hooks.json and
	// .claude/settings.json). Stronger signals: transcript path (file lives in
	// a specific agent's session dir) and the SessionStart hint (first agent
	// to claim the session ID).
	resolvedAgentType := resolveSessionAgentType(ctx, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath)

  "content": "package goose\n\nimport (\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n)\n\n// Goose stores sessions in a SQLite database (sessions.db) inside its data\n// directory; there are no native per-session files. Transcripts materialized\n// by prepare-transcript / parse-hook (via `goose session export`) are written\n// alongside the database as <session-id>.json.\n//\n// The session dir deliberately lives OUTSIDE the repo: Entire attributes a\n// session to the agent whose session dir contains its transcript path, and\n// repo-local scratch dirs like .entire/tmp nest inside other external\n// agents' session dirs, which misattributes sessions when several\n// entire-agent-* binaries are installed.\n\n// GetSessionDir returns goose's session storage directory. Goose keys\n// sessions by ID globally (not per-repo), so repoPath does not change the\n// location. GOOSE_PATH_ROOT overrides all goose paths (goose's own hermetic\n// test mechanism); otherwise the data dir follows XDG conventions.\nfunc (a *Agent) GetSessionDir(_ string) (string, error) {\n\tif root := os.Getenv(\"GOOSE_PATH_ROOT\"); root != \"\" {\n\t\treturn filepath.Join(root, \"data\", \"sessions\"), nil\n\t}\n\tdataHome := os.Getenv(\"XDG_DATA_HOME\")\n\tif dataHome == \"\" {\n\t\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t\t}\n\t\tdataHome = filepath.Join(home, \".local\", \"share\")\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(dataHome, \"goose\", \"sessions\"), nil\n}\n\nfunc (a *Agent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDirPath, sessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDirPath, sessionID+\".json\")\n}\n\n// transcriptPath is the materialized export location for a session.\nfunc transcriptPath(sessionID string) string {\n\ta := &Agent{}\n\tdir, err := a.GetSessionDir(\"\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(dir, sessionID+\".json\")\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "- Location: no native file. Implemented approach **(verified)**: materialize via\n  `goose session export --session-id <id> --format json`, written to\n  `<goose-data>/sessions/<session-id>.json` (next to sessions.db) by\n  `prepare-transcript` (`transcript_preparer` capability) and refreshed\n  best-effort by `parse-hook` on session-start/stop/session-end.\n  `session_ref` = that exported path.\n- The session dir must NOT be a repo-local scratch dir like `.entire/tmp/...`:\n  Entire attributes sessions to the agent whose `get-session-dir` contains the\n  transcript path (first match wins), and `.entire/tmp/goose` nests inside\n  amp's/kiro's `.entire/tmp`, so installed `entire-agent-amp` would claim every\n  goose session and TurnEnd checkpoints would be skipped (found the hard way)."
}

// TestLifecycle_SessionPersistence verifies that a session file is created in
// .entire/tmp/ after running a prompt.
func TestLifecycle_SessionPersistence(t *testing.T) {
	testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 2*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
		_, err := s.RunPrompt(t, ctx, "Create a file called session-test.txt with content 'test'. Do not ask for confirmation.")
		require.NoError(t, err, "prompt failed")

		tmpDir := filepath.Join(s.Dir, ".entire", "tmp")
		entries, err := os.ReadDir(tmpDir)
		require.NoError(t, err, "read .entire/tmp/")

		hasSession := false
		for _, entry := range entries {
			if filepath.Ext(entry.Name()) == ".json" {
				hasSession = true
				break
			}
		}
		assert.True(t, hasSession, "expected at least one .json session file in .entire/tmp/")
	})
}

// TestLifecycle_InteractiveSession verifies that an interactive tmux session
// can send prompts and receive responses.
func TestLifecycle_InteractiveSession(t *testing.T) {
	testutil.ForEachAgent(t, 3*time.Minute, func(t *testing.T, s *testutil.RepoState, ctx context.Context) {
		session := s.StartSession(t, ctx)
		if session == nil {
			t.Skip("agent does not support interactive sessions")
		}

		s.Send(t, session, "Create a file called interactive.txt with 'hello'. Do not ask for confirmation.")
		s.WaitFor(t, session, s.Agent.PromptPattern(), 60*time.Second)

		testutil.WaitForFileExists(t, s.Dir, "interactive.txt", 10*time.Second)

		s.Git(t, "add", ".")
		s.Git(t, "commit", "-m", "interactive test")
		testutil.WaitForCheckpoint(t, s, 30*time.Second)
	})
}

func hooksInstalled(t *testing.T, binPath, repoRoot string) bool {
	t.Helper()

	cmd := exec.Command(binPath, "are-hooks-installed")
	cmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
		"ENTIRE_REPO_ROOT="+repoRoot,
		"LANG=en_US.UTF-8",
	)

	out, err := cmd.Output()
	if err != nil {
		if exitErr, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {
			t.Fatalf("%s are-hooks-installed failed: %v\nstdout: %s\nstderr: %s", binPath, err, out, exitErr.Stderr)
		}
		t.Fatalf("%s are-hooks-installed failed: %v\nstdout: %s", binPath, err, out)
	}

	var resp struct {
		Installed bool `json:"installed"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &resp); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("parse are-hooks-installed response: %v\nraw output: %s", err, out)
	}

	return resp.Installed
}
