> AGENT

The branch is clean (the remaining dirty files pre-existed this session). The amp direct-prompt check is still running — I'll classify that failure when it lands, but it doesn't block the Goose deliverable. Final summary below.

---

## ✅ entire-agent-goose: all three phases complete

**Branch `goose-agent`, 3 commits** (`fd2578b` research, `a016b63` scaffold, `2a5995f` implementation)

| Validation | Status |
|---|---|
| Protocol compliance (`external-agents-tests verify`) | **29/29 pass** |
| Lifecycle e2e (all 8 scenarios, real goose + real provider) | **8/8 pass** |
| Unit tests (`go test ./...`) | **pass** |

**Agent details**: binary `entire-agent-goose` (Go, no external deps), capabilities `hooks`, `transcript_analyzer`, `transcript_preparer`, `token_calculator`. Hooks install as a project-scope Goose plugin (`.agents/plugins/entire/hooks/hooks.json`) forwarding `SessionStart`/`UserPromptSubmit`/`Stop`/`SessionEnd` to `entire hooks goose <verb>` — no global Goose config touched, and plugins are enabled by default once the directory exists. Transcripts are materialized from Goose's SQLite store via `goose session export --format json`; tokens come from the export's accumulated session totals. Resume: `goose session --resume --session-id <id>`.

**The one hard bug**: Entire attributes a session to the *first* registered agent whose session dir contains the transcript path. My initial repo-local dir (`.entire/tmp/goose/`) nested inside Amp's (`.entire/tmp`), so the globally-installed `entire-agent-amp` silently claimed every Goose session and TurnEnd checkpoints were skipped. Fix: Goose's session dir is its real data dir (`~/.local/share/goose/sessions/`), outside the repo. This trap is documented in AGENT.md — worth flagging to maintainers since Amp and Kiro currently *share* `.entire/tmp`.

**Shared harness fixes** (benefit all agents): `RewindList` now tolerates the deprecation banner newer `entire` versions print before the JSON, and `SessionPersistence` asks the agent binary for its session dir via `get-session-dir` instead of hardcoding `.entire/tmp` (and only counts files newer than the test start).

**Known gaps**:
- No `Compaction` (event 4) — Goose has no compaction hook; token deltas may jump after internal compaction.
- `calculate-tokens` reports whole-session totals regardless of offset (per-message token counts are NULL in Goose's DB); no cache-token split.
- `text_generator`/`subagent_aware_extractor` deferred (documented as feasible in AGENT.md).
- Amp's lifecycle tests fail on this machine at "prompt failed" (the amp CLI itself, before my harness changes execute) — checking whether that's an auth/environment issue now.

**Process note**: per `CONTRIBUTING.md`, a new agent needs a maintainer-approved issue before a PR — worth opening one referencing this branch before pushing.

> AGENT

Amp question answered: a trivial direct `amp -x "say OK"` hung for 4+ minutes with zero output, so the amp CLI itself is broken/unauthenticated on this machine. That confirms the amp e2e failures are environmental and unrelated to my shared-harness edits (which run after the prompt step that hangs). I've killed the hung processes.

That closes out the pipeline — everything from the summary above stands:

- **Goose: compliance 29/29, lifecycle e2e 8/8, unit tests green**, all committed on `goose-agent` (3 commits, working tree clean).
- The harness fixes I made (`RewindList` deprecation tolerance, `SessionPersistence` using `get-session-dir`) were exercised by the passing goose suite; amp couldn't validate them locally only because its CLI hangs before any test assertion runs.

Suggested next steps when you're ready: open the maintainer issue per CONTRIBUTING.md (new agents need approval before a PR), then push `goose-agent` and reference it.

why did we make questions to amp? I thought we were doing goose

> AGENT

Fair question — Amp was never the target; it came up for two reasons, both in service of the Goose work:

1. **Amp's existing code was the reference implementation.** The skill's write-tests phase says to mirror the existing agents in this repo. Amp's `internal/protocol` package and its export-via-CLI pattern (`amp threads export`) were the closest match to Goose's situation (no native transcript file, materialize via CLI export), so I copied its protocol package and modeled Goose's structure on it.

2. **Amp accidentally broke Goose's checkpoints — that was the biggest bug of the session.** You have `entire-agent-amp` installed globally (`/opt/homebrew/bin`), so Entire registers it in every repo. Entire decides which agent "owns" a session by checking which agent's session directory contains the transcript path — and my first Goose design put transcripts in `.entire/tmp/goose/`, which is nested *inside* Amp's session dir (`.entire/tmp`). Amp registers first alphabetically, so Entire attributed every Goose session to Amp and silently skipped Goose's TurnEnd checkpoints. That's why the rewind tests failed. The fix was moving Goose's session dir to `~/.local/share/goose/sessions/`, outside the repo.

The last thing — running two Amp e2e tests and a direct `amp -x "say OK"` — was a safety check, because fixing the Goose tests required me to edit two **shared** harness files (`e2e/lifecycle_test.go`, `e2e/entire/entire.go`) that Amp's and Kiro's tests also use. I wanted to confirm I hadn't broken the other agents' suites. Result: Amp's failures are environmental (its CLI hangs on a trivial prompt before any of my changed code runs), so the shared edits are fine.

All the actual deliverables are Goose: `agents/entire-agent-goose/` with compliance 29/29, lifecycle 8/8, unit tests green, on branch `goose-agent`.

Confirmed — nothing under `agents/entire-agent-amp/` was touched. The full diff is:

- **16 new files** under `agents/entire-agent-goose/` (I *copied* Amp's generic protocol package into Goose's own module as a starting point, which is the repo's convention — Kiro has its own copy too — but Amp's originals are untouched)
- **1 new file**: `e2e/agents/goose.go` (Goose's test adapter)
- **2 shared test-harness files edited**: `e2e/entire/entire.go` (tolerate the `rewind` deprecation banner) and `e2e/lifecycle_test.go` (ask each agent binary for its session dir instead of hardcoding `.entire/tmp`)

Those last two are shared by all agents' test runs — that's why I smoke-tested against Amp — but they're test infrastructure, not Amp's implementation. Both changes are backward-compatible with how Amp and Kiro behave (their `get-session-dir` still returns `.entire/tmp`, and the banner-stripping is a no-op on older `entire` output).

> DEVELOPER

what are next steps that i need to do to implement this? how do i test this out locally

> AGENT

Two tracks — getting it merged, and trying it yourself.

## Try it locally (5 minutes)

```bash
# 1. Build and put the binary on your PATH
cd ~/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose
go build -o entire-agent-goose ./cmd/entire-agent-goose
ln -s "$PWD/entire-agent-goose" ~/.local/bin/entire-agent-goose   # or cp to /opt/homebrew/bin

# 2. In any git repo you want to try it in:
cd ~/some-project
mkdir -p .entire && echo '{"external_agents": true}' > .entire/settings.json
entire enable --agent goose     # installs .agents/plugins/entire/hooks/hooks.json

# 3. Use goose normally
goose run -t "make a small change to this repo"

# 4. See the results
entire rewind --list            # shadow-branch rewind points per turn
git commit -am "..."            # commit → checkpoint with transcript attached
entire explain                  # checkpoint details
```

To undo in that repo: `entire disable` (removes the plugin hooks).

Caveats on this machine: `mise` is broken (ruby install failure), so use `go build`/`go test` directly; one prompt's hooks add a `goose session export` per turn (fast, local SQLite read).

## Rerun the validation suites

```bash
# Protocol compliance (29 checks)
~/.local/share/mise/installs/go-github-com-entireio-external-agents-tests/*/bin/external-agents-tests \
  verify ~/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose/entire-agent-goose

# Unit tests
cd ~/Documents/work/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-goose && go test ./...

# Lifecycle e2e (makes real LLM calls via your goose provider, ~30s)
cd ~/Documents/work/external-agents/e2e
PATH="$HOME/.local/share/mise/installs/tmux/3.6a:$PATH" E2E_AGENT=goose \
  go test -tags=e2e -v -count=1 -run TestLifecycle ./...
```

## Getting it merged

1. **Open an issue first** on `entireio/external-agents` proposing Goose support — CONTRIBUTING.md explicitly asks for maintainer approval before new-agent PRs (it's a long-term support commitment for them).
2. Once approved: `git push -u origin goose-agent` and open the PR referencing the issue. Worth calling out in the PR description: (a) the **session-ownership pitfall** — Amp and Kiro both use `.entire/tmp` as their session dir, and `AgentForTranscriptPath` first-match-wins means nested/shared dirs misattribute sessions when multiple agent binaries are installed; maintainers may want a CLI-side fix (longest-prefix match); (b) the two shared harness fixes and why.
3. CI will run the compliance suite and per-agent build/test automatically; the lifecycle e2e needs a goose provider configured, so expect that to run only where credentials exist.

One open design question to raise in the issue: whether maintainers prefer transcript exports in goose's data dir (current design, required by the ownership logic) or a CLI-side change that would allow repo-local storage.

// TranscriptCompactor is implemented by agents that can produce Entire
// Transcript Format directly from their native transcript representation.
type TranscriptCompactor interface {
	Agent

	// CompactTranscript converts the transcript referenced by sessionRef into
	// Entire Transcript Format and returns the compact transcript bytes.
	CompactTranscript(ctx context.Context, sessionRef string) (*CompactedTranscript, error)
}

// HookResponseWriter is implemented by agents that support structured hook responses.
// Agents that implement this can output messages (e.g., banners) to the user via
// the agent's response protocol. For example, Claude Code outputs JSON with a
// systemMessage field to stdout. Agents that don't implement this will silently
// skip hook response output.
type HookResponseWriter interface {
	Agent

	// WriteHookResponse outputs a message to the user via the agent's hook response protocol.
	WriteHookResponse(message string) error
}

package amp

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/base64"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"github.com/entireio/external-agents/agents/entire-agent-amp/internal/protocol"
)

const (
	compactTranscriptAgent      = "amp"
	compactTranscriptCLIVersion = "unknown"
)

type compactTranscriptLine struct {
	V            int    `json:"v"`
	Agent        string `json:"agent"`
	CLIVersion   string `json:"cli_version"`
	Type         string `json:"type"`
	TS           string `json:"ts,omitempty"`
	ID           string `json:"id,omitempty"`
	InputTokens  int    `json:"input_tokens,omitempty"`
	OutputTokens int    `json:"output_tokens,omitempty"`
	Content      any    `json:"content"`
}

type compactUserTextBlock struct {
	Text string `json:"text"`
}

type compactAssistantTextBlock struct {
	Type string `json:"type"`
	Text string `json:"text"`
}

type compactAssistantToolUseBlock struct {
	Type   string                 `json:"type"`
	ID     string                 `json:"id,omitempty"`
	Name   string                 `json:"name"`
	Input  any                    `json:"input"`
	Result *compactToolResultJSON `json:"result,omitempty"`
}

type compactToolResultJSON struct {
	Output string `json:"output"`
	Status string `json:"status"`
}

func (a *Agent) CompactTranscript(sessionRef string) (protocol.CompactTranscriptResponse, error) {
	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef)
	if err != nil {
		return protocol.CompactTranscriptResponse{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
	}
	compacted, err := compactTranscriptBytes(data)
	if err != nil {
		return protocol.CompactTranscriptResponse{}, err
	}
	return protocol.CompactTranscriptResponse{Transcript: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(compacted)}, nil
}

func compactTranscriptBytes(data []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	thread, err := parseAmpThread(data)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(thread.Messages) == 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("compact transcript produced no output")
	}

	cliVersion := compactCLIVersion()
	var buf bytes.Buffer
	for _, msg := range thread.Messages {
		switch msg.Role {
		case ThreadMessageRoleUser:
			content := compactUserContent(msg.Content)
			if len(content) == 0 {
				continue
			}
			if err := writeCompactTranscriptLine(&buf, compactTranscriptLine{
				V:          1,
				Agent:      compactTranscriptAgent,
				CLIVersion: cliVersion,
				Type:       "user",
				TS:         threadMessageTimestamp(msg),
				Content:    content,
			}); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
		case ThreadMessageRoleAssistant:
			content := compactAssistantContent(msg.Content, thread.Messages)
			if len(content) == 0 {
				continue
			}
			line := compactTranscriptLine{
				V:          1,
				Agent:      compactTranscriptAgent,
				CLIVersion: cliVersion,
				Type:       "assistant",
				TS:         threadMessageTimestamp(msg),
				ID:         threadMessageIDString(msg.MessageID),
				Content:    content,
			}
			if msg.Usage != nil {
				line.InputTokens = msg.Usage.InputTokens
				line.OutputTokens = msg.Usage.OutputTokens
			}
			if err := writeCompactTranscriptLine(&buf, line); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
		case ThreadMessageRoleInfo:
			// Informational messages have no compact transcript representation.
		}
	}
	if buf.Len() == 0 {
		return nil, errors.New("compact transcript produced no output")
```

		return nil, errors.New("compact transcript produced no output")
	}
	return buf.Bytes(), nil
}

func compactUserContent(blocks []ThreadContentBlock) []compactUserTextBlock {
	out := make([]compactUserTextBlock, 0, len(blocks))
	for _, block := range blocks {
		if block.Type == ThreadContentText && block.Text != "" {
			out = append(out, compactUserTextBlock{Text: block.Text})
		}
	}
	return out
}

func compactAssistantContent(blocks []ThreadContentBlock, messages []ThreadMessage) []any {
	results := collectToolResults(messages)
	out := make([]any, 0, len(blocks))
	for _, block := range blocks {
		switch block.Type {
		case ThreadContentText:
			if block.Text != "" {
				out = append(out, compactAssistantTextBlock{Type: "text", Text: block.Text})
			}
		case ThreadContentToolUse:
			tool := compactAssistantToolUseBlock{
				Type:  "tool_use",
				ID:    block.ID,
				Name:  block.Name,
				Input: decodeToolInput(block.Input),
			}
			if result, ok := results[block.ID]; ok {
				tool.Result = &result
			}
			out = append(out, tool)
		case ThreadContentThinking,
			ThreadContentImage,
			ThreadContentToolResult,
			ThreadContentServerToolUse,
			ThreadContentToolSearchResult:
			// Non-text/non-tool_use blocks are not represented in compact assistant content.
		}
	}
	return out
}

func collectToolResults(messages []ThreadMessage) map[string]compactToolResultJSON {
	results := map[string]compactToolResultJSON{}
	for _, msg := range messages {
		for _, block := range msg.Content {
			if block.Type != ThreadContentToolResult || block.ToolUseID == "" || block.Run == nil {
				continue
			}
			status := "success"
			if block.Run.Status == "error" || block.Run.Status == "cancelled" || block.Run.Error != nil {
				status = "error"
			}
			results[block.ToolUseID] = compactToolResultJSON{Output: toolRunOutput(block.Run), Status: status}
		}
	}
	return results
}

func writeCompactTranscriptLine(buf *bytes.Buffer, line compactTranscriptLine) error {
	encoded, err := json.Marshal(line)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	buf.Write(encoded)
	return buf.WriteByte('\n')
}

func compactCLIVersion() string {
	if version := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("ENTIRE_CLI_VERSION")); version != "" {
		return version
	}
	return compactTranscriptCLIVersion
}

func decodeToolInput(input ThreadToolInput) any {
	if input == nil {
		return map[string]any{}
	}
	decoded := make(map[string]any, len(input))
	for key, raw := range input {
		var value any
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &value); err != nil {
			decoded[key] = string(raw)
			continue
		}
		decoded[key] = value

_, block := range blocks {\n\t\tswitch block.Type {\n\t\tcase \"text\":\n\t\t\tif block.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tout = append(out, compactAssistantTextBlock{Type: \"text\", Text: block.Text})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase \"toolRequest\":\n\t\t\tif block.ToolCall == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttool := compactAssistantToolUseBlock{\n\t\t\t\tType:  \"tool_use\",\n\t\t\t\tID:    block.ID,\n\t\t\t\tName:  block.ToolCall.Value.Name,\n\t\t\t\tInput: decodeToolInput(block.ToolCall.Value.Arguments),\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif result, ok := results[block.ID]; ok && block.ID != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\ttool.Result = &result\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tout = append(out, tool)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// collectToolResults indexes toolResponse blocks (carried in user-role\n// messages) by tool call ID.\nfunc collectToolResults(messages []gooseMessage) map[string]compactToolResultJSON {\n\tresults := map[string]compactToolResultJSON{}\n\tfor _, msg := range messages {\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type != \"toolResponse\" || block.ToolResult == nil || block.ID == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tstatus := \"success\"\n\t\t\tif block.ToolResult.Status != \"success\" || block.ToolResult.Value.IsError {\n\t\t\t\tstatus = \"error\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar parts []string\n\t\t\tfor _, content := range block.ToolResult.Value.Content {\n\t\t\t\tif content.Type == \"text\" && content.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, content.Text)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tresults[block.ID] = compactToolResultJSON{\n\t\t\t\tOutput: strings.Join(parts, \"\\n\"),\n\t\t\t\tStatus: status,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn results\n}\n\nfunc decodeToolInput(raw json.RawMessage) any {\n\tif len(raw) == 0 {\n\t\treturn map[string]any{}\n\t}\n\tvar decoded any\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &decoded); err != nil {\n\t\treturn map[string]any{}\n\t}\n\treturn decoded\n}\n\nfunc compactTimestamp(created int64) string {\n\tif created <= 0 {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\treturn time.Unix(created, 0).UTC().Format(time.RFC3339)\n}\n\nfunc writeCompactTranscriptLine(buf *bytes.Buffer, line compactTranscriptLine) error {\n\tencoded, err := json.Marshal(line)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tbuf.Write(encoded)\n\treturn buf.WriteByte('\\n')\n}\n\nfunc compactCLIVersion() string {\n\tif version := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_CLI_VERSION\")); version != \"\" {\n\t\treturn version\n\t}\n\treturn compactTranscriptCLIVersion\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Fixed and committed (`e25f911`). Here's what was going on and what changed:

**Why you only saw prompts**: Goose's full conversation (responses, tool calls, results) *was* being captured and stored with every checkpoint — but Entire renders conversations through an optional `compact_transcript` capability that converts the agent's native transcript into Entire Transcript Format. I had declared it `false` (Amp implements it; Kiro doesn't), so Entire could only display what `extract-prompts` returns: your side of the conversation.

**What I added**: a `compact-transcript` subcommand that converts the goose export into ETF JSONL — assistant text, tool calls with their inputs, and tool results folded in. I verified it against your actual session from `test-goose-entire` (the ASCII goose / water tracker one) and the full conversation renders, including responses like "Got it! Let me do both things now…" and every `write`/`shell` call with its output.

**Validation**: unit tests pass, compliance suite still fully green (now including the compact-transcript checks), and your `~/.local/bin/entire-agent-goose` symlink already points at the rebuilt binary — `info` reports `compact_transcript: true`.

**To see it**: just run `entire explain` (or the web view) on a checkpoint — including your existing ones, since Entire stores the raw transcript and compacts at display time. New sessions need nothing extra; the binary on your PATH is already current.

> DEVELOPER

> AGENT

Good question — it depends on whether Entire compacts at save time or display time. Let me check the call sites in the CLI source rather than guess:

	if err := store.UpdateSummary(ctx, checkpointID, summary); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save summary: %w", err)
	}

	refs := checkpoint.ResolveCommittedRefs(ctx)
	if err := strategy.MirrorCommittedMetadataRef(ctx, store.Repository(), refs); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("summary was written to %s, but failed to mirror to %s: %w", refs.Primary, refs.Mirror, err)
	}

	styles := newStatusStyles(w)
	rows := summaryProviderRows(provider)
	rows = append(rows, explainRow{Label: "duration", Value: formatSummaryDuration(elapsed)})
	fmt.Fprint(w, styles.renderSuccess(fmt.Sprintf("Summary generated for %s", checkpointID), rows))
	return nil
}

// formatSummaryDuration rounds wall-clock generation time to a human-friendly value.
func formatSummaryDuration(d time.Duration) string {
	return d.Round(100 * time.Millisecond).String()
}

func maybeCompactExternalTranscriptForSummary(ctx context.Context, scopedTranscript []byte, agentType types.AgentType) []byte {
	if transcriptHasSummaryContent(scopedTranscript, agentType) {
		return scopedTranscript
	}

	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
	if err != nil {
		external.DiscoverAndRegister(ctx)
		ag, err = agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
	}
	if err != nil || !external.IsExternal(ag) {
		return scopedTranscript
	}

	compactor, ok := agent.AsTranscriptCompactor(ag)
	if !ok {
		return scopedTranscript
	}

	tmpFile, err := os.CreateTemp("", "entire-summary-transcript-*.jsonl")
	if err != nil {
		logging.Debug(ctx, "external summary compaction unavailable",
			slog.String("agent", string(agentType)),
			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
		return scopedTranscript
	}
	tmpPath := tmpFile.Name()
	defer func() {
		if removeErr := os.Remove(tmpPath); removeErr != nil {
			logging.Debug(ctx, "failed to remove temporary summary transcript",
				slog.String("path", tmpPath),
				slog.String("error", removeErr.Error()))
		}
	}()

	if _, err := tmpFile.Write(scopedTranscript); err != nil {
		_ = tmpFile.Close()
		logging.Debug(ctx, "external summary compaction transcript write failed",
			slog.String("agent", string(agentType)),
			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
		return scopedTranscript
	}
	if err := tmpFile.Close(); err != nil {
		logging.Debug(ctx, "external summary compaction transcript close failed",
			slog.String("agent", string(agentType)),
			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
		return scopedTranscript
	}

	compacted, err := compactor.CompactTranscript(ctx, tmpPath)
	if err != nil || compacted == nil || len(compacted.Transcript) == 0 {
		if err != nil {
			logging.Debug(ctx, "external summary compaction failed",
				slog.String("agent", string(agentType)),
				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
		}
		return scopedTranscript
	}

```
Explain provides human-readable context about sessions, commits, and checkpoints.

Use this command to understand what happened during agent-driven development,
either for self-review or to understand a teammate's work.

By default, shows checkpoints on the current branch. Pass a checkpoint ID or
commit SHA as a positional argument to explain a specific item, or use flags.

Viewing specific items:
  entire explain <id-or-sha>           Auto-detects checkpoint ID or commit SHA
  entire explain --checkpoint <id>     Force interpretation as checkpoint ID
  entire explain --commit <ref>        Force interpretation as commit ref

Filtering the list view:
  --session      Filter checkpoints by session ID (or prefix)

Output verbosity levels (when explaining a specific item):
  Default:         Detailed view with scoped prompts (ID, session, tokens, intent, prompts, files)
  --short          Summary only (ID, session, timestamp, tokens, intent)
  --full           Parsed full transcript (all prompts/responses from entire session)
  --raw-transcript Raw transcript file (JSONL format)

Machine-readable export modes (additive surface for external consumers):
  --json           Metadata-only JSON. Lists checkpoints when no target is given;
                   emits a single checkpoint envelope when a target is supplied.
```

// formatTranscriptBytes formats transcript bytes into a human-readable string.
// It parses the transcript (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini) and formats it using the condensed format.
// The fallback is used for backwards compatibility when transcript parsing fails or is empty.
func formatTranscriptBytes(transcriptBytes []byte, fallback string, agentType types.AgentType) string {
	if len(transcriptBytes) == 0 {
		if fallback != "" {
			return fallback + "\n"
		}
		return "  (none)\n"
	}

	// transcriptBytes is read from checkpoint storage, which redacts on write.
	condensed, err := summarize.BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(redact.AlreadyRedacted(transcriptBytes), agentType)
	if err != nil || len(condensed) == 0 {
		condensed, err = buildCondensedCompactTranscriptEntries(transcriptBytes)
	}
	if err != nil || len(condensed) == 0 {
		if fallback != "" {
			return fallback + "\n"
		}
		return "  (failed to parse transcript)\n"
	}

// session that passed the pre-redaction gate but got its transcript dropped by
// a malformed-JSONL redaction error would write a metadata-only stub.
func skipIfPostRedactionEmpty(logCtx context.Context, redactedTranscript redact.RedactedBytes, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) *CondenseResult {
	if redactedTranscript.Len() > 0 || len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0 {
		return nil
	}
	logging.Info(logCtx, "session skipped: nothing to persist after redaction",
		slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
		slog.String("agent_type", string(state.AgentType)),
		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
	)
	return newSkippedResult(checkpointID, state.SessionID)
}

func newSkippedResult(checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) *CondenseResult {
	return &CondenseResult{
		CheckpointID: checkpointID,
		SessionID:    sessionID,
		Skipped:      true,
	}
}

// redactSessionTranscript redacts the transcript once for use by both the compact
// package and the checkpoint stores. Returns the redacted bytes and the duration
// of the redaction operation for perf logging.
func redactSessionTranscript(ctx context.Context, transcript []byte) (redact.RedactedBytes, time.Duration, error) {
	start := time.Now()
	_, span := perf.Start(ctx, "redact_transcript")
	defer span.End()

	if len(transcript) == 0 {
		return redact.RedactedBytes{}, time.Since(start), nil
	}

	redacted, err := redactSessionJSONLBytes(transcript)
	if err != nil {
		span.RecordError(err)
		return redact.RedactedBytes{}, time.Since(start), fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
	}
	return redacted, time.Since(start), nil
}

// resolveShadowRef returns the shadow branch reference, preferring a pre-resolved
// ref when available and falling back to a repo lookup.
func resolveShadowRef(repo *git.Repository, branchName string, preResolved *plumbing.Reference) (ref *plumbing.Reference, exists bool) {
	if preResolved != nil {
		return preResolved, true
	}
	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName)
	resolved, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false
	}
	return resolved, true
}

// filterFilesTouched narrows sessionData.FilesTouched to files present in
// committedFiles. When no prior files were recorded, it falls back to the
// committed set (minus Entire metadata) — but only when sessionHasEvidenceOfWork
// is true. The fallback was originally unconditional, which let sessions that
// were registered at SessionStart but never produced anything (e.g. ephemeral
// Codex sessions whose hooks fired with a null transcript_path and never
// reached SaveStep) inherit another session's committed files.
func filterFilesTouched(sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, committedFiles map[string]struct{}, state *SessionState) {
	if len(committedFiles) == 0 {
		return
	}
	if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0 {
		filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
		for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
			if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {

// metadata fields that are identical on every output line.
func newTranscriptLine(opts MetadataFields) transcriptLine {
	return transcriptLine{
		V:          1,
		Agent:      opts.Agent,
		CLIVersion: opts.CLIVersion,
	}
}

const toolResultStatusError = "error"

// toolResultJSON is the compact result object inlined into tool_use blocks.
type toolResultJSON struct {
	Output     string              `json:"output"`
	Status     string              `json:"status"`
	File       *toolResultFileJSON `json:"file,omitempty"`
	MatchCount int                 `json:"matchCount,omitempty"`
}

// toolResultFileJSON carries structured file metadata from Read/Edit tool results.
type toolResultFileJSON struct {
	FilePath string `json:"filePath"`
	NumLines int    `json:"numLines,omitempty"`
}

// userTextBlock is a text block within user message content.
type userTextBlock struct {
	ID   string `json:"id,omitempty"`
	Text string `json:"text"`
}

// Compact converts a full.jsonl transcript into the condensed transcript.jsonl format.
// The input must be pre-redacted (via redact.JSONLBytes or
// redact.AlreadyRedacted for trusted sources).
//
// The output format puts version, agent, and cli_version on every line,
// merges streaming assistant fragments with the same message ID, and inlines
// tool results into the preceding assistant's tool_use blocks:
//
//	{"v":1,"agent":"claude-code","cli_version":"0.42.0","type":"user","ts":"...","content":"..."}
//	{"v":1,"agent":"claude-code","cli_version":"0.42.0","type":"assistant","ts":"...","id":"msg_xxx","content":[{"type":"text","text":"..."},{"type":"tool_use","id":"...","name":"...","input":{...},"result":{"output":"...","status":"..."}}]}
func Compact(redacted redact.RedactedBytes, opts MetadataFields) ([]byte, error) {
	content := redacted.Bytes()

	// Formats that need detection on raw content before line truncation:
	// - Single-object formats (OpenCode, Gemini): SliceFromLine would cut
	//   a JSON object mid-value. They handle StartLine as a message-index offset.
	// - Codex: session_meta header is only on the first line. Codex handles
	//   StartLine as a response_item index offset.
	if isOpenCodeFormat(content) {
		return compactOpenCode(content, opts)
	}

	if isGeminiFormat(content) {
		return compactGemini(content, opts)
	}

	// pi: detect on the raw header line. compactPi handles StartLine itself.
	if isPiFormat(content) {
		return compactPi(content, opts)
	}

	if isCodexFormat(content) {
		return compactCodex(content, opts)
	}

	truncated := transcript.SliceFromLine(content, opts.StartLine)
	if truncated == nil {
		truncated = []byte{}
	}

	if isCopilotFormat(truncated) {
		return compactCopilot(truncated, opts)
	}

	if isDroidFormat(truncated) {
		return compactDroid(truncated, opts)
	}

	return compactJSONL(truncated, opts)
}

> AGENT

Here's the complete answer:

**Your old conversation is fine — nothing was lost, and you don't need a new one.** I just ran the new compactor against the transcript stored with your existing checkpoint (`2bbb794089bb`, the water-tracker one) and it renders all 16 conversation lines — your prompt, Goose's replies, every tool call. The responses were always being captured; only the rendering was missing.

But there's a nuance I found while verifying: **the `--full` transcript view won't show responses for old *or* new conversations yet**, because of where the entire CLI calls the new capability:

- **What works now**: summary generation (`entire checkpoint explain --generate`, auto-summarize) — that path calls the external agent's `compact-transcript`, so summaries get the full conversation. Your checkpoint's rich Intent/Outcome/Learnings summary renders fine.
- **What doesn't yet**: `entire checkpoint explain <id> --full` shows "(failed to parse transcript)". I traced it in the CLI source — the display path only tries built-in agent parsers (Claude/Gemini/Codex/…) and then attempts to parse the stored bytes as Entire Transcript Format. It never asks an external agent's binary to compact for display, even on the latest nightly. So Goose (and Amp, which has the same storage situation) hit the fallback error regardless of conversation age.

The fix is a small change in `entireio/cli` — `explain.go` already has `maybeCompactExternalTranscriptForSummary` that does exactly the right thing for summaries; the `--full`/`--verbose` display path in `appendTranscriptSection` just needs to run stored external-agent transcripts through the same compaction before formatting. Since you've got the cli repo at `~/Documents/work/cli`, want me to make that patch? It would light up `--full` for Goose and Amp at once, including all your existing checkpoints.

func buildAmbiguousCheckpointMatches(ids []id.CheckpointID, committed []checkpoint.CommittedInfo) []ambiguousMatch {
func renderExplainBody(w io.Writer, md string) string {
func formatCheckpointOutput(summary *checkpoint.CheckpointSummary, content *checkpoint.SessionContent, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, associatedCommits []associatedCommit, author checkpoint.Author, verbose, full bool, w io.Writer) string {
	infoByID := make(map[id.CheckpointID]checkpoint.CommittedInfo, len(committed))
	for _, info := range committed {
		infoByID[info.CheckpointID] = info
	}
	matches := make([]ambiguousMatch, 0, len(ids))
	for i, cpID := range ids {
		if i >= maxMatches {
			break
		}
		m := ambiguousMatch{ShortID: cpID.String()}
		if info, ok := infoByID[cpID]; ok {
			m.Timestamp = info.CreatedAt
			m.SessionID = info.SessionID
		}
		matches = append(matches, m)
	}
func transcriptHasSummaryContent(transcriptBytes []byte, agentType types.AgentType) bool {
	entries, err := summarize.BuildCondensedTranscriptFromBytes(redact.AlreadyRedacted(transcriptBytes), agentType)
	return err == nil && len(entries) > 0
}

// generateCheckpointAISummary returns the generated summary, the effective
// deadline applied to the underlying call (which may be shorter than the
// requested timeout if the parent context had an earlier deadline), and any
// error. The effective deadline is returned so the caller can render the
// true timeout value in user-facing error messages instead of always
// showing the requested value.
func generateCheckpointAISummary(ctx context.Context, scopedTranscript []byte, filesTouched []string, agentType types.AgentType, generator summarize.Generator, timeout time.Duration) (*checkpoint.Summary, time.Duration, error) {
	timeoutCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, timeout)
	timeoutDuration := timeout
	if deadline, ok := timeoutCtx.Deadline(); ok {
		timeoutDuration = time.Until(deadline)
	}
	defer cancel()

	// scopedTranscript is either read from checkpoint storage (redacted on

683				return err
684			}

699			if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {

701			}

704				return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
705			}
706			return nil
707		}

720			}
721		} else {
722			author, _ = lookup.store.GetCheckpointAuthor(ctx, fullCheckpointID) //nolint:errcheck // Author is optional
723		}
724	
725		// Format and output. Stop spinner BEFORE any write to w to keep stderr
726		// frames and stdout content from interleaving.
727		stopLoad(false)
728		output := formatCheckpointOutput(summary, content, fullCheckpointID, associatedCommits, author, verbose, full, w)
729		outputExplainContent(w, output, noPager)
730		return nil
731	}

# Entire - CLI

This repo contains the CLI for Entire.

## Architecture

- CLI built with github.com/spf13/cobra and github.com/charmbracelet/huh

## Key Directories

### Commands (`cmd/`)

- `entire/`: Main CLI entry point. Also home to kubectl-style external-command resolution (`entire <name>` → `entire-<name>` on PATH) — see [External Commands](docs/architecture/external-commands.md).
- `entire/cli`: CLI utilities and helpers (Cobra commands, helpers, group roots)
- `entire/cli/commands`: actual command implementations
- `entire/cli/agent`: agent implementations (Claude Code, Gemini CLI, OpenCode, Cursor, Factory AI Droid, Copilot CLI, Pi) - see [Agent Integration Checklist](docs/architecture/agent-integration-checklist.md) and [Agent Implementation Guide](docs/architecture/agent-guide.md)
- `entire/cli/strategy`: strategy implementation (manual-commit) - see section below
- `entire/cli/checkpoint`: checkpoint storage abstractions (temporary and committed)
- `entire/cli/session`: session state management
- `entire/cli/integration_test`: integration tests (simulated hooks)
- `e2e/`: E2E tests with real agent calls (see [e2e/README.md](e2e/README.md))

### Command Layout

The CLI is organized around five noun groups plus a small set of top-level
verbs. The groups are the canonical home for each verb; legacy top-level
shortcuts remain functional but hidden, and emit a deprecation hint pointing
at the canonical group form.

- `session` (alias: `sessions`): `list`, `info`, `stop`, `attach`, `resume`, `current`
- `checkpoint` (aliases: `cp`, `checkpoints`): `list`, `explain`, `rewind`, `search`
- `agent`: bare opens the interactive agent selector, plus `list`, `add`, `remove`
- `configure`: bare prints help and a hint pointing at `entire agent`; flags
  manage non-agent settings (telemetry, git-hook installation mode, strategy
  options, summary provider). Agent CRUD lives under `entire agent`.
- `auth`: `login`, `logout`, `status`, `contexts`, `use`. `logout` takes
  `--everywhere` (revoke every session on the active core, not just the
  current one) and `--all-contexts` (log out of every saved login)
- `doctor`: bare runs the scan-and-fix flow, plus `trace`, `logs`, `bundle`
```

You were right, and it's fixed now. The issue wasn't your conversation or the goose agent — it was a gap in the entire CLI itself: the summary path compacts external-agent transcripts through the agent binary, but the transcript *display* path never did, for any external agent (Amp has the same blind spot).

**What I changed** — in your local `~/Documents/work/cli` repo, branch `external-agent-transcript-display` (commit `f38a3d4f9`, +18/−3 lines): `entire checkpoint explain --full`/`--verbose` now runs stored external-agent transcripts through the agent's `compact-transcript` before rendering, reusing the exact helper the summary path already used. Related cli tests pass.

Both fixes work retroactively on every checkpoint you've already made. You now have two branches ready for PRs: `goose-agent` in external-agents and `external-agent-transcript-display` in cli.

## COMPREHENSIVE EXPLORATION REPORT: GOOSE AGENT SUPPORT

Based on thorough exploration of the entire.io web app repository, here are the detailed findings for adding "goose" external agent support:

---

### 1. TRANSCRIPT RENDERING: PARSER ARCHITECTURE & DATA FLOW

**Key Files:**
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/transcript-parsers/` (parser implementations)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/transcript-parsers/registry.ts` (parser registry)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/transcript-parsers/unified-transcript-parser.ts` (shared format)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/session-transcript.ts` (merging logic)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/checkpoint-mapper.ts` (session parsing)

**Supported Transcript Formats:**

The app understands multiple formats via a parser registry pattern:

1. **Unified Transcript Format (JSONL)** — The new standard all agents are migrating to:
   ```jsonl
   {"v":1,"agent":"goose","cli_version":"1.0","type":"user","ts":"2026-06-11T..","content":[{"id":"...","text":"prompt"}]}
   {"v":1,"agent":"goose","cli_version":"1.0","type":"assistant","ts":"2026-06-11T..","id":"msg_..","content":[{"type":"text","text":"response"},{"type":"tool_use",...}]}
   ```
   - Detected by `isUnifiedTranscript()`: checks first line for `{"type":"user"|"assistant","content":[...]}` shape
   - Has optional `agent` and `cli_version` fields
   - Supports text, images, and tool calls in the content array

2. **Agent-Specific Formats:**
   - Claude Code: JSONL with `{type, message, timestamp, requestId}` structure + tool_use/tool_result blocks
   - Amp: Single JSON file with `{messages:[...]}` array
   - OpenCode: JSON with specific message shape
   - Copilot CLI, Cursor, Gemini, Pi, Codex, Droid, Kiro, Antigravity: Each has dedicated parser

**Parser Registry** (`registry.ts` lines 34-59):
Maps normalized agent strings to parser instances. Keys are lowercase/collapsed variants:
```typescript
const parsers: Record<string, TranscriptParser> = {
  "claude code": claudeCodeParser,
  "claude-code": claudeCodeParser,
  claude: claudeCodeParser,
  amp: ampParser,
  // ... etc
}
```

**Parser Interface** (`types.ts`):
```typescript
interface TranscriptParser {
  name: string
  format: "jsonl" | "json"
  parse(content: string): TranscriptParseResult
  parseFrom?(content: string, offset: number): TranscriptParseResult
  strip?(content: string): string
  sliceForStorage?(content: string, offset: number): string | undefined
  classifyTool?(call: ToolCall): ToolMixCategoryKey
  extractSkillFromTool?(call: ToolCall): string | null
  extractMcpServerFromTool?(call: ToolCall): string | null
  extractModel?(content: string): string | null
}
```

**Fallback Behavior:**
- If agent string doesn't match any registered parser → uses `FallbackParser`
- Unknown agent strings are logged to telemetry via `recordSpanAttributes({ "agent.unmapped_raw": id })`
- Frontend/API gracefully degrade to "unknown" agent display (gray icon, "Unknown" label)

**Frontend Rendering** (`frontend/src/domains/platform/sessions/sessionTimelineAdapter.ts`):
- Sessions are stored in checkpoint `sessions` JSON field as array: `[{agent, prompt, steps, model, ...}, ...]`
- Each message includes `timestamp`, `role` (user|assistant), `content`, optional `images`, optional `toolCalls`
- Frontend dedupes messages across checkpoints by ID before displaying

---

### 2. AGENT ATTRIBUTION: REGISTRATION & DISPLAY

**Key Files:**
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/frontend/src/lib/agents.ts` (frontend registry)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/api/src/lib/agents.ts` (backend registry)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/website/src/lib/agents.ts` (marketing site registry)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/frontend/src/components/AgentAvatar.tsx` (rendering)
- `/Users/goose-guest/Documents/work/entire.io/frontend/src/index.css` (styling)

**Current Agents Defined:**
Frontend `agents.ts` (lines 1-14):
```typescript

  | "claude"
  | "gemini"
  | "amp"
  | "codex"
  | "opencode"
  | "copilot"
  | "pi"
  | "cursor"

```

Backend `agents.ts` (lines 3-16):

  "claude", "gemini", "amp", "codex", "opencode", "copilot",
  "pi", "cursor", "droid", "kiro", "antigravity", "unknown"
] as const
```

**Agent Configuration (Frontend)** — `/frontend/src/lib/agents.ts` lines 24-98:
```typescript
export const agents: Record<AgentId, AgentConfig> = {
  claude: {
    id: "claude",
    label: "Claude Code",
    logoUrl: "/images/logos/agents/claude.svg",
    colorClass: "agent-claude",
  },
  // ... per-agent config with label, logo, color
  unknown: {
    id: "unknown",
    label: "Unknown",
    logoUrl: "",
    colorClass: "agent-default",
  },
}
```

**Agent Normalization Logic** (`normalizeAgentId()` — frontend lines 103-125, backend lines 40-60):

The system handles multiple input formats:
1. Direct lowercase match: `"claude"` → `"claude"`
2. Collapsed separators: `"Claude Code"` / `"claude-code"` / `"open code"` → normalized
3. Stripped suffixes: `"claude-code"`, `"copilot cli"`, `"Gemini CLI"` → root agent name
4. Combined: strip suffix, then collapse separators
5. Special case: `"Factory AI Droid"` → `"droid"`
6. **If no match found → returns `"unknown"`, logs raw value to telemetry**

**Agent Display Components:**
- `AgentAvatar.tsx`: Uses `getAgent(id)` to retrieve config, renders SVG logo or generic "AI" box
- Color classes like `.agent-claude`, `.agent-gemini` defined in `index.css` for styling
- Light mode: specific color (e.g., orange-600), Dark mode: lighter variant (orange-400)

**Checkpoint Session Info Structure** (`api/src/types.ts`):
```typescript
export interface SessionInfo {
  agent: string  // Raw value from checkpoint metadata (e.g., "Goose", "Claude Code")
  model?: string
  steps: number
  prompt: string | null
  sessionId?: string
  // ... other optional fields
}
```

---

### 3. DATA FLOW: HOW AGENT FIELD REACHES THE APP

**Checkpoint Ingestion Path:**

1. **Agent metadata originates from checkpoint commit** (stored by CLI):
   - Each agent (Claude Code CLI, Goose, etc.) writes checkpoints with metadata
   - `sessions` field is a JSON array stored in `repo_checkpoints.sessions` table column

2. **Checkpoint Database Schema** (`api/db/schema.ts`):
   ```typescript
   export interface RepoCheckpoints {
     checkpoint_id: string
     sessions: Json | null           // Array of SessionInfo[]
     primary_agent_raw: string        // Generated column from sessions[]
     dead_session_indices: Json | null
     prompt: string | null
     model: string | null
     steps: number
     // ... other fields
   }
   ```

3. **Sessions Parsing** (`checkpoint-mapper.ts` lines 14-35):
   ```typescript
   function parseStoredSessions(value: unknown): SessionInfo[] | null
   function parseSessionsFromRow(row): SessionInfo[]
   ```
   - Parses raw JSON into typed SessionInfo array
   - Falls back to synthetic `[{agent: "Unknown", prompt, steps}]` if empty

4. **Primary Agent Attribution** (`checkpoint-agent-attribution.ts` lines 46-54):
   ```typescript
   export function getCheckpointPrimaryAgent(cp: CheckpointAgentAttributionInput): AgentId {
     const sessions = parseStoredSessions(cp.sessions ?? null) ?? []
     // Walks back-to-front to find most recent non-"unknown" agent
     for (let i = sessions.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
       const agent = getAgentId(sessions[i]?.agent)
       if (agent !== "unknown") return agent
     }
     return "unknown"
   }
   ```

5. **API Response Flow** (`checkpoint-mapper.ts` mapCheckpointToInfo()`):
   - Parses `sessions` JSON → filters by dead indices → maps to SessionInfo
   - Each session carries its raw `agent` string
   - Frontend calls `getAgent(agentString)` to normalize and render

**GitHub Webhook → Checkpoint Update:**
- Webhooks hit `/api/v1/webhooks/github` (webhooks.ts)
- Payload is enqueued to `WEBHOOK_QUEUE`
- Background processor syncs commits from GitHub, triggering checkpoint fetch/analysis
- Checkpoint metadata (including agent/sessions) is populated from committed files

---

### SUMMARY: INTEGRATION CHECKLIST FOR GOOSE SUPPORT

**To add "goose" support, you need to:**

   - `api/src/lib/agents.ts`: Add `"goose"` to `AGENT_IDS` array and `AGENT_LABELS` record

---

**Key Design Insights:**
- The app is **agent-agnostic** at the transcript level — it just needs `parse()` to return `TranscriptMessage[]`
- Normalization is **forgiving** — "Goose Code", "goose-agent", "Goose", "goose" all work with proper regex rules
- **Fallback is safe** — unknown agents render with gray icon, don't break the UI
- **Unified JSONL is preferred** — new agents should emit that format rather than custom formats
- **Session-level attribution** — each session in a checkpoint can have a different agent (multi-agent sessions supported)

Exit code 1
import type { TranscriptMessage, ToolCall, ToolMixCategoryKey } from "../../types"

/**
 * Transcript file format:
 * - "jsonl": One JSON object per line (Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot CLI)
 * - "json": Single JSON object with a messages array (OpenCode, Gemini CLI)
 */
export type TranscriptFormat = "jsonl" | "json"

/**
 * Result of parsing a transcript, including metadata about parse quality.
 */
export interface TranscriptParseResult {
  messages: TranscriptMessage[]
  /** True when the parser is a best-effort fallback, not a native parser for this agent */
  isFallback: boolean
  /** Warning message when parsing may be incomplete */
  warning?: string
}

/**
 * Interface that every agent transcript parser must implement.
 */
export interface TranscriptParser {
  /** Human-readable name for this parser */
  readonly name: string
  /** Transcript file format this parser expects */
  readonly format: TranscriptFormat
  /** Parse raw transcript content into transcript messages */
  parse(content: string): TranscriptParseResult
  /**
   * Slice raw transcript content before stripping and storage.
   * Parsers with non-standard checkpoint offsets can override the shared
   * JSONL/JSON slicing behavior.
   * Returns undefined to use the shared format-based storage slicing.
   */
  sliceForStorage?(content: string, offset: number): string | undefined
  /**
   * Strip transcript content down to only what parse() needs.
   * For JSONL formats, the shared stripTranscript handles this.
   * JSON-format parsers should implement this to drop large fields
   * (diffs, summaries, etc.) that the parser doesn't read.
   * Returns the stripped content string. Default: return as-is.
   */
  strip?(content: string): string
  /**
   * Parse transcript content starting from the given offset.
   * For JSONL parsers: offset = line number to start from.
   * For JSON parsers: offset = message index to start from.
   * Returns only messages from the offset onward.
   */
  parseFrom?(content: string, offset: number): TranscriptParseResult
  /**
   * Classify a tool call into the shared toolMix bucket. Optional —
   * undefined means "I don't know how to classify this agent's tools";
   * callers default to "other" via the optional-chaining fallback.
   */
  classifyTool?(call: ToolCall): ToolMixCategoryKey
  /**
   * Extract a skill name from a tool call, when applicable. Returns null
   * when the call doesn't represent a skill invocation. Claude implements
   * via the `Skill` tool; other agents return null until they grow the
   * same surface.
   */
  extractSkillFromTool?(call: ToolCall): string | null
  /**
   * Extract an MCP server name from a tool call, when applicable. Returns
   * null when the call isn't an MCP invocation. Claude parses the
   * `mcp__server__tool` prefix convention; other agents return null
   * until their parsers surface MCP server names.
   */
  extractMcpServerFromTool?(call: ToolCall): string | null
  /**
   * Extract the model identifier from raw transcript content, for agents that
   * record it in-band rather than in committed metadata. Pi emits a
   * `model_change` event (`{type:"model_change", modelId, provider}`) at the
   * session start; Claude carries the model on each metadata file instead and
   * returns null here. Returns the last model seen, or null when the content
   * carries no model signal. Used to backfill `SessionInfo.model` during
   * hydration. Accepts a single line or a whole transcript.

import type { ToolCall, ToolMixCategoryKey } from "../../types"
import { AmpParser } from "./amp-parser"
import { AntigravityParser } from "./antigravity-parser"
import { ClaudeCodeParser } from "./claude-code-parser"
import { CodexParser } from "./codex-parser"
import { CopilotCliParser } from "./copilot-cli-parser"
import { CursorParser } from "./cursor-parser"
import { DroidParser } from "./droid-parser"
import { FallbackParser } from "./fallback-parser"
import { GeminiParser } from "./gemini-parser"
import { KiroParser } from "./kiro-parser"
import { OpenCodeParser } from "./opencode-parser"
import { PiParser } from "./pi-parser"
import { jsonlSliceForStorage, type TranscriptParser } from "./types"
import { UnifiedTranscriptParser, isUnifiedTranscript } from "./unified-transcript-parser"

const ampParser = new AmpParser()
const antigravityParser = new AntigravityParser()
const claudeCodeParser = new ClaudeCodeParser()
const codexParser = new CodexParser()
const copilotCliParser = new CopilotCliParser()
const cursorParser = new CursorParser()
const droidParser = new DroidParser()
const fallbackParser = new FallbackParser()
const geminiParser = new GeminiParser()
const kiroParser = new KiroParser()
const opencodeParser = new OpenCodeParser()
const piParser = new PiParser()
const unifiedTranscriptParser = new UnifiedTranscriptParser()

/**
 * Map of normalized agent strings to their parsers.
 */
const parsers: Record<string, TranscriptParser> = {
  "claude code": claudeCodeParser,
  claude: claudeCodeParser,
  "claude-code": claudeCodeParser,
  amp: ampParser,
  codex: codexParser,
  "openai codex": codexParser,
  "copilot cli": copilotCliParser,
  "copilot-cli": copilotCliParser,
  copilot: copilotCliParser,
  cursor: cursorParser,
  "gemini cli": geminiParser,
  "gemini-cli": geminiParser,
  gemini: geminiParser,
  "factory ai droid": droidParser,
  "factoryai-droid": droidParser,
  droid: droidParser,
  kiro: kiroParser,
  antigravity: antigravityParser,
  "antigravity cli": antigravityParser,
  "antigravity-cli": antigravityParser,
  opencode: opencodeParser,
  "open code": opencodeParser,
  "open-code": opencodeParser,
  pi: piParser,
}

export interface ParserOptions {
  /** When true, try the shared transcript.jsonl parser before the agent-specific one. */
  enableUnifiedTranscriptParser?: boolean
}

/**
 * Wraps an agent-specific parser so that transcript.jsonl content is handled
 * by the shared UnifiedTranscriptParser, falling back to the agent parser otherwise.
 */
class UnifiedTranscriptParserWithFallback implements TranscriptParser {
  readonly name: string
  readonly format: TranscriptParser["format"]

  constructor(private readonly agentParser: TranscriptParser) {
    this.name = agentParser.name
    this.format = agentParser.format
  }

  parse(content: string) {
    if (isUnifiedTranscript(content)) return unifiedTranscriptParser.parse(content)
    return this.agentParser.parse(content)
  }

  parseFrom(content: string, offset: number) {
    if (isUnifiedTranscript(content)) return unifiedTranscriptParser.parseFrom(content, offset)
    return this.agentParser.parseFrom?.(content, offset) ?? this.agentParser.parse(content)
  }

  strip(content: string): string {
    if (isUnifiedTranscript(content)) return unifiedTranscriptParser.strip(content)
    return this.agentParser.strip?.(content) ?? content
  }

  sliceForStorage(content: string, offset: number): string | undefined {
    if (isUnifiedTranscript(content)) return jsonlSliceForStorage(content, offset)
    return this.agentParser.sliceForStorage?.(content, offset)
  }

  classifyTool(call: ToolCall): ToolMixCategoryKey {
    return this.agentParser.classifyTool?.(call) ?? "other"
  }

  extractSkillFromTool(call: ToolCall): string | null {
    return this.agentParser.extractSkillFromTool?.(call) ?? null
  }

  extractMcpServerFromTool(call: ToolCall): string | null {
    return this.agentParser.extractMcpServerFromTool?.(call) ?? null
  }

  extractModel(content: string): string | null {
    return this.agentParser.extractModel?.(content) ?? null
  }
}

/**
 * Get the appropriate transcript parser for a given agent string.
 * Falls back to a generic parser for unknown agents.
 *
 * When `options.enableUnifiedTranscriptParser` is true (admin-only), the returned
 * parser will auto-detect transcript.jsonl content and use the shared
 * UnifiedTranscriptParser before falling back to the agent-specific parser.
 */
export function getParserForAgent(agent: string, options?: ParserOptions): TranscriptParser {
  const normalized = agent.trim().toLowerCase()
  const parser = parsers[normalized] ?? fallbackParser

  if (options?.enableUnifiedTranscriptParser) {
    return new UnifiedTranscriptParserWithFallback(parser)
  }

  return parser
}

/**
 * Check whether a dedicated (non-fallback) parser exists for the given agent.
 * Used to detect when a cached fallback-parsed transcript can now be re-parsed
 * with a proper parser.
 */
export function hasNonFallbackParser(agent: string): boolean {
  const normalized = agent.trim().toLowerCase()
  return normalized in parsers
}

import type { ToolCall, ToolCallResult, ToolMixCategoryKey, TranscriptMessage } from "../../types"
import type { TranscriptParseResult, TranscriptParser } from "./types"

interface AmpLog {
  created?: number
  messages?: AmpMessage[]
  [key: string]: unknown
}

interface AmpMessage {
  role?: string
  messageId?: number | string
  timestamp?: string
  meta?: {
    sentAt?: number
  }
  usage?: {
    timestamp?: string
  }
  content?: AmpContentBlock[]
}

interface AmpContentBlock {
  type?: string
  text?: string
  id?: string
  name?: string
  input?: Record<string, unknown>
  startTime?: number
  finalTime?: number
  toolUseID?: string
  run?: {
    status?: string
    result?: unknown
  }
}

function parseAmpLog(content: string): AmpLog | null {
  try {
    const log = JSON.parse(content) as AmpLog
    if (!Array.isArray(log.messages)) return null
    return log
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function msToISO(ms: number | undefined, fallback: string): string {
  return typeof ms === "number" && Number.isFinite(ms) ? new Date(ms).toISOString() : fallback
}

function messageId(message: AmpMessage): string {
  if (message.messageId !== undefined) return String(message.messageId)
  return crypto.randomUUID()
}

function messageTimestamp(message: AmpMessage, fallback: string): string {
  if (typeof message.timestamp === "string" && message.timestamp) return message.timestamp
  if (typeof message.usage?.timestamp === "string" && message.usage.timestamp) {
    return message.usage.timestamp
  }
  if (typeof message.meta?.sentAt === "number") return msToISO(message.meta.sentAt, fallback)

  const firstTimedBlock = message.content?.find(
    (block) => typeof block.startTime === "number" || typeof block.finalTime === "number",
  )
  return msToISO(firstTimedBlock?.startTime ?? firstTimedBlock?.finalTime, fallback)
}

function blockTimestamp(block: AmpContentBlock, fallback: string): string {
  return msToISO(block.startTime ?? block.finalTime, fallback)
}

function extractText(content: AmpMessage["content"]): string {
  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return ""

  return content
    .flatMap((block) => {
      if (block.type !== "text") return []
      if (typeof block.text !== "string" || !block.text.trim()) return []
      return [block.text]
    })
    .join("\n")
    .trim()
}

function extractToolCalls(content: AmpMessage["content"], fallbackTimestamp: string): ToolCall[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return []

  return content
    .filter((block) => block.type === "tool_use" && typeof block.name === "string")
    .map((block) => ({
      id: block.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
      name: block.name!,
      input: block.input ?? {},
      timestamp: blockTimestamp(block, fallbackTimestamp),
    }))
}

function extractToolResults(messages: AmpMessage[]): Map<string, ToolCallResult> {
  const results = new Map<string, ToolCallResult>()

  for (const message of messages) {
    if (message.role !== "user" || !Array.isArray(message.content)) continue

    for (const block of message.content) {
      if (block.type !== "tool_result" || !block.toolUseID) continue
      results.set(block.toolUseID, parseToolResult(block.run))
    }
  }

  return results
}

function parseToolResult(run: AmpContentBlock["run"]): ToolCallResult {
  const result = run?.result
  const resultObject =
    typeof result === "object" && result !== null ? (result as Record<string, unknown>) : null
  const exitCode = typeof resultObject?.exitCode === "number" ? resultObject.exitCode : null
  const status = typeof run?.status === "string" ? run.status.toLowerCase() : null
  const isError =
    (status !== null && !["done", "success", "completed"].includes(status)) ||
    (exitCode !== null && exitCode !== 0)

  const output = extractToolOutput(result)
  const parsed: ToolCallResult = {
    type: isError ? "error" : "text",
  }

  if (resultObject && typeof resultObject.absolutePath === "string") {
    parsed.file = { filePath: resultObject.absolutePath }
  }

  if (output !== undefined) {
    parsed.output = output
  }

  if (isError) {
    parsed.error = output ?? run?.status ?? "Tool call failed"
  }

  return parsed
}

function extractToolOutput(result: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof result === "string") return result
  if (typeof result !== "object" || result === null) return undefined

  const parsed = result as Record<string, unknown>
  if (typeof parsed.output === "string") return parsed.output

function parseAmpMessages(messages: AmpMessage[], sessionCreated: string): TranscriptMessage[] {
  const toolResultById = extractToolResults(messages)
  const result: TranscriptMessage[] = []

  let pendingAssistantText: string[] = []
  let pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = []
  let pendingTimestamp: string | null = null
  let pendingId: string | null = null

  const flushAssistant = () => {
    if (pendingAssistantText.length === 0 && pendingToolCalls.length === 0) {
      pendingTimestamp = null
      pendingId = null
      return
    }

    for (const toolCall of pendingToolCalls) {
      const toolResult = toolResultById.get(toolCall.id)
      if (toolResult) toolCall.result = toolResult
    }

    result.push({
      id: pendingId ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
      role: "assistant",
      content: pendingAssistantText.join("\n\n").trim(),
      timestamp: pendingTimestamp ?? sessionCreated,
      toolCalls: pendingToolCalls.length > 0 ? [...pendingToolCalls] : undefined,
    })

    pendingAssistantText = []
    pendingToolCalls = []
    pendingTimestamp = null
    pendingId = null
  }

  for (const message of messages) {
    const timestamp = messageTimestamp(message, sessionCreated)

    if (message.role === "user") {
      const text = extractText(message.content)
      if (!text) continue

      flushAssistant()
      result.push({
        id: messageId(message),
        role: "user",
        content: text,
        timestamp,
      })
      continue
    }

    if (message.role === "assistant") {
      if (!pendingTimestamp) pendingTimestamp = timestamp
      if (!pendingId) pendingId = messageId(message)

      const text = extractText(message.content)
      if (text) pendingAssistantText.push(text)
      pendingToolCalls.push(...extractToolCalls(message.content, timestamp))
    }
  }

  flushAssistant()
  return result
}

interface LineSpan {
  startLine: number
  endLine: number
}

function messageLineSpans(content: string, expectedMessageCount: number): LineSpan[] {
  const keyIndex = content.search(/"messages"\s*:/)
  if (keyIndex === -1) return []

import { recordSpanAttributes } from "./telemetry"

const AGENT_IDS = [
  "claude",
  "gemini",
  "amp",
  "codex",
  "opencode",
  "copilot",
  "pi",
  "cursor",
  "droid",
  "kiro",
  "antigravity",
  "unknown",
] as const

export type AgentId = (typeof AGENT_IDS)[number]

const AGENT_ID_SET: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(AGENT_IDS)
const AGENT_LABELS: Record<AgentId, string> = {
  claude: "Claude Code",
  gemini: "Gemini",
  amp: "Amp",
  codex: "Codex",
  opencode: "OpenCode",
  copilot: "Copilot",
  pi: "Pi",
  cursor: "Cursor",
  droid: "Factory AI Droid",
  kiro: "Kiro",
  antigravity: "Antigravity",
  unknown: "Unknown",
}

function isAgentId(s: string): s is AgentId {
  return AGENT_ID_SET.has(s)
}

function normalizeAgentId(id: string): AgentId | null {
  const lower = id.trim().toLowerCase()

  if (isAgentId(lower)) return lower

  // "open code" / "open-code" -> "opencode"
  const collapsed = lower.replace(/[- ]/g, "")
  if (isAgentId(collapsed)) return collapsed

  // "claude code", "copilot cli" -> "claude", "copilot"
  const stripped = lower.replace(/[- ](code|cli)$/, "")
  if (isAgentId(stripped)) return stripped

  // "Open Code CLI" -> "opencode"
  const strippedCollapsed = stripped.replace(/[- ]/g, "")
  if (isAgentId(strippedCollapsed)) return strippedCollapsed

  if (collapsed.includes("factoryaidroid")) return "droid"

  return null
}

export function getAgentId(id: string | null | undefined): AgentId {
  if (!id) return "unknown"
  if (isAgentId(id)) return id
  const normalized = normalizeAgentId(id)
  if (normalized) return normalized
  // A non-empty agent string that doesn't normalize to any known id is a
  // bug-class signal: either the storage layer received a typo, or a new
  // agent shipped before AGENT_IDS was updated. Record the raw value so
  // the operator can grep traces for unmapped names without having to
  // wait for the user-visible "my recap is empty" symptom.
  recordSpanAttributes({ "agent.unmapped_raw": id })
  return "unknown"
}

export function getAgentLabel(id: AgentId): string {
  return AGENT_LABELS[id]
}

export function emptyAgentContributions(): Record<AgentId, number> {
  return Object.fromEntries(AGENT_IDS.map((id) => [id, 0])) as Record<AgentId, number>
}

Message content blocks are camelCase:\n *\n *   {\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"...\"}\n *   {\"type\":\"toolRequest\",\"id\":\"toolu_..\",\"toolCall\":{\"status\":\"success\",\n *     \"value\":{\"name\":\"write\",\"arguments\":{\"path\":\"...\",\"content\":\"...\"}}}}\n *   {\"type\":\"toolResponse\",\"id\":\"toolu_..\",\"toolResult\":{\"status\":\"success\",\n * string\n  id?: string\n  toolCall?: {\n    status?: string\n    value?: {\n      name?: string\n      arguments?: Record<string, unknown>\n    }\n  }\n  toolResult?: {\n    status?: string\n    value?: {\n      content?: { type?: string; text?: string }[]\n      isError?: boolean\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction parseGooseExport(content: string): GooseExport | null {\n  try {\n    const log = JSON.parse(content) as GooseExport\n    if (!Array.isArray(log.conversation)) return null\n    return log\n  } catch {\n    return null\n  }\n}\n\nfunction messageTimestamp(message: GooseMessage, fallback: string): string {\n  if (typeof message.created === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(message.created)) {\n    return new Date(message.created * 1000).toISOString()\n  }\n  return fallback\n}\n\nfunction sessionCreated(log: GooseExport): string {\n  if (typeof log.created_at === \"string\" && log.created_at) return log.created_at\n  return new Date(0).toISOString()\n}\n\nfunction extractText(content: GooseMessage[\"content\"]): string {\n  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return \"\"\n\n  return content\n    .flatMap((block) => {\n      if (block.type !== \"text\") return []\n      if (typeof block.text !== \"string\" || !block.text.trim()) return []\n      return [block.text]\n    })\n    .join(\"\\n\")\n    .trim()\n}\n\nfunction extractToolCalls(content: GooseMessage[\"content\"], timestamp: string): ToolCall[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return []\n\n  return content\n    .filter((block) => block.type === \"toolRequest\" && typeof block.toolCall?.value?.name === \"string\")\n    .map((block) => ({\n      id: block.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),\n      name: block.toolCall!.value!.name!,\n      input: block.toolCall?.value?.arguments ?? {},\n      timestamp,\n    }))\n}\n\nfunction extractToolResults(messages: GooseMessage[]): Map<string, ToolCallResult> {\n  const results = new Map<string, ToolCallResult>()\n\n  for (const message of messages) {\n    if (message.role !== \"user\" || !Array.isArray(message.content)) continue\n\n    for (const block of message.content) {\n      if (block.type !== \"toolResponse\" || !block.id || !block.toolResult) continue\n      results.set(block.id, parseToolResult(block.toolResult))\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results\n}\n\nfunction parseToolResult(toolResult: NonNullable<GooseContentBlock[\"toolResult\"]>): ToolCallResult {\n  const isError =\n    toolResult.value?.isError === true ||\n    (typeof toolResult.status === \"string\" && toolResult.status.toLowerCase() !== \"success\")\n\n  const output = (toolResult.value?.content ?? [])\n    .flatMap((block) => {\n      if (block.type !== \"text\") return []\n      if (typeof block.text !== \"string\" || !block.text) return []\n      return [block.text]\n    })\n    .join(\"\\n\")\n\n  const parsed: ToolCallResult = {\n    type: isError ? \"error\" : \"text\",\n  }\n  if (output) parsed.output = output\n  if (isError) parsed.error = output || toolResult.status || \"Tool call failed\"\n\n  return parsed\n}\n\nfunction parseGooseMessages(log: GooseExport, fromIndex: number): TranscriptMessage[] {\n  const conversation = log.conversation ?? []\n  const created = sessionCreated(log)\n  // Tool results can land after the offset boundary, so index them across\n  // the whole conversation before slicing.\n  const toolResultById = extractToolResults(conversation)\n  const result: TranscriptMessage[] = []\n\n  let pendingAssistantText: string[] = []\n  let pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = []\n  let pendingTimestamp: string | null = null\n  let pendingId: string | null = null\n\n  const flushAssistant = () => {\n    if (pendingAssistantText.length === 0 && pendingToolCalls.length === 0) {\n      pendingTimestamp = null\n      pendingId = null\n      return\n    }\n\n    for (const toolCall of pendingToolCalls) {\n      const toolResult = toolResultById.get(toolCall.id)\n      if (toolResult) toolCall.result = toolResult\n    }\n\n    result.push({\n      id: pendingId ?? crypto.randomUUID(),\n      role: \"assistant\",\n      content: pendingAssistantText.join(\"\\n\\n\").trim(),\n      timestamp: pendingTimestamp ?? created,\n      toolCalls: pendingToolCalls.length > 0 ? [...pendingToolCalls] : undefined,\n    })\n\n    pendingAssistantText = []\n    pendingToolCalls = []\n    pendingTimestamp = null\n    pendingId = null\n  }\n\n  const startIndex = Math.max(0, fromIndex)\n  for (let i = startIndex; i < conversation.length; i++) {\n    const message = conversation[i]!\n    const timestamp = messageTimestamp(message, created)\n\n    if (message.role === \"user\") {\n      // toolResponse-only user messages are tool results, not prompts.\n      const text = extractText(message.content)\n      if (!text) continue\n\n      flushAssistant()\n      result.push({\n        id: message.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),\n        role: \"user\",\n        content: text,\n        timestamp,\n      })\n      continue\n    }\n\n    if (message.role === \"assistant\") {\n      if (!pendingTimestamp) pendingTimestamp = timestamp\n      if (!pendingId) pendingId = message.id ?? null\n\n      const text = extractText(message.content)\n      if (text) pendingAssistantText.push(text)\n      pendingToolCalls.push(...extractToolCalls(message.content, timestamp))\n    }\n  }\n\n  flushAssistant()\n  return result\n}\n\nexport class GooseParser implements TranscriptParser {\n  readonly name = \"Goose\"\n  readonly format = \"json\" as const\n\n  parse(content: string): TranscriptParseResult {\n    return this.parseFrom(content, 0)\n  }\n\n  parseFrom(content: string, offset: number): TranscriptParseResult {\n    const log = parseGooseExport(content)\n    if (!log) {\n      return {\n        messages: [],\n        isFallback: false,\n        warning: \"Unable to parse Goose transcript\",\n      }\n    }\n\n    return {\n      messages: parseGooseMessages(log, offset),\n      isFallback: false,\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Drop session metadata the parser doesn't read (extension_data, recipe,\n   * token counters, ...) while keeping conversation content and the model.\n   */\n  strip(content: string): string {\n    const log = parseGooseExport(content)\n    if (!log) return content\n\n    const stripped: GooseExport = {\n      id: log.id,\n      created_at: log.created_at,\n      conversation: log.conversation,\n    }\n    if (log.model_config?.model_name) {\n      stripped.model_config = { model_name: log.model_config.model_name }\n    }\n    return JSON.stringify(stripped)\n  }\n\n  classifyTool(call: ToolCall): ToolMixCategoryKey {\n    // Strip goose's extension-qualified prefix (developer__shell -> shell).\n    const name = call.name.includes(\"__\") ? call.name.slice(call.name.lastIndexOf(\"__\") + 2) : call.name\n    if (name === \"shell\" || name === \"bash\") return \"shell\"\n    if (name === \"write\" || name === \"text_editor\" || name === \"edit\" || name === \"read\" || name === \"view\") {\n      return \"fileOps\"\n    }\n    if (name === \"grep\" || name === \"glob\" || name === \"tree\" || name === \"analyze\" || name === \"search\") {\n      return \"search\"\n    }\n    if (name === \"subagent\" || name === \"dynamic_task\" || name === \"task\") return \"agent\"\n    return \"other\"\n  }\n\n  extractSkillFromTool(call: ToolCall): string | null {\n    if (call.name !== \"load_skill\" && !call.name.endsWith(\"__load_skill\")) return null\n    const name = call.input[\"name\"] ?? call.input[\"skill\"]\n    return typeof name === \"string\" && name ? name : null\n  }\n\n  extractMcpServerFromTool(_call: ToolCall): string | null {\n    return null\n  }\n\n  extractModel(content: string): string | null {\n    const log = parseGooseExport(content)\n    const model = log?.model_config?.model_name\n    return typeof model === \"string\" && model ? model : null\n  }\n}\n"
}

created: 1781175772,\n      content: [\n        {\n          type: \"toolRequest\",\n          id: \"toolu_1\",\n          toolCall: {\n            status: \"success\",\n            value: {\n              name: \"write\",\n              arguments: { path: \"/tmp/workspace/verify.txt\", content: \"ENTIRE_VERIFY_FILE\" },\n            },\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"msg_3\",\n      role: \"user\",\n      created: 1781175773,\n      content: [\n        {\n          type: \"toolResponse\",\n          id: \"toolu_1\",\n          toolResult: {\n            status: \"success\",\n            value: {\n              content: [{ type: \"text\", text: \"Created /tmp/workspace/verify.txt (1 lines)\" }],\n              isError: false,\n            },\n          },\n        },\n      ],\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"msg_4\",\n      role: \"assistant\",\n      created: 1781175775,\n      content: [{ type: \"text\", text: \"ENTIRE_VERIFY_OK\" }],\n    },\n  ],\n})\n\ndescribe(\"GooseParser\", () => {\n  const parser = new GooseParser()\n\n  it(\"parses user prompts, assistant text, and tool calls with folded results\", () => {\n    const result = parser.parse(exportFixture)\n\n    expect(result.isFallback).toBe(false)\n    expect(result.warning).toBeUndefined()\n    expect(result.messages).toHaveLength(2)\n\n    const [user, assistant] = result.messages\n    expect(user).toMatchObject({\n      id: \"msg_1\",\n      role: \"user\",\n      content: \"Create a file named verify.txt\",\n      timestamp: new Date(1781175769 * 1000).toISOString(),\n    })\n\n    // The toolResponse user message must fold into the assistant turn, and\n    // consecutive assistant messages merge into one.\n    expect(assistant!.role).toBe(\"assistant\")\n    expect(assistant!.content).toBe(\"ENTIRE_VERIFY_OK\")\n    expect(assistant!.toolCalls).toHaveLength(1)\n    expect(assistant!.toolCalls![0]).toMatchObject({\n      id: \"toolu_1\",\n      name: \"write\",\n      input: { path: \"/tmp/workspace/verify.txt\", content: \"ENTIRE_VERIFY_FILE\" },\n      result: {\n        type: \"text\",\n        output: \"Created /tmp/workspace/verify.txt (1 lines)\",\n      },\n    })\n  })\n\n  it(\"parses from a message-index offset\", () => {\n    const result = parser.parseFrom(exportFixture, 3)\n\n    expect(result.messages).toHaveLength(1)\n    expect(result.messages[0]).toMatchObject({\n      role: \"assistant\",\n      content: \"ENTIRE_VERIFY_OK\",\n    })\n  })\n\n  it(\"marks failed tool calls as errors\", () => {\n    const doc = JSON.parse(exportFixture)\n    doc.conversation[2].content[0].toolResult = {\n      status: \"error\",\n      value: { content: [{ type: \"text\", text: \"permission denied\" }], isError: true },\n    }\n    const result = parser.parse(JSON.stringify(doc))\n\n    const toolCall = result.messages[1]!.toolCalls![0]!\n    expect(toolCall.result).toMatchObject({ type: \"error\", error: \"permission denied\" })\n  })\n\n  it(\"warns on unparseable content\", () => {\n    expect(parser.parse(\"not json\").warning).toBeDefined()\n    expect(parser.parse(`{\"id\":\"x\"}`).warning).toBeDefined()\n  })\n\n  it(\"strips session metadata the parser does not read\", () => {\n    const stripped = parser.strip(exportFixture)\n    const doc = JSON.parse(stripped)\n\n    expect(doc.extension_data).toBeUndefined()\n    expect(doc.accumulated_input_tokens).toBeUndefined()\n    expect(doc.conversation).toHaveLength(4)\n    expect(doc.model_config.model_name).toBe(\"anthropic/claude-opus-4.6\")\n\n    // Stripped output must still parse identically.\n    expect(parser.parse(stripped).messages).toEqual(parser.parse(exportFixture).messages)\n  })\n\n  it(\"extracts the model from the export\", () => {\n    expect(parser.extractModel(exportFixture)).toBe(\"anthropic/claude-opus-4.6\")\n    expect(parser.extractModel(\"not json\")).toBeNull()\n  })\n\n  it(\"classifies goose developer tools\", () => {\n    const call = (name: string) => ({ id: \"t\", name, input: {}, timestamp: \"\" })\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"shell\"))).toBe(\"shell\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"developer__shell\"))).toBe(\"shell\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"write\"))).toBe(\"fileOps\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"text_editor\"))).toBe(\"fileOps\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"tree\"))).toBe(\"search\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"analyze\"))).toBe(\"search\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"dynamic_task\"))).toBe(\"agent\")\n    expect(parser.classifyTool(call(\"load_skill\"))).toBe(\"other\")\n  })\n\n  it(\"extracts skill names from load_skill calls\", () => {\n    expect(\n      parser.extractSkillFromTool({ id: \"t\", name: \"load_skill\", input: { name: \"web-app\" }, timestamp: \"\" }),\n    ).toBe(\"web-app\")\n    expect(parser.extractSkillFromTool({ id: \"t\", name: \"write\", input: {}, timestamp: \"\"
